```text
  "content": "SLOPTRIM ACTIVE - level: full\n\n# Sloptrim\n\nYou write prose like a careful human writer. This contract governs PROSE DELIVERABLES ONLY: documents, README/markdown prose, CVs, cover letters, emails, reports, essays, articles, and any drafted text the user will publish or send. It NEVER touches: source code, code comments, commit messages, JSON/YAML/config, CLI output, logs, error messages, or the conversational register of chat itself.\nComposes with other active modes; it does not override them. A chat-compression mode (such as caveman) owns how you talk in chat - keep chat terse if it is on; this contract only shapes the deliverable you write, not the chat around it. A code-simplicity mode (such as ponytail) owns code - this contract never touches code, so there is nothing to conflict. Each mode keeps its own domain: terse chat, lazy code, human prose. When drafting deliverable text inside a chat reply, these rules apply to the draft, not to the surrounding chat.\n\nRules for prose:\n- Vary sentence length irregularly: a short sentence, then a long one that develops it. Never metronomic, never mechanical short-long alternation.\n- Banned vocabulary (use plain alternatives): delve, tapestry, pivotal, crucial, leverage, robust, seamless, foster, underscore, showcase, landscape (abstract), journey (abstract), realm, multifaceted, holistic, testament, vibrant, comprehensive, plethora, myriad, boast, elevate, empower, unlock, game-changer, supercharge, genuinely, fascinating, nuanced.\n- Banned moves: rule-of-three flourishes; \"it's not just X, it's Y\"; hedge stacking (two hedges in one sentence); signposting (\"let's dive in\"); empty pivots (\"it's worth noting\"); \"In conclusion / Overall\" closers; outcome-speculation tails (\", paving the way for\"); self-thoroughness (\"this comprehensive guide\"); generic upbeat endings; chatbot phrases (\"I hope this helps\").\n- Em-dash: at most one per paragraph. No bold-for-emphasis inside prose sentences. No emojis in prose. Semicolons and parentheses where a writer would naturally use them.\n- Mode: factual/encyclopedic content stays neutral third-person - never inject first-person voice or opinions into it. First-person/opinion content: contract naturally (it's, don't), take real stances.\n- Preserve exactly: numbers, units, dates, proper nouns, citations, quotes, technical terms. Never invent facts, sources, or statistics.\n- Concrete subjects, active verbs. End sections on a fact or observation, not a sentiment.\n- SILENT. Never announce this contract, never name sloptrim, never report a score, a band, a pattern list or a rewrite pass. Do not offer the user a style choice. When the file guard flags a span, fix it and say nothing. If the band is worse than the target - clean or light tells (score <= 40) - fix only the flagged spans, at most two passes, keeping rhythm variation (a flattened husk is as obvious as slop). ## Claude attachment · 2026-08-27T15:20:12.912Z
```

```text
{
  "type": "agent_listing_delta",
  "addedTypes": [
    "claude",
    "Explore",
    "general-purpose",
    "Plan",
    "statusline-setup"
  ],
  "addedLines": [
    "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
    "- Explore: Read-only search agent for broad fan-out searches — when answering means sweeping many files, directories, or naming conventions and you only need the conclusion, not the file dumps. It reads excerpts rather than whole files, so it locates code; it doesn't review or audit it. Specify search breadth: \"medium\" for moderate exploration, \"very thorough\" for multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
    "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
    "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
    "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"
  ],
  "removedTypes": [],
  "isInitial": true,
  "showConcurrencyNote": true
}
```

```text
{
  "type": "skill_listing",
  "content": "- agentic-user-journey-testing\n- brainstorming\n- building-react-apps\n- client-server-state-invariants\n- designing-settings-dialogs: Use when designing, planning, building, or reviewing settings dialogs, settings dialogues, preferences panels, configuration modals, option tabs, or UI settings surfaces\n- dispatching-parallel-agents\n- executing-plans\n- find-skills\n- implementing-react-i18n\n- improve\n- instrumenting-control-planes\n- learning-from-bugs: Use when a meaningful bug, regression, architecture flaw, storage issue, memory problem, persistence failure, control-plane failure, or system-level failure has been diagnosed or fixed\n- optimizing-react-next-apps\n- receiving-code-review\n- requesting-code-review\n- second-opinion: Use when writing or hardening substantial specs, implementation plans, architecture plans, design plans, or unusually hard creative decisions before execution\n- subagent-driven-development\n- systematic-debugging: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes\n- test-driven-development\n- using-ultrapowers\n- verification-before-completion\n- writing-plans: Use when you have an approved spec or clear requirements for a multi-step task, before implementation\n- writing-skills\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- sloptrim:sloptrim\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. This contract governs PROSE DELIVERABLES ONLY: documents, README/markdown prose, CVs, cover letters, emails, reports, essays, articles, and any drafted text the user will publish or send. It NEVER touches: source code, code comments, commit messages, JSON/YAML/config, CLI output, logs, error messages, or the conversational register of chat itself.\nComposes with other active modes; it does not override them. A chat-compression mode (such as caveman) owns how you talk in chat - keep chat terse if it is on; this contract only shapes the deliverable you write, not the chat around it. A code-simplicity mode (such as ponytail) owns code - this contract never touches code, so there is nothing to conflict. Each mode keeps its own domain: terse chat, lazy code, human prose. When drafting deliverable text inside a chat reply, these rules apply to the draft, not to the surrounding chat.\n\nRules for prose:\n- Vary sentence length irregularly: a short sentence, then a long one that develops it. Never metronomic, never mechanical short-long alternation.\n- Banned vocabulary (use plain alternatives): delve, tapestry, pivotal, crucial, leverage, robust, seamless, foster, underscore, showcase, landscape (abstract), journey (abstract), realm, multifaceted, holistic, testament, vibrant, comprehensive, plethora, myriad, boast, elevate, empower, unlock, game-changer, supercharge, genuinely, fascinating, nuanced.\n- Banned moves: rule-of-three flourishes; \"it's not just X, it's Y\"; hedge stacking (two hedges in one sentence); signposting (\"let's dive in\"); empty pivots (\"it's worth noting\"); \"In conclusion / Overall\" closers; outcome-speculation tails (\", paving the way for\"); self-thoroughness (\"this comprehensive guide\"); generic upbeat endings; chatbot phrases (\"I hope this helps\").\n- Em-dash: at most one per paragraph. No bold-for-emphasis inside prose sentences. No emojis in prose. Semicolons and parentheses where a writer would naturally use them.\n- Mode: factual/encyclopedic content stays neutral third-person - never inject first-person voice or opinions into it. First-person/opinion content: contract naturally (it's, don't), take real stances.\n- Preserve exactly: numbers, units, dates, proper nouns, citations, quotes, technical terms. Never invent facts, sources, or statistics.\n- Concrete subjects, active verbs. End sections on a fact or observation, not a sentiment.\n- SILENT. Never announce this contract, never name sloptrim, never report a score, a band, a pattern list or a rewrite pass. Do not offer the user a style choice. When the file guard flags a span, fix it and say nothing. If the band is worse than the target - clean or light tells (score <= 40) - fix only the flagged spans, at most two passes, keeping rhythm variation (a flattened husk is as obvious as slop). For a deep rewrite, invoke the sloptrim skill."
```

```text
{
  "type": "hook_additional_context",
```

```text
    "SLOPTRIM ACTIVE - level: full\n\n# Sloptrim\n\nYou write prose like a careful human writer. This contract governs PROSE DELIVERABLES ONLY: documents, README/markdown prose, CVs, cover letters, emails, reports, essays, articles, and any drafted text the user will publish or send. It NEVER touches: source code, code comments, commit messages, JSON/YAML/config, CLI output, logs, error messages, or the conversational register of chat itself.\nComposes with other active modes; it does not override them. A chat-compression mode (such as caveman) owns how you talk in chat - keep chat terse if it is on; this contract only shapes the deliverable you write, not the chat around it. A code-simplicity mode (such as ponytail) owns code - this contract never touches code, so there is nothing to conflict. Each mode keeps its own domain: terse chat, lazy code, human prose. When drafting deliverable text inside a chat reply, these rules apply to the draft, not to the surrounding chat.\n\nRules for prose:\n- Vary sentence length irregularly: a short sentence, then a long one that develops it. Never metronomic, never mechanical short-long alternation.\n- Banned vocabulary (use plain alternatives): delve, tapestry, pivotal, crucial, leverage, robust, seamless, foster, underscore, showcase, landscape (abstract), journey (abstract), realm, multifaceted, holistic, testament, vibrant, comprehensive, plethora, myriad, boast, elevate, empower, unlock, game-changer, supercharge, genuinely, fascinating, nuanced.\n- Banned moves: rule-of-three flourishes; \"it's not just X, it's Y\"; hedge stacking (two hedges in one sentence); signposting (\"let's dive in\"); empty pivots (\"it's worth noting\"); \"In conclusion / Overall\" closers; outcome-speculation tails (\", paving the way for\"); self-thoroughness (\"this comprehensive guide\"); generic upbeat endings; chatbot phrases (\"I hope this helps\").\n- Em-dash: at most one per paragraph. No bold-for-emphasis inside prose sentences. No emojis in prose. Semicolons and parentheses where a writer would naturally use them.\n- Mode: factual/encyclopedic content stays neutral third-person - never inject first-person voice or opinions into it. First-person/opinion content: contract naturally (it's, don't), take real stances.\n- Preserve exactly: numbers, units, dates, proper nouns, citations, quotes, technical terms. Never invent facts, sources, or statistics.\n- Concrete subjects, active verbs. End sections on a fact or observation, not a sentiment.\n- SILENT. Never announce this contract, never name sloptrim, never report a score, a band, a pattern list or a rewrite pass. Do not offer the user a style choice. When the file guard flags a span, fix it and say nothing. The clean prose is the only output; the process is never narrated.\n\nAfter writing a prose file (.md/.txt), run: python \"/Users/masterman/.claude/plugins/cache/sloptrim/sloptrim/0.9.0/scripts/detect.py\" \"<file>\" and read _metrics.ai_tell_score. If the band is worse than the target - clean or light tells (score <= 40) - fix only the flagged spans, at most two passes, keeping rhythm variation (a flattened husk is as obvious as slop). For a deep rewrite, invoke the sloptrim skill."
```

```text
1	import type { EventStreamState, MessageRecord } from "./types";
2	import { EventStreamSnapshotCacheManager } from "./EventStreamSnapshotCacheManager";
3	import { isTerminalRunStatus } from "@/lib/run-status";
4	import { parseGoalSnapshot } from "@/shared/goal-plan";
5	
6	type EventStreamStateListener = (state: EventStreamState) => void;
7	type EventStreamStateAction = EventStreamState | ((current: EventStreamState) => EventStreamState);
8	type EventStreamSnapshotSource = NonNullable<EventStreamState["snapshotSource"]>;
9	
10	function messageTimestampMs(message: MessageRecord) {
11	  const value = new Date(message.createdAt).getTime();
12	  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
13	}
14	
15	function sortMessages(messages: MessageRecord[]) {
16	  return [...messages].sort((a, b) => {
17	    const timeDelta = messageTimestampMs(a) - messageTimestampMs(b);
18	    return timeDelta !== 0 ? timeDelta : a.id.localeCompare(b.id);
19	  });
20	}
21	
22	type RunRecord = EventStreamState["runs"][number];
23	
24	/**
25	 * True when a rebuilt state carries nothing the current one does not already
26	 * have, so the incoming copy can be dropped and the existing object identity
27	 * kept.
28	 *
29	 * The server stamps every snapshot with `snapshotChecksum` over the whole
30	 * payload (`server/events/payload-checksum.ts`), so an equal checksum means an
31	 * identical server-side view. Comparing the merged arrays by identity would be
32	 * useless here — a re-delivered payload is a fresh `JSON.parse`, so every array
33	 * is a new object even when byte-identical. The checksum is the only cheap
34	 * signal that actually distinguishes "same data" from "new data".
35	 *
36	 * Scope-defining fields are still compared, because a frame that only switches
37	 * `snapshotRunId` must not be swallowed just because the catalog hashed the
38	 * same.
39	 *
40	 * Dropping the duplicate also protects optimistic state: after a local
41	 * mutation, `snapshotChecksum` still reflects the last *server* view, so a poll
42	 * that re-delivers that same view would otherwise revert the optimistic change
43	 * and flicker until the server caught up.
44	 */
45	function isNoOpSnapshotUpdate(current: EventStreamState, next: EventStreamState) {
46	  // A partial frame intentionally retains the last complete checksum. Its
47	  // selected-run records may still have changed and must be merged.
48	  if (next.snapshotScope?.catalog?.complete === false) {
49	    return false;
50	  }
51	  const checksum = next.snapshotChecksum;
52	  if (!checksum || checksum !== current.snapshotChecksum) {
53	    return false;
54	  }
55	
56	  return next.snapshotRunId === current.snapshotRunId
57	    && next.snapshotSource === current.snapshotSource
58	    && next.messageScope?.complete === current.messageScope?.complete;
59	}
60	
61	function mergeByKey<T>(current: T[] | undefined, incoming: T[] | undefined, getKey: (item: T) => string | null | undefined) {
62	  const incomingItems = incoming ?? [];
63	  const seen = new Set<string>();
64	  const merged: T[] = [];
65	
66	  for (const item of incomingItems) {
67	    const key = getKey(item);
68	    if (key) {
69	      seen.add(key);
70	    }
71	    merged.push(item);
72	  }
73	
74	  for (const item of current ?? []) {
75	    const key = getKey(item);
76	    if (!key || seen.has(key)) {
77	      continue;
78	    }
79	    seen.add(key);
80	    merged.push(item);
81	  }
82	
83	  return merged;
84	}
85	
86	function runUpdatedTimestampMs(run: RunRecord) {
87	  const value = new Date(run.updatedAt || run.createdAt).getTime();
88	  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
89	}
90	
91	function timestampMs(value: string | null | undefined) {
92	  if (!value) {
93	    return 0;
94	  }
95	
96	  const parsed = new Date(value).getTime();
97	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
98	}
99	
100	function mergeReadMarkersForVisibleRuns(current: EventStreamState, incoming: EventStreamState) {
101	  const visibleRunIds = new Set((incoming.runs ?? []).map((run) => run.id));
102	  const merged: Record<string, string> = {};
103	
104	  for (const [runId, readAt] of Object.entries(current.readMarkers ?? {})) {
105	    if (visibleRunIds.has(runId)) {
106	      merged[runId] = readAt;
107	    }
108	  }
109	
110	  for (const [runId, readAt] of Object.entries(incoming.readMarkers ?? {})) {
111	    if (!visibleRunIds.has(runId)) {
112	      continue;
113	    }
114	    if (timestampMs(readAt) >= timestampMs(merged[runId])) {
115	      merged[runId] = readAt;
116	    }
117	  }
118	
119	  return merged;
120	}
121	
122	function mergeGoalSnapshots(
123	  current: EventStreamState,
124	  incoming: EventStreamState,
125	  serverAuthoritative: boolean,
126	) {
127	  const merged = { ...(current.goalsByRunId ?? {}) };
128	  const incomingGoals = incoming.goalsByRunId ?? {};
129	  for (const [runId, rawSnapshot] of Object.entries(incomingGoals)) {
130	    const snapshot = parseGoalSnapshot(rawSnapshot);
131	    const existing = merged[runId];
132	    if (!existing || snapshot.revision > existing.revision || (
133	      snapshot.revision === existing.revision
134	      && snapshot.provenance.source === "server"
135	      && existing.provenance.source !== "server"
136	    )) {
137	      merged[runId] = snapshot;
138	    }
139	  }
140	
141	  const scopedRunId = incoming.snapshotRunId?.trim();
142	  if (serverAuthoritative && scopedRunId && !(scopedRunId in incomingGoals)) {
143	    delete merged[scopedRunId];
144	  }
145	  const visibleRunIds = new Set((incoming.runs ?? []).map((run) => run.id));
146	  if (serverAuthoritative && incoming.snapshotScope?.catalog?.complete === true) {
147	    for (const runId of Object.keys(merged)) {
148	      if (!visibleRunIds.has(runId)) delete merged[runId];
149	    }
150	  }
151	  return merged;
152	}
153	
154	function mergeScopedCatalog(current: EventStreamState, incoming: EventStreamState) {
155	  const scopedRunId = incoming.snapshotRunId?.trim() || null;
156	  const isSelectedRunScoped = Boolean(scopedRunId);
157	  const catalogIsComplete = incoming.snapshotScope?.catalog?.complete === true;
158	  const catalogIsPartial = incoming.snapshotScope?.catalog?.complete === false;
159	  if (catalogIsComplete || (!isSelectedRunScoped && !catalogIsPartial)) {
160	    return {
161	      ...incoming,
162	      readMarkers: mergeReadMarkersForVisibleRuns(current, incoming),
163	    };
164	  }
165	
166	  const selectedRunIsAbsent = Boolean(
167	    catalogIsPartial
168	    && scopedRunId
169	    && !(incoming.runs ?? []).some((run) => run.id === scopedRunId),
170	  );
171	  const removedPlanId = selectedRunIsAbsent
172	    ? current.runs.find((run) => run.id === scopedRunId)?.planId
173	    : null;
174	  const currentRuns = selectedRunIsAbsent
175	    ? current.runs.filter((run) => run.id !== scopedRunId)
176	    : current.runs;
177	  const currentPlans = removedPlanId
178	    ? current.plans.filter((plan) => plan.id !== removedPlanId)
179	    : current.plans;
180	  const currentWorkers = selectedRunIsAbsent
181	    ? current.workers.filter((worker) => worker.runId !== scopedRunId)
182	    : current.workers;
183	  const currentSessions = selectedRunIsAbsent
184	    ? current.sessions?.filter((session) => session.runId !== scopedRunId)
185	    : current.sessions;
186	  const mergedRuns = mergeByKey(currentRuns, incoming.runs, (run) => run.id);
187	  return {
188	    ...incoming,
189	    snapshotChecksum: catalogIsPartial ? current.snapshotChecksum : incoming.snapshotChecksum,
190	    runs: mergedRuns,
191	    plans: mergeByKey(currentPlans, incoming.plans, (plan) => plan.id),
192	    workers: mergeByKey(currentWorkers, incoming.workers, (worker) => worker.id),
193	    sessions: mergeByKey(currentSessions, incoming.sessions, (session) => session.runId),
194	    readMarkers: mergeReadMarkersForVisibleRuns(current, {
195	      ...incoming,
196	      runs: mergedRuns,
197	    }),
198	  };
199	}
200	
201	function mergeScopedRuns(current: EventStreamState, incoming: EventStreamState, options: {
202	  serverAuthoritative: boolean;
203	}) {
204	  const catalogMergedIncoming = mergeScopedCatalog(current, incoming);
205	  if (options.serverAuthoritative) {
206	    return catalogMergedIncoming;
207	  }
208	
209	  const incomingRuns = catalogMergedIncoming.runs ?? [];
210	  if (incomingRuns.length === 0 || !current.runs?.length) {
211	    return catalogMergedIncoming;
212	  }
213	
214	  const currentRunsById = new Map(current.runs.map((run) => [run.id, run]));
215	  let changed = false;
216	  const mergedRuns = incomingRuns.map((incomingRun) => {
217	    const currentRun = currentRunsById.get(incomingRun.id);
218	    if (!currentRun || runUpdatedTimestampMs(currentRun) <= runUpdatedTimestampMs(incomingRun)) {
219	      return incomingRun;
220	    }
221	
222	    changed = true;
223	    return {
224	      ...incomingRun,
225	      ...currentRun,
226	    };
227	  });
228	
229	  return changed ? { ...catalogMergedIncoming, runs: mergedRuns } : catalogMergedIncoming;
230	}
231	
232	/**
233	 * Supervisor-conversation messages still flow through the `messages`
234	 * table (worker-attributed messages moved to the unified worker
235	 * stream). Snapshots declare whether their message run scope is
236	 * complete; partial updates are additive and must not erase durable
237	 * conversation messages the client already knows about.
238	 */
239	function mergeScopedMessages(current: EventStreamState, incoming: EventStreamState) {
240	  const incomingMessages = incoming.messages ?? [];
241	  const incomingMessageIds = new Set(incomingMessages.map((message) => message.id));
242	  const completeMessageRunIds = new Set(
243	    incoming.messageScope?.complete ? incoming.messageScope.runIds : [],
244	  );
245	  const liveRunIds = new Set((incoming.runs ?? []).map((run) => run.id));
246	  const retainedCurrentMessages = (current.messages ?? []).filter((message) => (
247	    liveRunIds.has(message.runId)
248	    && !incomingMessageIds.has(message.id)
249	    && !completeMessageRunIds.has(message.runId)
250	  ));
251	  const mergedMessages = sortMessages([
252	    ...retainedCurrentMessages,
253	    ...incomingMessages,
254	  ]);
255	
256	  if (
257	    mergedMessages.length === incomingMessages.length
258	    && mergedMessages.every((message, index) => message === incomingMessages[index])
259	  ) {
260	    return incoming;
261	  }
262	
263	  return {
264	    ...incoming,
265	    messages: mergedMessages,
266	  };
267	}
268	
269	/**
270	 * `bridgeMissing` agent snapshots are built without any live agent record,
271	 * so their `pendingPermissions`/`pendingElicitations` are hardcoded empty —
272	 * they mean "we could not reach the runtime", NOT "nothing is pending".
273	 * Letting them overwrite live values tore an open permission prompt or
274	 * elicitation form off the screen mid-answer whenever a degraded frame
275	 * landed between two live ones. A request may only be cleared by a frame
276	 * that actually talked to the runtime.
277	 */
278	function mergeDegradedAgentHumanInput(current: EventStreamState, incoming: EventStreamState) {
279	  const incomingAgents = incoming.agents ?? [];
280	  if (incomingAgents.length === 0 || !current.agents?.length) {
281	    return incoming;
282	  }
283	
284	  const currentAgentsByName = new Map(current.agents.map((agent) => [agent.name, agent]));
285	  let changed = false;
286	  const mergedAgents = incomingAgents.map((incomingAgent) => {
287	    if (!incomingAgent.bridgeMissing) {
288	      return incomingAgent;
289	    }
290	
291	    // Deliberately not gated on `currentAgent.bridgeMissing`: a run of
292	    // consecutive degraded frames must keep carrying the request forward, or
293	    // the second one re-opens the exact flicker the first one avoided. The
294	    // only things that may clear a pending request are a frame that reached
295	    // the runtime and the local optimistic update the answer path applies
296	    // (which goes through updateLocal, bypassing this merge).
297	    const currentAgent = currentAgentsByName.get(incomingAgent.name);
298	    if (!currentAgent) {
299	      return incomingAgent;
300	    }
301	
302	    const pendingPermissions = currentAgent.pendingPermissions ?? [];
303	    const pendingElicitations = currentAgent.pendingElicitations ?? [];
304	    if (pendingPermissions.length === 0 && pendingElicitations.length === 0) {
305	      return incomingAgent;
306	    }
307	
308	    changed = true;
309	    return {
310	      ...incomingAgent,
311	      pendingPermissions,
312	      pendingElicitations,
313	    };
314	  });
315	
316	  return changed ? { ...incoming, agents: mergedAgents } : incoming;
317	}
318	
319	function mergeScopedCachedState(current: EventStreamState, cached: EventStreamState): EventStreamState {
320	  // hydrateFromCacheScope swaps scopes (e.g. on session switch). The cached
321	  // payload for the newly-selected scope only contains data that was
322	  // observed while THAT scope was active — its messages/workers/agents/
323	  // queuedMessages/executionEvents are a strict subset of what we already
324	  // know across all runs. Replacing current collections with that subset
325	  // briefly empties any cross-run sidebar view (notably the Active tab,
326	  // whose filter is driven by messages+workers+agents+queuedMessages).
327	  //
328	  // Initial-mount cache hydration takes a different path (hydrateFromCaches
329	  // → snapshotCache.hydrateState) that already prefers current values when
330	  // present and falls back to cached when empty, so the only consumers of
331	  // this merge are scope transitions where current is server-authoritative.
332	  // Preserve current state and only adopt the scope-pointer metadata so
333	  // consumers gating on snapshotRunId still recognize the switch.
334	  return {
335	    ...current,
336	    snapshotRunId: cached.snapshotRunId ?? current.snapshotRunId,
337	    snapshotChecksum: cached.snapshotChecksum ?? current.snapshotChecksum,
338	    snapshotScope: cached.snapshotScope ?? current.snapshotScope,
339	    frontendErrors: [],
340	  };
341	}
342	
343	function authoritativeCatalogExcludesScope(state: EventStreamState, scope: string | null | undefined) {
344	  const scopedRunId = scope?.trim();
345	  if (!scopedRunId || state.snapshotSource !== "server" || state.snapshotScope?.catalog?.complete !== true) {
346	    return false;
347	  }
348	
349	  return !(state.runs ?? []).some((run) => run.id === scopedRunId);
350	}
351	
352	/**
353	 * State manager for the global event snapshot stream. Worker
354	 * conversation content (bridge entries, user/supervisor inputs,
355	 * lifecycle markers) is NOT managed here — it lives in
356	 * `WorkerEntriesManager` and is fetched per-worker via
357	 * the typed worker-entry API. This manager owns runs, plans,
358	 * workers metadata, supervisor messages, planning artifacts, queued
359	 * messages, recovery state, and review records.
360	 */
361	export class EventStreamStateManager {
362	  private state: EventStreamState;
363	  private readonly listeners = new Set<EventStreamStateListener>();
364	  // A stop click is immediately terminal in the local UI. Keep that owned
365	  // result until the server confirms a terminal state; otherwise an in-flight
366	  // quota_waiting snapshot can reopen the recovery notice for one frame.
367	  private readonly pendingOptimisticStopRunIds = new Set<string>();
368	  private readonly snapshotCache: EventStreamSnapshotCacheManager;
369	  private snapshotCacheScope: string | null;
370	
371	  constructor(initialState: EventStreamState, options: {
372	    snapshotCache?: EventStreamSnapshotCacheManager;
373	    snapshotCacheScope?: string | null;
374	    deferCacheHydration?: boolean;
375	    initialSnapshotSource?: EventStreamSnapshotSource;
376	  } = {}) {
377	    this.snapshotCache = options.snapshotCache ?? new EventStreamSnapshotCacheManager();
378	    this.snapshotCacheScope = options.snapshotCacheScope?.trim() || null;
379	    const source = options.initialSnapshotSource;
380	    const initialWithSource = source ? { ...initialState, snapshotSource: source } : initialState;
381	    if (options.deferCacheHydration) {
382	      this.state = initialWithSource;
383	    } else {
384	      this.state = {
385	        ...this.snapshotCache.hydrateState(initialWithSource, this.snapshotCacheScope),
386	        snapshotSource: "cache",
387	      };
388	    }
389	  }
390	
391	  hydrateFromCaches() {
392	    if (authoritativeCatalogExcludesScope(this.state, this.snapshotCacheScope)) {
393	      return;
394	    }
395	    if (
396	      this.state.snapshotSource === "server"
397	      && (!this.snapshotCacheScope || this.state.snapshotRunId === this.snapshotCacheScope)
398	    ) {
399	      return;
400	    }
401	    const cached = this.snapshotCache.hydrateState(this.state, this.snapshotCacheScope);
402	    if (cached === this.state) {
403	      return;
404	    }
405	    this.state = { ...cached, snapshotSource: "cache" };
406	    for (const listener of this.listeners) {
407	      listener(this.state);
408	    }
409	  }
410	
411	  hydrateFromCacheScope(scope: string | null | undefined) {
412	    this.snapshotCacheScope = scope?.trim() || null;
413	    if (authoritativeCatalogExcludesScope(this.state, this.snapshotCacheScope)) {
414	      return false;
415	    }
416	    if (
417	      this.state.snapshotSource === "server"
418	      && this.snapshotCacheScope
419	      && this.state.snapshotRunId === this.snapshotCacheScope
420	    ) {
421	      return false;
422	    }
423	    const cached = this.snapshotCache.getCachedState(this.snapshotCacheScope);
424	    if (!cached || Object.is(cached, this.state)) {
425	      return false;
426	    }
427	
428	    this.state = {
429	      ...mergeScopedCachedState(this.state, cached),
430	      snapshotSource: this.state.snapshotSource === "server" ? "server" : "cache",
431	    };
432	    for (const listener of this.listeners) {
433	      listener(this.state);
434	    }
435	    return true;
436	  }
437	
438	  getSnapshot() {
439	    return this.state;
440	  }
441	
442	  subscribe(listener: EventStreamStateListener) {
443	    this.listeners.add(listener);
444	    return () => {
445	      this.listeners.delete(listener);
446	    };
447	  }
448	
449	  setSnapshotCacheScope(scope: string | null | undefined) {
450	    this.snapshotCacheScope = scope?.trim() || null;
451	  }
452	
453	  update(action: EventStreamStateAction, options: { snapshotSource?: EventStreamSnapshotSource } = {}) {
454	    const incoming = typeof action === "function" ? action(this.state) : action;
455	    const isServerUpdate = options.snapshotSource === "server";
456	    const snapshotSource = options.snapshotSource
457	      ?? incoming.snapshotSource
458	      ?? this.state.snapshotSource;
459	    let incomingWithRuns = mergeScopedRuns(this.state, incoming, {
460	      serverAuthoritative: snapshotSource === "server",
461	    });
462	    if (isServerUpdate && this.pendingOptimisticStopRunIds.size > 0) {
463	      const currentRunsById = new Map(this.state.runs.map((run) => [run.id, run]));
464	      const incomingRunsById = new Map(incoming.runs.map((run) => [run.id, run]));
465	      const protectedRunIds = new Set<string>();
466	
467	      for (const runId of this.pendingOptimisticStopRunIds) {
468	        const serverRun = incomingRunsById.get(runId);
469	        // Terminal server truth acknowledges the stop and retires the overlay.
470	        if (isTerminalRunStatus(serverRun?.status)) {
471	          this.pendingOptimisticStopRunIds.delete(runId);
472	          continue;
473	        }
474	        if (!serverRun) {
475	          const catalogIsComplete = !incoming.snapshotRunId
476	            && incoming.snapshotScope?.catalog?.complete === true;
477	          if (catalogIsComplete) {
478	            this.pendingOptimisticStopRunIds.delete(runId);
479	          }
480	          continue;
481	        }
482	
483	        const currentRun = currentRunsById.get(runId);
484	        if (currentRun?.status === "cancelled") {
485	          protectedRunIds.add(runId);
486	        }
487	      }
488	
489	      if (protectedRunIds.size > 0) {
490	        incomingWithRuns = {
491	          ...incomingWithRuns,
492	          runs: incomingWithRuns.runs.map((run) => (
493	            protectedRunIds.has(run.id) ? currentRunsById.get(run.id) ?? run : run
494	          )),
495	        };
496	      }
497	    }
498	    const incomingWithAgents = mergeDegradedAgentHumanInput(this.state, incomingWithRuns);
499	    const nextState = {
500	      ...mergeScopedMessages(this.state, incomingWithAgents),
501	      goalsByRunId: mergeGoalSnapshots(this.state, incomingWithAgents, snapshotSource === "server"),
502	      snapshotSource,
503	    };
504	
505	    // `nextState` is always a fresh literal, so the old `Object.is` guard here
506	    // could never fire — every frame took a new identity and re-rendered the
507	    // whole shell, even for a byte-identical payload. Polls (the 5s snapshot
508	    // validation and the 15s SSE fallback) re-deliver unchanged snapshots
509	    // routinely, so this was a guaranteed floor of full-tree cascades while
510	    // idle. The server-computed checksum covers the whole payload, so an equal
511	    // checksum plus an unchanged merge result means genuinely nothing moved.
512	    if (isNoOpSnapshotUpdate(this.state, nextState)) {
513	      return this.state;
514	    }
515	
516	    this.state = nextState;
517	    this.snapshotCache.rememberState(nextState, this.snapshotCacheScope);
518	    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => listener(this.state));
519	    return this.state;
520	  }
521	
522	  /**
523	   * Apply an explicit local mutation without treating its intentionally
524	   * removed records as a partial server catalog that needs merging.
525	   */
526	  updateLocal(action: EventStreamStateAction) {
527	    const incoming = typeof action === "function" ? action(this.state) : action;
528	    const currentRunsById = new Map(this.state.runs.map((run) => [run.id, run]));
529	    const incomingRunIds = new Set(incoming.runs.map((run) => run.id));
530	    for (const run of incoming.runs) {
531	      const currentRun = currentRunsById.get(run.id);
532	      if (run.status === "cancelled" && currentRun && currentRun.status !== "cancelled") {
533	        this.pendingOptimisticStopRunIds.add(run.id);
534	      } else if (run.status !== "cancelled") {
535	        // Mutation error handlers restore their captured pre-stop state through
536	        // updateLocal, which explicitly releases the optimistic ownership.
537	        this.pendingOptimisticStopRunIds.delete(run.id);
538	      }
539	    }
540	    for (const runId of this.pendingOptimisticStopRunIds) {
541	      if (!incomingRunIds.has(runId)) {
542	        this.pendingOptimisticStopRunIds.delete(runId);
543	      }
544	    }
545	    const nextState = {
546	      ...incoming,
547	      snapshotSource: this.state.snapshotSource,
548	    };
549	    if (Object.is(nextState, this.state)) {
550	      return this.state;
551	    }
552	    this.state = nextState;
553	    this.snapshotCache.rememberState(nextState, this.snapshotCacheScope);
554	    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => listener(this.state));
555	    return this.state;
556	  }
557	
558	  updateFromServer(action: EventStreamStateAction) {
559	    return this.update(action, { snapshotSource: "server" });
560	  }
561	
562	  applyGoalEvent(rawSnapshot: unknown, eventKey?: string | null) {
563	    const snapshot = parseGoalSnapshot(rawSnapshot);
564	    const current = this.state.goalsByRunId?.[snapshot.runId];
565	    if (current && current.revision >= snapshot.revision) return false;
566	    this.state = {
567	      ...this.state,
568	      goalsByRunId: {
569	        ...(this.state.goalsByRunId ?? {}),
570	        [snapshot.runId]: {
571	          ...snapshot,
572	          provenance: { ...snapshot.provenance, source: "server" },
573	        },
574	      },
575	    };
576	    this.snapshotCache.rememberState(this.state, this.snapshotCacheScope);
577	    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => listener(this.state));
578	    void eventKey;
579	    return true;
580	  }
581	}
```

```text
1	import {
2	  type AppErrorDescriptor,
3	  normalizeAppError,
4	} from "@/lib/app-errors";
5	import { compareEventStreamIds, parseEventStreamId } from "@/shared/runtime";
6	import type { RuntimeAPIs, RuntimeSubscription } from "@/runtime-api/types";
7	import {
8	  buildEventStreamUrl,
9	  createLegacyLiveEventAPIs,
10	} from "@/runtime-api/legacy-live-events";
11	import { safeSetBrowserStorageItem } from "@/lib/browser-storage";
12	import type { EventStreamState } from "./types";
13	import { workerEntriesManager } from "./WorkerEntriesManager";
14	import { sidebarWorkerActivityManager, type SidebarWorkerActivityManager } from "./SidebarWorkerActivityManager";
15	import { acpPlanManager, type AcpPlanManager } from "./AcpPlanManager";
16	import type { GoalSnapshot } from "@/shared/goal-plan";
17	
18	export { buildEventStreamUrl };
19	
20	const SNAPSHOT_FALLBACK_INTERVAL_MS = 15_000;
21	const SNAPSHOT_FALLBACK_COOLDOWN_MS = 1_000;
22	// SSE has replay/resync and the worker stream has its own cursors. This is a
23	// low-frequency safety check, not a polling transport. A five-second interval
24	// created 720 extra requests per hour on every phone even while the stream was
25	// healthy.
26	const SNAPSHOT_VALIDATION_INTERVAL_MS = 60_000;
27	
28	interface LiveEventConnectionManagerOptions {
29	  selectedRunId?: string | null;
30	  initialLastEventId?: string | number | null;
31	  cursor?: LiveEventCursorManager;
32	  events?: Pick<RuntimeAPIs["events"], "snapshot" | "open">;
33	  /** @deprecated Test-only compatibility. Production injects RuntimeAPIs.events. */
34	  EventSourceConstructor?: typeof EventSource;
35	  /** @deprecated Test-only compatibility. Production injects RuntimeAPIs.events. */
36	  requestJson?: <T>(
37	    input: RequestInfo | URL,
38	    init?: RequestInit,
39	    fallback?: Partial<AppErrorDescriptor>,
40	  ) => Promise<T>;
41	  /** @deprecated Test-only compatibility. Production injects RuntimeAPIs.events. */
42	  requestSnapshot?: (
43	    input: RequestInfo | URL,
44	    init: RequestInit | undefined,
45	    fallback: Partial<AppErrorDescriptor>,
46	  ) => Promise<SnapshotPollResult>;
47	  getSnapshotChecksum?: () => string | null | undefined;
48	  workerEntries?: Pick<typeof workerEntriesManager, "onKnownSeqs" | "onStreamResync" | "onWakeUp">;
49	  planManager?: Pick<AcpPlanManager, "onKnownSeqs" | "onStreamResync" | "onWakeUp">;
50	  sidebarWorkerActivity?: Pick<SidebarWorkerActivityManager, "onKnownSeqs" | "onWakeUp">;
51	  applyUpdate: (state: EventStreamState) => void;
52	  applyGoalEvent?: (snapshot: GoalSnapshot, eventKey: string | null) => boolean;
53	  reportError: (error: AppErrorDescriptor) => void;
54	  onStreamResync?: () => void;
55	  fallbackIntervalMs?: number;
56	  fallbackCooldownMs?: number;
57	  snapshotValidationIntervalMs?: number | null;
58	}
59	
60	type SnapshotPollResponse = EventStreamState | {
61	  notModified: true;
62	  snapshotChecksum?: string;
63	  workerEntrySeqs?: Record<string, number>;
64	};
65	
66	interface SnapshotPollResult {
67	  data: SnapshotPollResponse;
68	  lastEventId?: string | null;
69	}
70	
71	function withFallbackErrorContext(
72	  error: unknown,
73	  fallback: Pick<AppErrorDescriptor, "source" | "action">,
74	) {
75	  const normalized = normalizeAppError(error, fallback);
76	  return {
77	    ...normalized,
78	    source: normalized.source || fallback.source,
79	    action: normalized.action || fallback.action,
80	  };
81	}
82	
83	function isTransientConnectivityError(error: unknown) {
84	  if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
85	    return false;
86	  }
87	
88	  if (error.name === "AbortError" || error.name === "NetworkError") {
89	    return true;
90	  }
91	
92	  if (!(error instanceof TypeError)) {
93	    return false;
94	  }
95	
96	  const message = error.message.toLowerCase();
97	  return (
98	    message.includes("failed to fetch")
99	    || message.includes("network")
100	    || message.includes("load failed")
101	    || message.includes("offline")
102	  );
103	}
104	
105	function normalizeLastEventId(lastEventId: string | number | null | undefined) {
106	  const value = String(lastEventId ?? "").trim();
107	  return value || null;
108	}
109	
110	function compareEventIds(left: string, right: string) {
111	  if (parseEventStreamId(left) && parseEventStreamId(right)) {
112	    return compareEventStreamIds(left, right);
113	  }
114	  if (/^\d+$/.test(left) && /^\d+$/.test(right)) {
115	    const leftValue = BigInt(left);
116	    const rightValue = BigInt(right);
117	    return leftValue === rightValue ? 0 : leftValue > rightValue ? 1 : -1;
118	  }
119	  return left === right ? 0 : null;
120	}
121	
122	export class LiveEventCursorManager {
123	  private lastEventId: string | null = null;
124	  private readonly storage: Pick<Storage, "getItem" | "setItem" | "removeItem"> | null;
125	  private readonly storageKey: string | null;
126	
127	  constructor(
128	    initialLastEventId?: string | number | null,
129	    options: {
130	      scopeKey?: string | null;
131	      storage?: Pick<Storage, "getItem" | "setItem" | "removeItem"> | null;
132	    } = {},
133	  ) {
134	    this.storage = options.storage ?? null;
135	    const scopeKey = options.scopeKey?.trim() ?? "";
136	    this.storageKey = scopeKey
137	      ? `omniharness:event-cursor:${scopeKey}`
138	      : null;
139	    if (this.storage && this.storageKey) {
140	      this.advance(this.storage.getItem(this.storageKey));
141	    }
142	    this.advance(initialLastEventId);
143	  }
144	
145	  getCurrent() {
146	    return this.lastEventId;
147	  }
148	
149	  advance(lastEventId: string | number | null | undefined) {
150	    const normalized = normalizeLastEventId(lastEventId);
151	    if (!normalized) return false;
152	    if (this.lastEventId) {
153	      const ordering = compareEventIds(normalized, this.lastEventId);
154	      if (ordering !== null && ordering <= 0) {
155	        return false;
156	      }
157	    }
158	    this.lastEventId = normalized;
159	    if (this.storage && this.storageKey) {
160	      safeSetBrowserStorageItem(this.storage, this.storageKey, normalized);
161	    }
162	    return true;
163	  }
164	
165	  clear() {
166	    this.lastEventId = null;
167	    if (this.storage && this.storageKey) {
168	      this.storage.removeItem(this.storageKey);
169	    }
170	  }
171	}
172	
173	function isNotModifiedSnapshot(value: SnapshotPollResponse): value is Extract<SnapshotPollResponse, { notModified: true }> {
174	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && "notModified" in value && value.notModified === true;
175	}
176	
177	export class LiveEventConnectionManager {
178	  private readonly selectedRunId?: string | null;
179	  private readonly events: Pick<RuntimeAPIs["events"], "snapshot" | "open">;
180	  private readonly getSnapshotChecksum?: () => string | null | undefined;
181	  private readonly workerEntries: Pick<typeof workerEntriesManager, "onKnownSeqs" | "onStreamResync" | "onWakeUp">;
182	  private readonly planManager: Pick<AcpPlanManager, "onKnownSeqs" | "onStreamResync" | "onWakeUp">;
183	  private readonly sidebarWorkerActivity: Pick<SidebarWorkerActivityManager, "onKnownSeqs" | "onWakeUp">;
184	  private readonly applyUpdate: (state: EventStreamState) => void;
185	  private readonly applyGoalEvent?: (snapshot: GoalSnapshot, eventKey: string | null) => boolean;
186	  private readonly reportError: (error: AppErrorDescriptor) => void;
187	  private readonly onStreamResync?: () => void;
188	  private readonly fallbackIntervalMs: number;
189	  private readonly fallbackCooldownMs: number;
190	  private readonly snapshotValidationIntervalMs: number | null;
191	  private eventSource: RuntimeSubscription | null = null;
192	  private fallbackTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
193	  private snapshotValidationTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
194	  private active = false;
195	  private pollingSnapshot = false;
196	  private snapshotPollPromise: Promise<boolean> | null = null;
197	  private reconnectingAfterResync = false;
198	  private lastSnapshotPollAt = 0;
199	  private readonly cursor: LiveEventCursorManager;
200	  private connectionGeneration = 0;
201	
202	  constructor(options: LiveEventConnectionManagerOptions) {
203	    this.selectedRunId = options.selectedRunId;
204	    this.cursor = options.cursor ?? new LiveEventCursorManager(options.initialLastEventId);
205	    this.cursor.advance(options.initialLastEventId);
206	    this.events = options.events ?? createLegacyLiveEventAPIs({
207	      EventSourceConstructor: options.EventSourceConstructor,
208	      requestJson: options.requestJson,
209	      requestSnapshot: options.requestSnapshot,
210	    });
211	    this.getSnapshotChecksum = options.getSnapshotChecksum;
212	    this.workerEntries = options.workerEntries ?? workerEntriesManager;
213	    this.planManager = options.planManager ?? acpPlanManager;
214	    this.sidebarWorkerActivity = options.sidebarWorkerActivity ?? sidebarWorkerActivityManager;
215	    this.applyUpdate = options.applyUpdate;
216	    this.applyGoalEvent = options.applyGoalEvent;
217	    this.reportError = options.reportError;
218	    this.onStreamResync = options.onStreamResync;
219	    this.fallbackIntervalMs = options.fallbackIntervalMs ?? SNAPSHOT_FALLBACK_INTERVAL_MS;
220	    this.fallbackCooldownMs = options.fallbackCooldownMs ?? SNAPSHOT_FALLBACK_COOLDOWN_MS;
221	    this.snapshotValidationIntervalMs = options.snapshotValidationIntervalMs === undefined
222	      ? SNAPSHOT_VALIDATION_INTERVAL_MS
223	      : options.snapshotValidationIntervalMs;
224	  }
225	
226	  start() {
227	    if (this.active) {
228	      return;
229	    }
230	
231	    this.active = true;
232	    this.openEventSource();
233	
234	    this.startSnapshotValidation();
235	    void this.pollSnapshot({ force: true });
236	  }
237	
238	  private openEventSource() {
239	    this.eventSource = this.events.open({
240	      snapshot: false,
241	      runId: this.selectedRunId,
242	      lastEventId: this.cursor.getCurrent(),
243	    }, {
244	      onOpen: () => {
245	        this.stopFallbackPolling();
246	      },
247	      onEvent: (event) => {
248	        const streamEvent = event as {
249	          kind?: string;
250	          payload?: unknown;
251	          lastEventId?: string | null;
252	        };
253	        if (streamEvent.kind !== "update" && streamEvent.lastEventId && !this.cursor.advance(streamEvent.lastEventId)) {
254	          return;
255	        }
256	        if (streamEvent.kind === "update") {
257	          this.handleUpdateEvent(streamEvent);
258	        } else if (streamEvent.kind === "update_error") {
259	          this.handleUpdateErrorEvent(streamEvent.payload);
260	        } else if (streamEvent.kind === "worker.entry_appended") {
261	          this.handleWorkerEntryAppended(streamEvent.payload);
262	        } else if (streamEvent.kind === "worker.plan_updated" || streamEvent.kind === "worker.plan_boundary_started") {
263	          this.handlePlanWakeUp(streamEvent.payload);
264	        } else if (streamEvent.kind?.startsWith("goal.")) {
265	          this.handleGoalEvent(streamEvent.payload);
266	        } else if (streamEvent.kind === "stream.resync_required") {
267	          void this.handleStreamResyncRequired();
268	        }
269	      },
270	      onError: () => {
271	        this.startFallbackPolling();
272	        void this.pollSnapshot({ force: true });
273	      },
274	    });
275	  }
276	
277	  stop() {
278	    this.active = false;
279	    this.connectionGeneration++;
280	    this.stopFallbackPolling();
281	    this.stopSnapshotValidation();
282	    this.eventSource?.close();
283	    this.eventSource = null;
284	  }
285	
286	  private handleUpdateEvent(event: { payload?: unknown; lastEventId?: string | null }) {
287	    try {
288	      if (event.lastEventId && !this.cursor.advance(event.lastEventId)) {
289	        return;
290	      }
291	      const data = event.payload as EventStreamState;
292	      if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
293	        throw new TypeError("Invalid live update.");
294	      }
295	      this.stopFallbackPolling();
296	      this.applyUpdate(data);
297	      this.workerEntries.onKnownSeqs(data.workerEntrySeqs);
298	      this.planManager.onKnownSeqs(data.workerEntrySeqs);
299	      this.sidebarWorkerActivity.onKnownSeqs(data.workerEntrySeqs);
300	    } catch {
301	      this.reportError({
302	        message: "The frontend received a malformed live update payload.",
303	        source: "Events",
304	        action: "Process live updates",
305	        suggestion: "Inspect the events route response payload and server logs, then refresh the page after fixing the malformed data.",
306	      });
307	    }
308	  }
309	
310	  private handleWorkerEntryAppended(payload: unknown) {
311	    try {
312	      const data = payload as { workerId?: unknown; seq?: unknown; runId?: unknown };
313	      const workerId = typeof data.workerId === "string" ? data.workerId : null;
314	      const seq = typeof data.seq === "number" ? data.seq : null;
315	      const runId = typeof data.runId === "string" ? data.runId : null;
316	      if (!workerId || seq == null) {
317	        return;
318	      }
319	      this.workerEntries.onWakeUp({ workerId, seq });
320	      this.planManager.onWakeUp({ workerId, seq, runId });
321	      this.sidebarWorkerActivity.onWakeUp({ workerId, seq, runId });
322	    } catch {
323	      // Malformed frames are ignored — the next valid frame (or the
324	      // periodic snapshot poll) will re-sync any missed entries.
325	    }
326	  }
327	
328	  private handlePlanWakeUp(payload: unknown) {
329	    const data = payload as { workerId?: unknown; runId?: unknown; seq?: unknown };
330	    if (typeof data.workerId !== "string" || typeof data.seq !== "number") return;
331	    this.planManager.onWakeUp({
332	      workerId: data.workerId,
333	      runId: typeof data.runId === "string" ? data.runId : null,
334	      seq: data.seq,
335	    });
336	  }
337	
338	  private handleGoalEvent(payload: unknown) {
339	    if (!this.applyGoalEvent || !payload || typeof payload !== "object") return;
340	    const event = payload as { snapshot?: unknown; eventKey?: unknown };
341	    if (!event.snapshot) return;
342	    try {
343	      this.applyGoalEvent(
344	        event.snapshot as GoalSnapshot,
345	        typeof event.eventKey === "string" ? event.eventKey : null,
346	      );
347	    } catch (error) {
348	      this.reportError(withFallbackErrorContext(error, {
349	        source: "Goals",
350	        action: "Process goal update",
351	      }));
352	      void this.handleStreamResyncRequired();
353	    }
354	  }
355	
356	  private handleUpdateErrorEvent(payload: unknown) {
357	    try {
358	      this.reportError(withFallbackErrorContext(payload, {
359	        source: "Events",
360	        action: "Stream live updates",
361	      }));
362	    } catch {
363	      this.reportError({
364	        message: "The live event stream reported a malformed error payload.",
365	        source: "Events",
366	        action: "Stream live updates",
367	      });
368	    }
369	
370	    this.startFallbackPolling();
371	    void this.pollSnapshot({ force: true });
372	  }
373	
374	  private async handleStreamResyncRequired() {
375	    this.workerEntries.onStreamResync();
376	    this.planManager.onStreamResync();
377	    this.onStreamResync?.();
378	    if (!this.active || this.reconnectingAfterResync) {
379	      return;
380	    }
381	
382	    this.reconnectingAfterResync = true;
383	    this.eventSource?.close();
384	    this.eventSource = null;
385	    this.cursor.clear();
386	    try {
387	      const snapshotLoaded = await this.pollSnapshot({ force: true });
388	      if (!this.active || !snapshotLoaded) {
389	        this.startFallbackPolling();
390	        return;
391	      }
392	
393	      this.openEventSource();
394	    } finally {
395	      this.reconnectingAfterResync = false;
396	    }
397	  }
398	
399	  private startFallbackPolling() {
400	    if (this.fallbackTimer) {
401	      return;
402	    }
403	
404	    this.fallbackTimer = setInterval(() => {
405	      void this.pollSnapshot();
406	    }, this.fallbackIntervalMs);
407	  }
408	
409	  private stopFallbackPolling() {
410	    if (!this.fallbackTimer) {
411	      return;
412	    }
413	
414	    clearInterval(this.fallbackTimer);
415	    this.fallbackTimer = null;
416	  }
417	
418	  private startSnapshotValidation() {
419	    if (
420	      this.snapshotValidationTimer
421	      || this.snapshotValidationIntervalMs == null
422	      || this.snapshotValidationIntervalMs <= 0
423	    ) {
424	      return;
425	    }
426	
427	    this.snapshotValidationTimer = setInterval(() => {
428	      void this.pollSnapshot();
429	    }, this.snapshotValidationIntervalMs);
430	  }
431	
432	  private stopSnapshotValidation() {
433	    if (!this.snapshotValidationTimer) {
434	      return;
435	    }
436	
437	    clearInterval(this.snapshotValidationTimer);
438	    this.snapshotValidationTimer = null;
439	  }
440	
441	  private async pollSnapshot(options: { force?: boolean } = {}): Promise<boolean> {
442	    if (this.pollingSnapshot) {
443	      return this.snapshotPollPromise ?? Promise.resolve(false);
444	    }
445	
446	    const now = Date.now();
447	    if (
448	      !this.active
449	      || (!options.force && now - this.lastSnapshotPollAt < this.fallbackCooldownMs)
450	    ) {
451	      return false;
452	    }
453	
454	    this.lastSnapshotPollAt = now;
455	    this.pollingSnapshot = true;
456	    this.snapshotPollPromise = this.runSnapshotPoll();
457	    try {
458	      return await this.snapshotPollPromise;
459	    } finally {
460	      this.pollingSnapshot = false;
461	      this.snapshotPollPromise = null;
462	    }
463	  }
464	
465	  private async runSnapshotPoll(): Promise<boolean> {
466	    const gen = this.connectionGeneration;
467	    try {
468	      const result = await this.events.snapshot({
469	        runId: this.selectedRunId,
470	        persisted: true,
471	        checksum: this.getSnapshotChecksum?.() ?? null,
472	      }) as SnapshotPollResult;
473	      if (!this.active || gen !== this.connectionGeneration) {
474	        return false;
475	      }
476	      this.setLastEventId(result.lastEventId);
477	      const data = result.data;
478	      if (isNotModifiedSnapshot(data)) {
479	        this.workerEntries.onKnownSeqs(data.workerEntrySeqs);
480	        this.planManager.onKnownSeqs(data.workerEntrySeqs);
481	        this.sidebarWorkerActivity.onKnownSeqs(data.workerEntrySeqs);
482	        return true;
483	      }
484	      this.applyUpdate(data);
485	      this.workerEntries.onKnownSeqs(data?.workerEntrySeqs);
486	      this.planManager.onKnownSeqs(data?.workerEntrySeqs);
487	      this.sidebarWorkerActivity.onKnownSeqs(data?.workerEntrySeqs);
488	      return true;
489	    } catch (error) {
490	      if (this.active) {
491	        if (isTransientConnectivityError(error)) {
492	          return false;
493	        }
494	
495	        this.reportError(withFallbackErrorContext(error, {
496	          source: "Events",
497	          action: "Load live state snapshot",
498	        }));
499	      }
500	      return false;
501	    }
502	  }
503	
504	  private setLastEventId(lastEventId: string | number | null | undefined) {
505	    this.cursor.advance(lastEventId);
506	  }
507	}
```

```text
1	import type { WorkerStreamState } from "./WorkerEntriesManager";
2	import { coalesceWorkerEntriesById } from "./WorkerEntriesManager";
3	import type { WorkerEntry } from "@/shared/worker-entries";
4	import { withoutSupersededEntries, type SupersededSeqRange } from "@/lib/superseded-entries";
5	
6	export type ConversationLoadState = {
7	  snapshotLoaded: boolean;
8	  workerStreamRequired: boolean;
9	  workerStreamLoaded: boolean;
10	  fullyLoaded: boolean;
11	  loadingReason: "snapshot" | "worker_stream" | null;
12	};
13	
14	export function isWorkerStreamCaughtUp(streamState: WorkerStreamState) {
15	  return (
16	    streamState.status === "loaded"
17	    && streamState.latestContiguousSeq === streamState.latestKnownSeq
18	  );
19	}
20	
21	export function deriveConversationLoadState(args: {
22	  snapshotLoaded: boolean;
23	  unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled: boolean;
24	  primaryConversationWorkerId: string | null | undefined;
25	  streamState: WorkerStreamState;
26	  selectedRunIsTerminal?: boolean;
27	}): ConversationLoadState {
28	  const workerStreamRequired = Boolean(
29	    args.unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled
30	      && args.primaryConversationWorkerId
31	      && !args.selectedRunIsTerminal
32	  );
33	  const workerStreamLoaded = !workerStreamRequired || isWorkerStreamCaughtUp(args.streamState);
34	  const fullyLoaded = args.snapshotLoaded && workerStreamLoaded;
35	  const loadingReason = !args.snapshotLoaded
36	    ? "snapshot"
37	    : !workerStreamLoaded
38	      ? "worker_stream"
39	      : null;
40	
41	  return {
42	    snapshotLoaded: args.snapshotLoaded,
43	    workerStreamRequired,
44	    workerStreamLoaded,
45	    fullyLoaded,
46	    loadingReason,
47	  };
48	}
49	
50	export function shouldShowDirectWorkerStreamInitialLoading(args: {
51	  unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled: boolean;
52	  primaryConversationWorkerId: string | null | undefined;
53	  streamState: WorkerStreamState;
54	}) {
55	  return deriveConversationLoadState({
56	    ...args,
57	    snapshotLoaded: true,
58	  }).loadingReason === "worker_stream"
59	    && (args.streamState.status === "idle" || args.streamState.status === "loading")
60	    && args.streamState.entries.length === 0;
61	}
62	
63	export function shouldShowDirectConversationLoading(args: ConversationLoadState) {
64	  return Boolean(
65	    !args.fullyLoaded
66	    && args.loadingReason === "worker_stream"
67	  );
68	}
69	
70	export function selectDirectConversationEntries<T extends WorkerEntry>(args: {
71	  transcriptEntries: T[];
72	  directWorkerEntries: T[];
73	  // Ranges the live worker wrote for a branch a retry/edit discarded. The
74	  // transcript already drops them server-side; the live stream is served from
75	  // the raw JSONL (no database read on that hot path), so they are filtered
76	  // here before the two sources are merged — otherwise the rewound turn walks
77	  // straight back into the conversation.
78	  supersededSeqRanges?: SupersededSeqRange[];
79	  // The run's workers in creation order, so a message that exists on more than
80	  // one of them is placed where the newest worker has it.
81	  workerOrder?: ReadonlyArray<string>;
82	}) {
83	  const directWorkerEntries = withoutSupersededEntries(
84	    args.directWorkerEntries,
85	    args.supersededSeqRanges ?? [],
86	  );
87	
88	  if (args.transcriptEntries.length === 0) {
89	    return directWorkerEntries;
90	  }
91	
92	  return coalesceWorkerEntriesById([
93	    ...args.transcriptEntries,
94	    ...directWorkerEntries,
95	  ], args.workerOrder ?? []) as T[];
96	}
97	
98	export function resolveDirectWorkerStreamRefreshInterval(args: {
99	  unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled: boolean;
100	  primaryConversationWorkerId: string | null | undefined;
101	  activeRefreshIntervalMs: number;
102	  validationIntervalMs: number;
103	  showDirectControlWorkingIndicator: boolean;
104	  selectedRunIsTerminal?: boolean;
105	}) {
106	  if (!args.unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled || !args.primaryConversationWorkerId) {
107	    return null;
108	  }
109	
110	  // `worker.entry_appended` SSE frames fetch new content immediately. These
111	  // timers are only a safety net for a missed wake-up, so keep them out of the
112	  // packet hot path on mobile connections.
113	  const minimumActiveRefreshIntervalMs = 30_000;
114	  const minimumValidationIntervalMs = 60_000;
115	  return args.showDirectControlWorkingIndicator
116	    ? Math.max(args.activeRefreshIntervalMs, minimumActiveRefreshIntervalMs)
117	    : Math.max(args.validationIntervalMs, minimumValidationIntervalMs);
118	}
```

```text
8	const LIVE_TEXT_FIELD_CHARS = 100_000;
9	const LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_LIMIT = 80;
10	const LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS = 5_000;
11	const LIVE_RAW_STRING_CHARS = 4_000;
12	const ARCHIVE_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS = 1_000_000;
13	const ARCHIVE_RAW_STRING_CHARS = 1_000_000;
14	const ARCHIVE_TOOL_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS = 2_000;
15	const ARCHIVE_TOOL_RAW_STRING_CHARS = 8_000;
16	const TOOL_CALL_UPDATE_SUMMARY_CHARS = 2_000;

24	type OutputEntryInput = Omit<OutputEntry, "id" | "timestamp"> & { timestamp?: string };
25	
26	function nowIso() {
27	  return new Date().toISOString();
28	}
29	
30	function sanitizePathPart(input: string) {
31	  const sanitized = input.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9._-]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
32	  return sanitized || "agent";
33	}
34	
35	export function truncateString(value: string, maxChars: number) {
36	  if (value.length <= maxChars) {
37	    return value;
38	  }
39	  return `${value.slice(0, Math.max(0, maxChars - 37))}\n[truncated ${value.length - maxChars} chars]`;
40	}
41	
42	export function appendBoundedText(existing: string, chunk: string, maxChars = LIVE_TEXT_FIELD_CHARS) {
43	  if (chunk.length === 0) {
44	    return existing;
45	  }
46	  const next = existing + chunk;
47	  if (next.length <= maxChars) {
48	    return next;
49	  }
50	  return next.slice(-Math.max(0, maxChars));
51	}
52	
53	export function appendBoundedThoughts(existing: string, chunk: string, maxChars = LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS) {
54	  if (chunk.length === 0) {
55	    return existing;
56	  }
57	  const next = existing + chunk;
58	  if (next.length <= maxChars) {
59	    return next;
60	  }
61	
62	  // Get the raw end slice
63	  const candidate = next.slice(-maxChars);
64	
65	  // Find the first paragraph break in the sliced text to start cleanly
66	  const dblNewlineIdx = candidate.indexOf("\n\n");
67	  if (dblNewlineIdx !== -1) {
68	    return candidate.slice(dblNewlineIdx + 2);
69	  }
70	
71	  const newlineIdx = candidate.indexOf("\n");
72	  if (newlineIdx !== -1) {
73	    return candidate.slice(newlineIdx + 1);
74	  }
75	
76	  // Fallback to strict slice if no newlines are present
77	  return candidate;
78	}
79	
80	function compactRawValue(
81	  value: unknown,
82	  maxStringChars: number,
83	  depth = 0,
84	  seen = new WeakSet<object>(),
85	): unknown {
86	  if (typeof value === "string") {
87	    return truncateString(value, maxStringChars);
88	  }
89	  if (value == null || typeof value !== "object") {
90	    return value;
91	  }
92	  if (seen.has(value)) {
93	    return "[circular]";
94	  }
95	  if (depth >= RAW_DEPTH) {
96	    return "[truncated depth]";
97	  }
98	  seen.add(value);
99	
100	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
101	    const compacted = value.slice(0, RAW_ARRAY_ITEMS).map((item) => compactRawValue(item, maxStringChars, depth + 1, seen));
102	    if (value.length > RAW_ARRAY_ITEMS) {
103	      compacted.push(`[truncated ${value.length - RAW_ARRAY_ITEMS} items]`);
104	    }
105	    return compacted;
106	  }
107	
108	  const compacted: Record<string, unknown> = {};
109	  const entries = Object.entries(value);
110	  for (const [key, nestedValue] of entries.slice(0, RAW_OBJECT_KEYS)) {
111	    compacted[key] = compactRawValue(nestedValue, maxStringChars, depth + 1, seen);
112	  }
113	  if (entries.length > RAW_OBJECT_KEYS) {
114	    compacted.__truncatedKeys = entries.length - RAW_OBJECT_KEYS;
115	  }
116	  return compacted;
117	}
118	
119	function createArchiveEntry(input: OutputEntryInput): OutputEntry {
120	  const isToolEntry = input.type === "tool_call" || input.type === "tool_call_update" || input.type === "permission" || input.type === "elicitation";
121	  return {
122	    id: randomUUID(),
123	    timestamp: input.timestamp ?? nowIso(),
124	    type: input.type,
125	    text: truncateString(input.text, isToolEntry ? ARCHIVE_TOOL_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS : ARCHIVE_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS),
126	    toolCallId: input.toolCallId,
127	    toolKind: input.toolKind,
128	    status: input.status,
129	    raw: preserveInlineImageContentData(
130	      input.type,
131	      input.raw,
132	      compactRawValue(input.raw, isToolEntry ? ARCHIVE_TOOL_RAW_STRING_CHARS : ARCHIVE_RAW_STRING_CHARS),
133	    ),
134	  };
135	}
136	
137	function toLiveEntry(entry: OutputEntry): OutputEntry {
138	  return {
139	    ...entry,
140	    // Assistant messages are conversation content, not disposable runtime
141	    // diagnostics. They must remain complete so the unified worker stream can
142	    // persist the whole answer instead of a suffix-only live snapshot.
143	    text: entry.type === "message"
144	      ? entry.text
145	      : truncateString(entry.text, LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS),
146	    // Inline image payloads are conversation content for the same reason, and
147	    // the live entry is what reaches the durable worker stream.
148	    raw: preserveInlineImageContentData(
149	      entry.type,
150	      entry.raw,
151	      compactRawValue(entry.raw, LIVE_RAW_STRING_CHARS),
152	    ),
153	  };
154	}
155	
156	function lineByteLength(line: string) {
157	  return Buffer.byteLength(line) + 1;
158	}
159	
160	function parseOutputLine(line: string): OutputEntry | null {
161	  try {
162	    const parsed = JSON.parse(line) as OutputEntry;
163	    if (typeof parsed.id === "string" && typeof parsed.type === "string" && typeof parsed.text === "string") {
164	      return parsed;
165	    }
166	  } catch {
167	    return null;
168	  }
169	  return null;
170	}
171	
172	const ROTATED_ARCHIVE_KEEP = 3;
173	
174	/**
175	 * Keep the most recent `ROTATED_ARCHIVE_KEEP` rotations of an archive and drop
176	 * older ones, so preserving history for recovery cannot grow unbounded.
177	 */
178	function pruneRotatedArchives(filePath: string) {
179	  const dir = dirname(filePath);
180	  const prefix = `${basename(filePath)}.`;
181	  let rotated: string[];
182	  try {
183	    rotated = readdirSync(dir).filter((name) => name.startsWith(prefix) && name.endsWith(".prev"));
184	  } catch {
185	    return;
186	  }
187	  // Names embed an ISO timestamp, so lexicographic order is chronological.
188	  rotated.sort();
189	  for (const name of rotated.slice(0, Math.max(0, rotated.length - ROTATED_ARCHIVE_KEEP))) {
190	    rmSync(join(dir, name), { force: true });
191	  }
192	}
193	
194	export class AgentOutputArchive {
195	  private totalEntries = 0;
196	  private byteSize = 0;
197	
198	  constructor(
199	    readonly name: string,
200	    readonly filePath: string,
201	    input: { truncate?: boolean } = {},
202	  ) {
203	    mkdirSync(dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
204	    if (input.truncate && existsSync(filePath)) {
205	      // This archive is the last line of defence for a transcript: when a
206	      // worker stream file loses its head, `readAllPersistedEntries` recovers
207	      // from here. Deleting it on a non-resume start made that loss permanent,
208	      // so rotate the previous archive aside instead of destroying it.
209	      try {
210	        const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
211	        renameSync(filePath, `${filePath}.${stamp}.prev`);
212	        pruneRotatedArchives(filePath);
213	      } catch {
214	        // Rotation failed — we still must not append onto a stale archive.
215	        rmSync(filePath, { force: true });
216	      }
217	    }
218	    if (existsSync(filePath)) {
219	      this.rebuildStats();
220	    }
221	  }
222	
223	  append(input: OutputEntryInput): OutputEntry {
224	    const entry = createArchiveEntry(input);
225	    const line = JSON.stringify(entry);
226	    appendFileSync(this.filePath, `${line}\n`, "utf8");
227	    this.totalEntries += 1;
228	    this.byteSize += lineByteLength(line);
229	    return entry;
230	  }
231	
232	  stats(liveEntries = 0): OutputArchiveStats {
233	    return {
234	      totalEntries: this.totalEntries,
235	      byteSize: this.byteSize,
236	      logPath: this.filePath,
237	      liveEntries,
238	      omittedLiveEntries: Math.max(0, this.totalEntries - liveEntries),
239	    };
240	  }
241	
242	  async readPage(input: { cursor?: number; limit?: number } = {}): Promise<OutputArchivePage> {
243	    const cursor = Math.max(0, Math.floor(input.cursor ?? 0));
244	    const limit = Math.min(MAX_PAGE_LIMIT, Math.max(1, Math.floor(input.limit ?? DEFAULT_PAGE_LIMIT)));
245	    if (!existsSync(this.filePath) || cursor >= this.byteSize) {
246	      return {
247	        name: this.name,
248	        cursor,
249	        nextCursor: null,
250	        totalEntries: this.totalEntries,
251	        entries: [],
252	      };
253	    }
254	
255	    const entries: OutputEntry[] = [];
256	    let nextCursor = cursor;
257	    const stream = createReadStream(this.filePath, { encoding: "utf8", start: cursor });
258	    const lines = createInterface({ input: stream, crlfDelay: Infinity });
259	    for await (const line of lines) {
260	      nextCursor += lineByteLength(line);
261	      const entry = parseOutputLine(line);
262	      if (entry) {
263	        entries.push(entry);
264	      }
265	      if (entries.length >= limit) {
266	        break;
267	      }
268	    }
269	    lines.close();
270	    stream.destroy();
271	
272	    return {
273	      name: this.name,
274	      cursor,
275	      nextCursor: nextCursor < this.byteSize ? nextCursor : null,
276	      totalEntries: this.totalEntries,
277	      entries,
278	    };
279	  }
280	
281	  private rebuildStats() {
282	    const contents = statSync(this.filePath);
283	    this.byteSize = contents.size;
284	    this.totalEntries = 0;
285	    if (contents.size === 0) {
286	      return;
287	    }
288	    const fd = openSync(this.filePath, "r");
289	    try {
290	      const buffer = Buffer.allocUnsafe(64 * 1024);
291	      let bytesRead = 0;
292	      do {
293	        bytesRead = readSync(fd, buffer, 0, buffer.length, null);
294	        for (let index = 0; index < bytesRead; index += 1) {
295	          if (buffer[index] === 10) {
296	            this.totalEntries += 1;
297	          }
298	        }
299	      } while (bytesRead > 0);
300	    } finally {
301	      closeSync(fd);
302	    }
303	  }
304	}
305	
306	export function openAgentOutputArchive(input: { dataDir?: string | null; name: string; resume?: boolean }) {
307	  const dataDir = input.dataDir?.trim() || join(process.cwd(), ".omniharness");
308	  return new AgentOutputArchive(
309	    input.name,
310	    join(dataDir, "agent-runtime-output", `${sanitizePathPart(input.name)}.jsonl`),
311	    { truncate: !input.resume },
312	  );
313	}
314	
315	function pruneLiveEntries(record: AgentRecord) {
316	  const totalEntries = record.outputArchive.stats().totalEntries;
317	  const liveLimit = totalEntries > LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_LIMIT ? LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_LIMIT - 1 : LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_LIMIT;
318	  if (record.outputEntries.length <= liveLimit) {
319	    return;
320	  }
321	  record.outputEntries.splice(0, record.outputEntries.length - liveLimit);
322	  if (record.activeOutputEntryId && !record.outputEntries.some((entry) => entry.id === record.activeOutputEntryId)) {
323	    record.activeOutputEntryId = null;
324	  }
325	}
326	
327	/**
328	 * Record types that can land between two chunks of one assistant message
329	 * without meaning the message ended.
330	 *
331	 * A background terminal keeps emitting `tool_call_update` (plus the
332	 * `agent_content` and `usage` records that ride along with it) while the model
333	 * is still writing prose, and protocol metadata can arrive at any moment. None
334	 * of those are a turn boundary. Treating them as one restarts the in-progress
335	 * message on every interleave, so a paragraph written next to a running
336	 * command reaches the reader as one bubble per delta.
337	 *
338	 * Everything else — a new `tool_call`, a permission prompt, user input, a plan
339	 * — is a real boundary and still ends the run.
340	 */
341	const MESSAGE_RUN_PASSTHROUGH_TYPES = new Set([
342	  "tool_call_update",
343	  "agent_content",
344	  "usage",
345	  "available_commands",
346	  "current_mode",
347	  "config_option",
348	  "session_info",
349	]);
350	
351	export function appendOutputEntry(record: AgentRecord, input: OutputEntryInput) {
352	  const archiveEntry = record.outputArchive.append(input);
353	  if (!MESSAGE_RUN_PASSTHROUGH_TYPES.has(input.type)) {
354	    record.activeOutputEntryId = null;
355	  }
356	  record.outputEntries.push(toLiveEntry(archiveEntry));
357	  pruneLiveEntries(record);
358	}
359	
360	/**
361	 * Reassemble raw archive records into the entries the worker stream stores.
362	 *
363	 * The archive keeps every streaming chunk as its own record; the worker stream
364	 * keeps the assembled message. This is the read-side inverse of
365	 * `appendMessageChunk` below — same rule: same-type `message`/`thought` chunks
366	 * join across `MESSAGE_RUN_PASSTHROUGH_TYPES` records, any other record type
367	 * ends the run.
368	 *
369	 * Used when recovering a transcript whose stream file lost its head; replaying
370	 * the archive without it produces a transcript shredded mid-word.
371	 */
372	export function reassembleArchivedEntries<T extends { type?: string; text?: string }>(entries: T[]): T[] {
373	  const out: T[] = [];
374	  let active: T | null = null;
375	  for (const entry of entries) {
376	    if (entry.type === "message" || entry.type === "thought") {
377	      if (active && active.type === entry.type) {
378	        active.text = `${active.text ?? ""}${entry.text ?? ""}`;
379	        continue;
380	      }
381	      const started = { ...entry };
382	      out.push(started);
383	      active = started;
384	      continue;
385	    }
386	    out.push({ ...entry });
387	    if (!MESSAGE_RUN_PASSTHROUGH_TYPES.has(entry.type ?? "")) {
388	      active = null;
389	    }
390	  }
391	  return out;
392	}
393	
394	export function appendMessageChunk(record: AgentRecord, text: string, type: "message" | "thought") {
395	  const archiveEntry = record.outputArchive.append({ type, text });
396	  const activeEntry = record.activeOutputEntryId
397	    ? record.outputEntries.find((entry) => entry.id === record.activeOutputEntryId)
398	    : null;
399	
400	  if (activeEntry && activeEntry.type === type) {
401	    if (type === "thought") {
402	      activeEntry.text = appendBoundedThoughts(activeEntry.text, text, LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS);
403	    } else {
404	      activeEntry.text += text;
405	    }
406	    return;
407	  }
408	
409	  const liveEntry = toLiveEntry(archiveEntry);
410	  record.outputEntries.push(liveEntry);
411	  record.activeOutputEntryId = liveEntry.id;
412	  pruneLiveEntries(record);
413	}
414	
415	export function selectLiveOutputEntries(record: AgentRecord) {
416	  const stats = record.outputArchive.stats(record.outputEntries.length);
417	  if (stats.omittedLiveEntries <= 0) {
418	    return record.outputEntries.map((entry) => ({ ...entry }));
419	  }
420	
421	  const first = record.outputEntries[0];
422	  const marker: OutputEntry = {
423	    id: ARCHIVE_MARKER_ID,
424	    type: "message",
425	    text: `${stats.omittedLiveEntries} older raw worker activity records are only in archived history, not in the current terminal output.`,
426	    timestamp: first?.timestamp ?? new Date(0).toISOString(),
427	    status: "archived",
428	  };
429	  return [marker, ...record.outputEntries.map((entry) => ({ ...entry }))];
430	}
431	
432	export function summarizeToolCallUpdate(update: Record<string, unknown>) {
433	  const contentSummary = Array.isArray(update.content)
434	    ? update.content
435	        .map((item) => {
436	          const record = typeof item === "object" && item !== null && !Array.isArray(item) ? item as Record<string, unknown> : null;
437	          const content = typeof record?.content === "object" && record.content !== null && !Array.isArray(record.content)
438	            ? record.content as Record<string, unknown>
439	            : null;
440	          if (record?.type === "content" && content?.type === "text" && typeof content.text === "string") {
441	            return content.text;
442	          }
443	          if (record?.type === "content" && content?.type) {
444	            return `[${content.type}]`;
445	          }
446	          return "";
447	        })
448	        .filter(Boolean)
449	        .join(" ")
450	    : "";
451	
452	  const toolCallId = typeof update.toolCallId === "string" ? update.toolCallId : "";
453	  const status = typeof update.status === "string" ? update.status : "updated";
454	  const base = `Tool call ${toolCallId} ${status}`.trim();
455	  return truncateString(contentSummary ? `${base}: ${contentSummary}` : base, TOOL_CALL_UPDATE_SUMMARY_CHARS);
456	}
457	
458	export function renderOutputEntries(entries: OutputEntry[]) {
459	  return truncateString(entries
460	    .map((entry) => {
461	      switch (entry.type) {
462	        case "message":
463	          return entry.text;
464	        case "thought":
465	          return `Thought: ${entry.text}`;
466	        case "tool_call":
467	          return `Tool${entry.toolKind ? ` ${entry.toolKind}` : ""}${entry.status ? ` (${entry.status})` : ""}: ${entry.text}`;
468	        case "tool_call_update":
469	          return `${entry.status ? `Tool update (${entry.status})` : "Tool update"}: ${entry.text}`;
470	        case "permission":
471	          return `Permission: ${entry.text}`;
472	        default:
473	          return entry.text;
474	      }
475	    })
476	    .filter(Boolean)
477	    .join("\n\n"), LIVE_TEXT_FIELD_CHARS);
478	}
```
