147	function unwrapCodeFence(value: string): { text: string; language?: string } {
148	  const normalized = normalizeMultilineText(value).trim();
149	  const match = normalized.match(/^```([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)?\n([\s\S]*?)\n```$/);
150	  if (!match) {
151	    return { text: normalized };
152	  }
153	
154	  return {
155	    text: match[2] ?? "",
156	    language: match[1] || undefined,
157	  };
158	}
159	
160	function stripLineNumbersIfNeeded(value: string): string {
161	  const lines = value.split("\n");
162	  const contentLines = lines.filter((line) => line.trim().length > 0);
163	  if (contentLines.length < 2) {
164	    return value;
165	  }
166	
167	  const numberedLines = contentLines.filter((line) => /^\s*\d+\s{2,}\S/.test(line)).length;
168	  if (numberedLines < Math.max(2, Math.ceil(contentLines.length * 0.5))) {
169	    return value;
170	  }
171	
172	  return lines.map((line) => line.replace(/^\s*\d+\s{2,}/, "")).join("\n");
173	}
174	
175	function cleanPaneText(value: string | null): string | null {
176	  if (!value) {

181	  const stripped = stripLineNumbersIfNeeded(unfenced.text)

185	  return stripped.trim().length > 0 ? stripped : null;
186	}

235	/**
236	 * Entry types a background terminal keeps emitting while the model writes.
237	 *
238	 * They sit between the chunks of one assistant message without ending it, so
239	 * the fragment run has to survive them. Mirrors
240	 * `MESSAGE_RUN_PASSTHROUGH_TYPES` in `@/server/agent-runtime/output-store`.
241	 */
242	const FRAGMENT_RUN_PASSTHROUGH_TYPES = new Set([
243	  "tool_call_update",
244	  "agent_content",
245	  "usage",

247	  "current_mode",

252	function isFragmentedMessageCandidate(entry: AgentOutputEntry) {
253	  if (entry.type !== "message" || entry.toolCallId || entry.status) {
254	    return false;
255	  }
256	  const text = entry.text ?? "";
257	  if (text.length === 0) {
258	    return false;
259	  }
260	  // Length alone misses the long tail of a shredded paragraph. A chunk that
261	  // opens mid-sentence — leading space, or a joiner like the "-" of a
262	  // hyphenated word split across deltas — is a continuation at any length.
263	  return text.length <= 24 || MESSAGE_CONTINUATION_START.test(text);
264	}
265	
266	function hasFragmentedMessageEvidence(entries: AgentOutputEntry[]) {
267	  if (entries.length < 4) {
268	    return false;
269	  }
270	
271	  const totalLength = entries.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + (entry.text?.length ?? 0), 0);
272	  if (totalLength < 32) {
273	    return false;
274	  }
275	
276	  return entries.some((entry) => {
277	    const text = entry.text ?? "";
278	    return /^\s/.test(text)
279	      || /\s$/.test(text)
280	      || /^[`.,:;!?()[\]{}<>=+\-_/\\\n]/.test(text);
281	  });
282	}
283	
284	function mergeFragmentedMessageRun(entries: AgentOutputEntry[]) {
285	  const first = entries[0]!;
286	  const last = entries[entries.length - 1]!;
287	  return {
288	    ...first,
289	    id: `message-fragment-run:${first.id}:${last.id}`,
290	    text: entries.map((entry) => normalizeMultilineText(entry.text ?? "")).join(""),
291	    timestamp: first.timestamp,
292	  } satisfies AgentOutputEntry;
293	}
294	
295	function coalesceFragmentedMessageEntries(entries: AgentOutputEntry[]) {
296	  const coalesced: AgentOutputEntry[] = [];
297	  // `buffered` holds the whole run in arrival order; `fragments` is the subset
298	  // that gets joined into one bubble. They differ whenever a background
299	  // terminal interleaves output between two chunks of the same message.
300	  let buffered: AgentOutputEntry[] = [];
301	  let fragments: AgentOutputEntry[] = [];
302	  let fragmentSet = new Set<AgentOutputEntry>();
303	  const fragmentIds = new Set<string>();
304	
305	  const flush = () => {
306	    if (buffered.length === 0) {
307	      return;
308	    }
309	    if (hasFragmentedMessageEvidence(fragments)) {
310	      coalesced.push(mergeFragmentedMessageRun(fragments));
311	      for (const entry of buffered) {
312	        if (!fragmentSet.has(entry)) {
313	          coalesced.push(entry);
314	        }
315	      }
316	    } else {
317	      coalesced.push(...buffered);
318	    }
319	    buffered = [];
320	    fragments = [];
321	    fragmentSet = new Set();
322	    fragmentIds.clear();
323	  };
324	
325	  for (const entry of entries) {
326	    // A repeated id is a growing revision of one entry, not a new fragment.
327	    // Joining those would print the message once per revision.
328	    if (isFragmentedMessageCandidate(entry) && !fragmentIds.has(entry.id)) {
329	      buffered.push(entry);
330	      fragments.push(entry);
331	      fragmentSet.add(entry);
332	      fragmentIds.add(entry.id);
333	      continue;
334	    }
335	    if (fragments.length > 0 && FRAGMENT_RUN_PASSTHROUGH_TYPES.has(entry.type)) {
336	      buffered.push(entry);
337	      continue;
338	    }
339	    flush();
340	    coalesced.push(entry);
341	  }
342	
343	  flush();
344	  return coalesced;
345	}

347	function stringifyToolRawOutput(value: unknown): string | null {
348	  const rawOutput = asRecord(value);
349	  if (rawOutput && typeof rawOutput.formatted_output === "string") {
350	    return asNonEmptyString(rawOutput.formatted_output);
351	  }
352	
353	  return stringifyUnknown(value);
354	}

356	function extractContentText(value: unknown): string | null {
357	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
358	    return null;
359	  }
360	
361	  const parts = value
362	    .map((item) => {
363	      const record = asRecord(item);
364	      if (!record) {
365	        return null;
366	      }
367	
368	      const nested = asRecord(record.content);
369	      if (record.type === "content" && nested?.type === "text" && typeof nested.text === "string") {
370	        return nested.text;
371	      }
372	
373	      if (typeof record.text === "string") {
374	        return record.text;
375	      }
376	
377	      return null;
378	    })
379	    .filter((part): part is string => Boolean(part));
380	
381	  return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join("\n\n") : null;
382	}

384	function extractPrimaryPath(raw: Record<string, unknown> | null): string | null {
385	  if (!raw) {
386	    return null;
387	  }
388	
389	  const locations = Array.isArray(raw.locations) ? raw.locations : [];

393	    if (path) {
394	      return path;
395	    }
396	  }
397	
398	  const rawInput = asRecord(raw.rawInput);
399	  const meta = asRecord(raw._meta);

402	  const responseFile = asRecord(toolResponse?.file);
403	  const candidates = [
404	    raw.path,
405	    raw.filePath,

410	    rawInput?.file_path,
411	    rawInput?.filename,
412	    responseFile?.filePath,
413	    responseFile?.file_path,
414	  ];
415	
416	  for (const candidate of candidates) {
417	    const path = asNonEmptyString(candidate);
418	    if (path) {
419	      return path;
420	    }
421	  }
422	
423	  return null;
424	}

528	function pathPrefixForDiff(path: string | null, diff: string): string {
529	  if (!path || /^(?:diff --|--- |\+\+\+ )/m.test(diff) || diff.includes(path)) {
530	    return "";
531	  }
532	  return `diff -- ${path}\n`;
533	}
534	
535	function stringLooksLikeDiff(value: string): boolean {
536	  const normalized = normalizeMultilineText(value).trim();
537	  return normalized.length > 0 && (
538	    /^@@\s/m.test(normalized)
539	    || /^diff --/m.test(normalized)
540	    || /^\*\*\* (?:Begin Patch|Update File|Add File|Delete File)/m.test(normalized)
541	  );
542	}
543	
544	function extractStringDiff(value: unknown): string | null {
545	  if (typeof value !== "string" || !stringLooksLikeDiff(value)) {
546	    return null;
547	  }
548	
549	  return normalizeMultilineText(value).trim();
550	}
551	
552	function buildLineDiff(path: string | null, marker: "+" | "-", value: unknown, label: string): string | null {
553	  if (typeof value !== "string") {
554	    return null;
555	  }
556	
557	  const normalized = normalizeMultilineText(value);
558	  if (normalized.length === 0) {
559	    return null;
560	  }
561	
562	  const lines = normalized.split("\n").map((line) => `${marker}${line}`).join("\n");
563	  return `${pathPrefixForDiff(path, "")}@@ ${label} @@\n${lines}`;
564	}
565	
566	type ReplacementDiffOp = {
567	  kind: "context" | "remove" | "add";
568	  text: string;
569	  oldLine?: number;
570	  newLine?: number;
571	};
572	
573	const REPLACEMENT_DIFF_CONTEXT_LINES = 1;
574	const REPLACEMENT_DIFF_MAX_LCS_CELLS = 1_500_000;
575	
576	function buildFallbackReplacementDiff(path: string | null, oldString: string, newString: string): string {
577	  const removed = oldString.split("\n").map((line) => `-${line}`).join("\n");
578	  const added = newString.split("\n").map((line) => `+${line}`).join("\n");
579	  return `${pathPrefixForDiff(path, "")}@@ -1,${oldString.split("\n").length} +1,${newString.split("\n").length} @@\n${removed}\n${added}`;
580	}
581	
582	function diffReplacementLines(oldLines: string[], newLines: string[]): ReplacementDiffOp[] | null {
583	  const width = newLines.length + 1;
584	  const cells = (oldLines.length + 1) * width;
585	  if (cells > REPLACEMENT_DIFF_MAX_LCS_CELLS) {
586	    return null;
587	  }
588	
589	  const table = new Uint32Array(cells);
590	  const at = (oldIndex: number, newIndex: number) => oldIndex * width + newIndex;
591	
592	  for (let oldIndex = oldLines.length - 1; oldIndex >= 0; oldIndex -= 1) {
593	    for (let newIndex = newLines.length - 1; newIndex >= 0; newIndex -= 1) {
594	      table[at(oldIndex, newIndex)] = oldLines[oldIndex] === newLines[newIndex]
595	        ? table[at(oldIndex + 1, newIndex + 1)] + 1
596	        : Math.max(table[at(oldIndex + 1, newIndex)], table[at(oldIndex, newIndex + 1)]);
597	    }
598	  }
599	
600	  const ops: ReplacementDiffOp[] = [];
601	  let oldIndex = 0;
602	  let newIndex = 0;
603	  let oldLine = 1;
604	  let newLine = 1;
605	
606	  while (oldIndex < oldLines.length && newIndex < newLines.length) {
607	    if (oldLines[oldIndex] === newLines[newIndex]) {
608	      ops.push({ kind: "context", text: oldLines[oldIndex], oldLine, newLine });
609	      oldIndex += 1;
610	      newIndex += 1;
611	      oldLine += 1;
612	      newLine += 1;
613	      continue;
614	    }
615	
616	    if (table[at(oldIndex + 1, newIndex)] >= table[at(oldIndex, newIndex + 1)]) {
617	      ops.push({ kind: "remove", text: oldLines[oldIndex], oldLine });
618	      oldIndex += 1;
619	      oldLine += 1;
620	    } else {
621	      ops.push({ kind: "add", text: newLines[newIndex], newLine });
622	      newIndex += 1;
623	      newLine += 1;
624	    }
625	  }
626	
627	  while (oldIndex < oldLines.length) {
628	    ops.push({ kind: "remove", text: oldLines[oldIndex], oldLine });
629	    oldIndex += 1;
630	    oldLine += 1;
631	  }
632	
633	  while (newIndex < newLines.length) {
634	    ops.push({ kind: "add", text: newLines[newIndex], newLine });
635	    newIndex += 1;
636	    newLine += 1;
637	  }
638	
639	  return ops;
640	}
641	
642	function replacementDiffHunks(ops: ReplacementDiffOp[], contextLines = REPLACEMENT_DIFF_CONTEXT_LINES): ReplacementDiffOp[][] {
643	  const changedIndexes = ops
644	    .map((op, index) => (op.kind === "context" ? -1 : index))
645	    .filter((index) => index >= 0);
646	
647	  if (changedIndexes.length === 0) {
648	    return [];
649	  }
650	
651	  const ranges: Array<{ start: number; end: number }> = [];
652	  for (const index of changedIndexes) {
653	    const start = Math.max(0, index - contextLines);
654	    const end = Math.min(ops.length, index + contextLines + 1);
655	    const last = ranges[ranges.length - 1];
656	    if (last && start <= last.end) {
657	      last.end = Math.max(last.end, end);
658	    } else {
659	      ranges.push({ start, end });
660	    }
661	  }
662	
663	  return ranges.map((range) => ops.slice(range.start, range.end));
664	}
665	
666	function formatHunkRange(start: number, count: number): string {
667	  return count === 1 ? `${start}` : `${start},${count}`;
668	}
669	
670	function hunkHeader(hunk: ReplacementDiffOp[]): string {
671	  const oldLines = hunk.filter((op) => op.kind !== "add");
672	  const newLines = hunk.filter((op) => op.kind !== "remove");
673	  const firstOld = oldLines[0]?.oldLine ?? hunk[0]?.oldLine ?? 1;
674	  const firstNew = newLines[0]?.newLine ?? hunk[0]?.newLine ?? 1;
675	  return `@@ -${formatHunkRange(firstOld, oldLines.length)} +${formatHunkRange(firstNew, newLines.length)} @@`;
676	}
677	
678	function formatReplacementDiffOp(op: ReplacementDiffOp): string {
679	  if (op.kind === "add") {
680	    return `+${op.text}`;
681	  }
682	  if (op.kind === "remove") {
683	    return `-${op.text}`;
684	  }
685	  return ` ${op.text}`;
686	}
687	
688	function collectChangeDiffs(value: unknown): string[] {
689	  const changes = asRecord(value);
690	  if (!changes) {
691	    return [];
692	  }
693	
694	  return Object.entries(changes)
695	    .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right))
696	    .flatMap(([path, change]) => {
697	      const record = asRecord(change);
698	      const diff = extractStringDiff(record?.unified_diff)
699	        || extractStringDiff(record?.diff)
700	        || extractStringDiff(record?.patch);
701	      if (diff) {
702	        return [`${pathPrefixForDiff(path, diff)}${diff}`];
703	      }
704	
705	      if (record?.type === "add") {
706	        const addDiff = buildLineDiff(path, "+", record.content, "add");
707	        return addDiff ? [addDiff] : [];
708	      }
709	
710	      if (record?.type === "delete") {
711	        const deleteDiff = buildLineDiff(path, "-", record.content, "delete");
712	        return deleteDiff ? [deleteDiff] : [];
713	      }
714	
715	      return [];
716	    });
717	}
718	
719	function buildReplacementDiffFromStrings(path: string | null, oldValue: unknown, newValue: unknown): string | null {
720	  const oldString = typeof oldValue === "string" ? normalizeMultilineText(oldValue) : null;
721	  const newString = typeof newValue === "string" ? normalizeMultilineText(newValue) : null;
722	  if (oldString == null || newString == null || oldString === newString) {
723	    return null;
724	  }
725	
726	  const oldLines = oldString.split("\n");
727	  const newLines = newString.split("\n");
728	  const ops = diffReplacementLines(oldLines, newLines);
729	  if (!ops) {
730	    return buildFallbackReplacementDiff(path, oldString, newString);
731	  }
732	
733	  const hunks = replacementDiffHunks(ops);
734	  if (hunks.length === 0) {
735	    return null;
736	  }
737	
738	  const hunkText = hunks
739	    .map((hunk) => [
740	      hunkHeader(hunk),
741	      ...hunk.map(formatReplacementDiffOp),
742	    ].join("\n"))
743	    .join("\n");
744	
745	  return `${pathPrefixForDiff(path, "")}${hunkText}`;
746	}
747	
748	function buildReplacementDiff(rawInput: Record<string, unknown> | null): string | null {
749	  if (!rawInput) {
750	    return null;
751	  }
752	
753	  return buildReplacementDiffFromStrings(
754	    extractPrimaryPath({ rawInput }),
755	    rawInput.old_string,
756	    rawInput.new_string,
757	  );
758	}
759	
760	function collectContentDiffs(value: unknown): string[] {
761	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
762	    return [];
763	  }
764	
765	  return value.flatMap((item) => {
766	    const record = asRecord(item);
767	    if (!record) {
768	      return [];
769	    }
770	
771	    const nested = asRecord(record.content);
772	    const diff = extractStringDiff(nested?.text) || extractStringDiff(record.text);
773	    if (diff) {
774	      return [diff];
775	    }
776	
777	    const contentKind = asNonEmptyString(record.type) || asNonEmptyString(asRecord(record._meta)?.kind);
778	    if (contentKind === "diff" || contentKind === "modify") {
779	      const replacementDiff = buildReplacementDiffFromStrings(
780	        asNonEmptyString(record.path),
781	        record.oldText,
782	        record.newText,
783	      );
784	      if (replacementDiff) {
785	        return [replacementDiff];
786	      }
787	
788	      const addDiff = buildLineDiff(asNonEmptyString(record.path), "+", record.newText, "add");
789	      if (addDiff) {
790	        return [addDiff];
791	      }
792	
793	      const deleteDiff = buildLineDiff(asNonEmptyString(record.path), "-", record.oldText, "delete");
794	      return deleteDiff ? [deleteDiff] : [];
795	    }
796	
797	    return [];
798	  });
799	}
800	
801	function extractToolDiff(raw: Record<string, unknown> | null, contentText?: string | null): string | null {
802	  if (!raw) {
803	    return null;
804	  }
805	
806	  const rawInput = asRecord(raw.rawInput);
807	  const rawOutput = asRecord(raw.rawOutput);
808	  const diffs = [
809	    ...collectChangeDiffs(rawOutput?.changes),
810	    ...collectChangeDiffs(rawInput?.changes),
811	    ...collectContentDiffs(raw.content),
812	  ];
813	
814	  for (const candidate of [
815	    rawOutput?.unified_diff,
816	    rawOutput?.diff,
817	    rawOutput?.patch,
818	    rawInput?.unified_diff,
819	    rawInput?.diff,
820	    rawInput?.patch,
821	    rawInput?.text,
822	    contentText ?? null,
823	  ]) {
824	    const diff = extractStringDiff(candidate);
825	    if (diff) {
826	      diffs.push(diff);
827	    }
828	  }
829	
830	  const replacementDiff = buildReplacementDiff(rawInput);
831	  if (replacementDiff) {
832	    diffs.push(replacementDiff);
833	  }
834	
835	  const uniqueDiffs = [...new Set(diffs)];
836	  return uniqueDiffs.length > 0 ? uniqueDiffs.join("\n\n") : null;
837	}
838	
839	function normalizeToolKind(toolKind: string | null | undefined, title: string): AgentToolActivityKind {
840	  const haystack = `${toolKind ?? ""} ${title}`.toLowerCase();
841	  if (/\b(read|open|view)\b/.test(haystack)) {
842	    return "read";
843	  }
844	  if (/\b(agent|delegate|dispatch|spawn|worker)\b/.test(haystack)) {
845	    return "agent";
846	  }
847	  if (/\b(edit|write|replace|patch|create)\b/.test(haystack)) {
848	    return "edit";
849	  }
850	  if (/\b(execute|terminal|shell|bash|command|run)\b/.test(haystack)) {
851	    return "bash";
852	  }
853	  if (/\b(search|find|grep|glob)\b/.test(haystack)) {
854	    return "search";
855	  }
856	  return "tool";
857	}
858	
859	function isAskUserQuestionEntry(entry: AgentOutputEntry) {
860	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
861	  const meta = asRecord(raw?._meta);
862	  const claudeCode = asRecord(meta?.claudeCode);
863	  return asNonEmptyString(claudeCode?.toolName) === "AskUserQuestion";
864	}
865	
866	function normalizeToolKindForEntry(entry: AgentOutputEntry, title: string): AgentToolActivityKind {

868	    return "tool";
869	  }
870	
871	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
872	  return normalizeToolKind(entry.toolKind ?? asNonEmptyString(raw?.kind), title);
873	}
874	
875	function toolLabel(kind: AgentToolActivityKind): string {
876	  switch (kind) {
877	    case "read":
878	      return "Read";
879	    case "bash":
880	      return "Bash";
881	    case "agent":
882	      return "Agent";
883	    case "edit":
884	      return "Edit";
885	    case "search":
886	      return "Search";
887	    default:
888	      return "Tool";
889	  }
890	}
891	
892	function deriveToolTitle(entry: AgentOutputEntry): string {
893	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
894	  const rawTitle = asNonEmptyString(raw?.title);
895	  const baseTitle = rawTitle || entry.text || "Tool activity";
896	  const kind = normalizeToolKindForEntry(entry, baseTitle);
897	  const primaryPath = extractPrimaryPath(raw);
898	  const promptLike = extractDescriptionLikeInput(raw) || summarizePromptLikeInput(raw);
899	
900	  if ((kind === "read" || kind === "edit") && primaryPath) {
901	    return basenamePath(primaryPath);
902	  }
903	
904	  const titlePath = kind === "read" || kind === "edit" ? pathFromToolTitle(baseTitle) : null;
905	  if (titlePath) {
906	    return basenamePath(titlePath);
907	  }
908	
909	  if ((kind === "bash" || kind === "agent") && promptLike) {
910	    return truncateInline(promptLike);
911	  }
912	
913	  if (primaryPath) {
914	    return basenamePath(primaryPath);
915	  }
916	
917	  return truncateInline(baseTitle);
918	}
919	
920	function deriveToolInputPane(entry: AgentOutputEntry): AgentOutputPane | undefined {
921	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
922	  const kind = normalizeToolKindForEntry(entry, asNonEmptyString(raw?.title) || entry.text || "");
923	  const promptLike = extractCommandLikeInput(raw) || summarizePromptLikeInput(raw);
924	
925	  if (!promptLike) {
926	    return undefined;
927	  }
928	
929	  if (kind === "bash") {
930	    const text = cleanPaneText(promptLike);
931	    return text ? { label: "IN", text } : undefined;
932	  }
933	
934	  return undefined;
935	}
936	
937	function extractSummaryTail(entry: AgentOutputEntry): string | null {
938	  const colonIndex = entry.text.indexOf(":");
939	  if (colonIndex === -1) {
940	    return null;
941	  }
942	  return entry.text.slice(colonIndex + 1).trim();
943	}
944	
945	function deriveToolOutputPane(entry: AgentOutputEntry): AgentOutputPane | undefined {
946	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
947	  const kind = normalizeToolKindForEntry(entry, asNonEmptyString(raw?.title) || entry.text || "");
948	  const meta = asRecord(raw?._meta);
949	  const claudeCode = asRecord(meta?.claudeCode);
950	  const toolResponse = asRecord(claudeCode?.toolResponse);
951	  const file = asRecord(toolResponse?.file);
952	  const stdout = asNonEmptyString(toolResponse?.stdout);
953	  const stderr = asNonEmptyString(toolResponse?.stderr);
954	  const rawOutput = raw ? raw.rawOutput : undefined;
955	  const contentText = extractContentText(raw?.content);
956	  const rawOutputText = stringifyToolRawOutput(rawOutput);
957	  const metaOutputText = stringifyUnknown(file?.content) || stdout || stderr;
958	  const summaryTail = extractSummaryTail(entry);
959	  const isTerminal = entry.status ? TERMINAL_TOOL_STATUSES.has(entry.status) : false;
960	  const structuredDiffText = extractToolDiff(raw);
961	  const diffText = structuredDiffText || (kind === "edit" ? extractToolDiff(raw, contentText) : null);
962	
963	  if (diffText) {
964	    return { label: "DIFF", text: diffText, kind: "diff" };
965	  }
966	
967	  if (!isTerminal && !metaOutputText && !rawOutputText) {
968	    return undefined;
969	  }
970	
971	  const text = cleanPaneText(metaOutputText || contentText || rawOutputText || summaryTail);
972	
973	  return text ? { label: "OUT", text, kind: "text" } : undefined;
974	}
975	
976	function normalizeStatus(value: string | null | undefined, fallback = "pending"): string {
977	  if (!value) {
978	    return fallback;
979	  }
980	  const normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase();

983	
984	function permissionTitleKeyForStatus(status: string) {
985	  if (status === "approved") {
986	    return "terminal.permission.approved";
987	  }
988	  if (status === "denied") {
989	    return "terminal.permission.denied";
990	  }
991	  if (status === "cancelled" || status === "canceled") {
992	    return "terminal.permission.cancelled";
993	  }
994	  return "terminal.permission.requested";
995	}
996	
997	function permissionRequestId(entry: AgentOutputEntry): number | null {
998	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
999	  const requestId = raw?.requestId;
1000	  if (typeof requestId === "number" && Number.isFinite(requestId)) {
1001	    return requestId;
1002	  }
1003	  const match = entry.text.match(/\brequest\s+(\d+)\b/i);
1004	  if (!match) {
1005	    return null;
1006	  }
1007	  const parsed = Number(match[1]);
1008	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
1009	}
1010	
1011	function permissionToolCallDetail(entry: AgentOutputEntry): string | null {
1012	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
1013	  const toolCall = asRecord(raw?.toolCall);
1014	  if (toolCall) {
1015	    const title = asNonEmptyString(toolCall.title);
1016	    const kind = asNonEmptyString(toolCall.kind);
1017	    if (title && kind) {
1018	      return `${kind}: ${title}`;
1019	    }
1020	    return title ?? kind;
1021	  }
1022	
1023	  const requestedTarget = entry.text.match(/^Permission requested for\s+(.+?)(?::\s*(?:allow|reject|proceed)[\w-]*\b.*)?$/i);
1024	  return requestedTarget?.[1]?.trim() || null;
1025	}
1026	
1027	function permissionDisplayText(entry: AgentOutputEntry, detail: string | null | undefined): string {
1028	  if (detail || /^Permission (?:requested|approved|denied|cancelled|canceled)\b/i.test(entry.text)) {
1029	    return "";
1030	  }
1031	  return normalizeMultilineText(entry.text || "").trim();
1032	}
1033	
1034	function isFinalToolStatus(value: string | null | undefined): boolean {
1035	  return value ? TERMINAL_TOOL_STATUSES.has(value.trim().toLowerCase()) : false;
1036	}
1037	
1038	function mergeToolStatus(currentStatus: string, nextStatus: string | null | undefined): string {
1039	  const normalizedNext = normalizeStatus(nextStatus, currentStatus);
1040	  if (isFinalToolStatus(currentStatus) && !isFinalToolStatus(normalizedNext)) {
1041	    return currentStatus;
1042	  }
1043	  return normalizedNext;
1044	}
1045	
1046	function isFailedToolStatus(value: string | null | undefined): boolean {
1047	  const normalized = value?.trim().toLowerCase();
1048	  return normalized === "failed" || normalized === "error" || normalized === "cancelled" || normalized === "canceled";
1049	}
1050	
1051	function isReplaceableToolTitle(value: string): boolean {
1052	  return ["Tool activity", "Terminal", "Read File", "Execute", "Bash"].includes(value)
1053	    || FALLBACK_TOOL_TITLE_PATTERN.test(value);
1054	}
1055	
1056	function createToolActivity(entry: AgentOutputEntry): MutableToolActivity {
1057	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
1058	  const baseTitle = asNonEmptyString(raw?.title) || entry.text || "Tool activity";
1059	  const kind = normalizeToolKindForEntry(entry, baseTitle);
1060	
1061	  return {
1062	    id: entry.toolCallId || entry.id,
1063	    kind: "tool",
1064	    actionKind: kind,
1065	    label: toolLabel(kind),
1066	    title: deriveToolTitle(entry),
1067	    status: normalizeStatus(entry.status),
1068	    timestamp: entry.timestamp,
1069	    targetPath: extractToolTargetPath(raw, kind, baseTitle),
1070	    inputPane: deriveToolInputPane(entry),
1071	    outputPane: deriveToolOutputPane(entry),
1072	  };
1073	}
1074	
1075	function applyToolUpdate(target: MutableToolActivity, entry: AgentOutputEntry): void {
1076	  target.status = mergeToolStatus(target.status, entry.status);
1077	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
1078	  const baseTitle = asNonEmptyString(raw?.title) || entry.text || "Tool activity";
1079	  const incomingKind = normalizeToolKindForEntry(entry, baseTitle);
1080	  const incomingLabel = toolLabel(incomingKind);
1081	  if (target.label === "Tool" && incomingLabel !== "Tool") {
1082	    target.label = incomingLabel;
1083	  }
1084	  if (target.actionKind === "tool" && incomingKind !== "tool") {
1085	    target.actionKind = incomingKind;
1086	  }
1087	
1088	  const updatedTitle = deriveToolTitle(entry);
1089	  if (
1090	    updatedTitle
1091	    && updatedTitle !== target.title
1092	    && isReplaceableToolTitle(target.title)
1093	  ) {
1094	    target.title = updatedTitle;
1095	  }
1096	
1097	  const inputPane = deriveToolInputPane(entry);
1098	  if (inputPane && !target.inputPane) {
1099	    target.inputPane = inputPane;
1100	  }
1101	
1102	  const targetPath = extractToolTargetPath(raw, incomingKind, baseTitle);
1103	  if (targetPath && !target.targetPath) {
1104	    target.targetPath = targetPath;
1105	  }
1106	
1107	  const outputPane = deriveToolOutputPane(entry);
1108	  if (outputPane) {
1109	    if (
1110	      target.outputPane?.kind === "diff"
1111	      && outputPane.kind !== "diff"
1112	      && target.actionKind === "edit"
1113	      && !isFailedToolStatus(target.status)
1114	    ) {
1115	      return;
1116	    }
1117	    target.outputPane = outputPane;
1118	  }
1119	}
1120	
1121	function countToolGroup(tools: AgentToolActivity[]): AgentToolGroupCounts {
1122	  const editedFiles = new Set<string>();
1123	  const readFiles = new Set<string>();
1124	  let searches = 0;
1125	  let commands = 0;
1126	  let agents = 0;
1127	  let genericTools = 0;
1128	
1129	  for (const tool of tools) {
1130	    if (tool.actionKind === "edit") {
1131	      // Dedupe by target path when we have one, otherwise count each tool
1132	      // call separately. Previously a missing targetPath silently dropped
1133	      // the edit from the summary entirely.
1134	      editedFiles.add(tool.targetPath || `:${tool.id}`);
1135	      continue;
1136	    }
1137	    if (tool.actionKind === "read") {
1138	      readFiles.add(tool.targetPath || `:${tool.id}`);
1139	      continue;
1140	    }
1141	    if (tool.actionKind === "search") {
1142	      searches += 1;
1143	      continue;
1144	    }
1145	    if (tool.actionKind === "bash") {
1146	      commands += 1;
1147	      continue;
1148	    }
1149	    if (tool.actionKind === "agent") {
1150	      agents += 1;
1151	      continue;
1152	    }
1153	    genericTools += 1;
1154	  }
1155	
1156	  return {
1157	    editedFiles: editedFiles.size,
1158	    readFiles: readFiles.size,
1159	    searches,
1160	    commands,
1161	    agents,
1162	    tools: genericTools,
1163	    total: tools.length,
1164	  };
1165	}
1166	
1167	function deriveToolGroupStatus(tools: AgentToolActivity[]): string {
1168	  if (tools.some((tool) => ["failed", "error"].includes(tool.status))) {
1169	    return "failed";
1170	  }
1171	  if (tools.some((tool) => ["cancelled", "canceled"].includes(tool.status))) {
1172	    return "cancelled";
1173	  }
1174	  if (tools.some((tool) => RUNNING_TOOL_STATUSES.has(tool.status))) {
1175	    return "in_progress";
1176	  }
1177	  if (tools.every((tool) => ["completed", "done"].includes(tool.status))) {
1178	    return "completed";
1179	  }
1180	  return tools[tools.length - 1]?.status ?? "pending";
1181	}
1182	
1183	function createToolGroup(tools: AgentToolActivity[]): AgentActivityItem {
1184	  const firstTool = tools[0];
1185	
1186	  return {
1187	    id: `tool-group:${firstTool.id}`,
1188	    kind: "tool_group",
1189	    status: deriveToolGroupStatus(tools),
1190	    timestamp: firstTool.timestamp,
1191	    counts: countToolGroup(tools),
1192	    tools,
1193	  };
1194	}
1195	
1196	function groupConsecutiveTools(items: AgentActivityItem[]): AgentActivityItem[] {
1197	  const grouped: AgentActivityItem[] = [];
1198	  let pendingTools: AgentToolActivity[] = [];
1199	
1200	  const flushTools = () => {
1201	    if (pendingTools.length === 0) {
1202	      return;
1203	    }
1204	    if (pendingTools.length === 1) {
1205	      grouped.push(pendingTools[0]);
1206	    } else {
1207	      grouped.push(createToolGroup(pendingTools));
1208	    }
1209	    pendingTools = [];
1210	  };
1211	
1212	  for (const item of items) {
1213	    if (item.kind === "tool") {
1214	      pendingTools.push(item);
1215	      continue;
1216	    }
1217	
1218	    flushTools();
1219	    grouped.push(item);
1220	  }
1221	
1222	  flushTools();
1223	  return grouped;
1224	}
1225	
1226	function timestampDeltaMs(startTimestamp: string, endTimestamp: string): number | undefined {
1227	  const start = Date.parse(startTimestamp);
1228	  const end = Date.parse(endTimestamp);
1229	  if (!Number.isFinite(start) || !Number.isFinite(end)) {
1230	    return undefined;
1231	  }
1232	
1233	  return Math.max(0, end - start);
1234	}
1235	
1236	export function formatActivityStatus(status: string): string {
1237	  return status
1238	    .split(/[_\s]+/)
1239	    .filter(Boolean)
1240	    .map((segment) => segment.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + segment.slice(1))
1241	    .join(" ");
1242	}
1243	
1244	/**
1245	 * Index of the last item that is part of the conversation itself. Protocol
1246	 * chips (usage counters, session info, config options) are metadata that the
1247	 * agent interleaves with its own output, so they do not break the adjacency of
1248	 * two consecutive assistant messages.
1249	 */
1250	function lastConversationItemIndex(items: AgentActivityItem[]): number | null {
1251	  for (let index = items.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
1252	    if (items[index]?.kind !== "protocol") {
1253	      return index;
1254	    }
1255	  }
1256	  return null;
1257	}
1258	
1259	export function buildAgentOutputActivity(snapshot: AgentOutputSnapshot): AgentActivityItem[] {
1260	  const items: AgentActivityItem[] = [];
1261	  const toolIndexById = new Map<string, number>();
1262	  const messageIndexById = new Map<string, number>();
1263	  const permissionDetailByRequestId = new Map<number, string>();
1264	  const permissionIndexByRequestId = new Map<number, number>();
1265	  const protocolIndexByKey = new Map<string, number>();
1266	  const thoughtLocationById = new Map<string, { activity: MutableThinkingActivity; index: number }>();
1267	  const outputEntries = coalesceFragmentedMessageEntries(
1268	    Array.isArray(snapshot.outputEntries) ? snapshot.outputEntries : [],
1269	  );
1270	  const seenOutputEntryFingerprints = new Set<string>();
1271	  let openThinking: MutableThinkingActivity | null = null;
1272	
1273	  const finishOpenThinking = (endTimestamp: string) => {
1274	    if (!openThinking) {
1275	      return;
1276	    }
1277	
1278	    openThinking.inProgress = false;
1279	    openThinking.durationMs = timestampDeltaMs(openThinking.timestamp, endTimestamp);
1280	    openThinking = null;
1281	  };
1282	
1283	  for (const entry of outputEntries) {
1284	    if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") {
1285	      continue;
1286	    }
1287	    const fingerprint = outputEntryFingerprint(entry);
1288	    if (seenOutputEntryFingerprints.has(fingerprint)) {
1289	      continue;
1290	    }
1291	    seenOutputEntryFingerprints.add(fingerprint);
1292	
1293	    if (entry.type === "thought") {
1294	      const text = normalizeMultilineText(entry.text || "").trim();
1295	      if (!text) {
1296	        continue;
1297	      }
1298	
1299	      const existingThought = thoughtLocationById.get(entry.id);
1300	      if (existingThought) {
1301	        existingThought.activity.thoughts[existingThought.index] = text;
1302	        continue;
1303	      }
1304	
1305	      if (openThinking) {
1306	        openThinking.thoughts.push(text);
1307	        thoughtLocationById.set(entry.id, {
1308	          activity: openThinking,
1309	          index: openThinking.thoughts.length - 1,
1310	        });
1311	      } else {
1312	        openThinking = {
1313	          id: entry.id,
1314	          kind: "thinking",
1315	          thoughts: [text],
1316	          timestamp: entry.timestamp,
1317	          inProgress: true,
1318	        };
1319	        items.push(openThinking);
1320	        thoughtLocationById.set(entry.id, { activity: openThinking, index: 0 });
1321	      }
1322	      continue;
1323	    }
1324	
1325	    if (entry.type === "message") {
1326	      if (isOmittedOutputEntriesMarker(entry)) {
1327	        continue;
1328	      }
1329	      const text = normalizeMultilineText(entry.text || "").trim();
1330	      if (!text) {
1331	        continue;
1332	      }
1333	      finishOpenThinking(entry.timestamp);
1334	      const existingIndex = messageIndexById.get(entry.id);
1335	      if (existingIndex != null) {
1336	        const existing = items[existingIndex];
1337	        if (existing?.kind === "message") {
1338	          const messageActivity = existing as MutableMessageActivity;
1339	          messageActivity.text = text;
1340	          messageActivity.live = undefined;
1341	          continue;
1342	        }
1343	      }
1344	      // A retried turn re-emits the identical assistant message once per
1345	      // attempt — an API error such as "Usage credits required for 1M context"
1346	      // arrives a dozen times in a row. Each retry is a distinct entry id, so
1347	      // the id map above cannot collapse them; fold them into the bubble that
1348	      // is already on screen instead of repeating it.
1349	      const previousIndex = lastConversationItemIndex(items);
1350	      const previousItem = previousIndex == null ? null : items[previousIndex];
1351	      if (previousItem?.kind === "message" && previousItem.text === text) {
1352	        messageIndexById.set(entry.id, previousIndex as number);
1353	        continue;
1354	      }
1355	      messageIndexById.set(entry.id, items.length);
1356	      items.push({
1357	        id: entry.id,
1358	        kind: "message",
1359	        text,
1360	        timestamp: entry.timestamp,
1361	      });
1362	      continue;
1363	    }
1364	
1365	    if (entry.type === "permission") {
1366	      const text = normalizeMultilineText(entry.text || "").trim();
1367	      if (!text) {
1368	        continue;
1369	      }
1370	      finishOpenThinking(entry.timestamp);
1371	      const status = normalizeStatus(entry.status);
1372	      const requestId = permissionRequestId(entry);
1373	      const directDetail = permissionToolCallDetail(entry);
1374	      if (requestId !== null && directDetail) {
1375	        permissionDetailByRequestId.set(requestId, directDetail);
1376	      }
1377	      const detail = directDetail ?? (requestId !== null ? permissionDetailByRequestId.get(requestId) ?? null : null);
1378	      const existingIndex = requestId !== null ? permissionIndexByRequestId.get(requestId) : undefined;
1379	      if (existingIndex != null) {
1380	        const existing = items[existingIndex];
1381	        if (existing?.kind === "permission") {
1382	          const permissionActivity = existing as MutablePermissionActivity;
1383	          permissionActivity.title = permissionTitleKeyForStatus(status);
1384	          permissionActivity.text = permissionDisplayText(entry, detail);
1385	          permissionActivity.detail = detail;
1386	          permissionActivity.timestamp = entry.timestamp;
1387	          permissionActivity.status = status;
1388	          continue;
1389	        }
1390	      }
1391	      if (requestId !== null) {
1392	        permissionIndexByRequestId.set(requestId, items.length);
1393	      }
1394	      items.push({
1395	        id: entry.id,
1396	        kind: "permission",
1397	        title: permissionTitleKeyForStatus(status),
1398	        text: permissionDisplayText(entry, detail),
1399	        detail,
1400	        timestamp: entry.timestamp,
1401	        status,
1402	      });
1403	      continue;
1404	    }
1405	
1406	    const protocolTitleByType: Partial<Record<WorkerEntryType, string>> = {
1407	      elicitation: "terminal.protocol.elicitation",
1408	      plan: "terminal.protocol.plan",
1409	      plan_update: "terminal.protocol.planUpdated",
1410	      plan_removed: "terminal.protocol.planRemoved",
1411	      current_mode: "terminal.protocol.mode",
1412	      config_option: "terminal.protocol.configuration",
1413	      session_info: "terminal.protocol.sessionInfo",
1414	      usage: "terminal.protocol.usage",
1415	      agent_content: "terminal.protocol.content",
1416	      user_content: "terminal.protocol.content",
1417	    };
1418	    const protocolTitle = protocolTitleByType[entry.type];
1419	    if (protocolTitle) {
1420	      finishOpenThinking(entry.timestamp);
1421	      const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
1422	      const plan = asRecord(raw?.plan);
1423	      const protocolContent = asRecord(raw?.content);
1424	      const protocolKey = entry.type === "elicitation" && (typeof raw?.requestId === "number" || typeof raw?.requestId === "string")

1426	        : (entry.type === "plan_update" || entry.type === "plan_removed") && typeof (plan?.id ?? raw?.id) === "string"
1427	          ? `plan:${String(plan?.id ?? raw?.id)}`
1428	          : entry.type === "plan"
1429	            ? "plan:legacy"
1430	            : entry.type === "agent_content" && protocolContent?.type === "terminal" && typeof protocolContent.terminalId === "string"
1431	              ? `terminal:${protocolContent.terminalId}`
1432	            : ["current_mode", "config_option", "session_info", "usage"].includes(entry.type)
1433	              ? `latest:${entry.type}`
1434	              : `entry:${entry.id}`;
1435	      const protocolActivity: Extract<AgentActivityItem, { kind: "protocol" }> = {
1436	        id: entry.id,
1437	        kind: "protocol",
1438	        title: protocolTitle,
1439	        text: normalizeMultilineText(entry.text || "").trim(),
1440	        timestamp: entry.timestamp,
1441	        protocolType: (entry.type === "agent_content" || entry.type === "user_content" ? "content" : entry.type) as Extract<AgentActivityItem, { kind: "protocol" }>["protocolType"],
1442	        raw: entry.raw,
1443	      };
1444	      const existingIndex = protocolIndexByKey.get(protocolKey);
1445	      if (existingIndex !== undefined && items[existingIndex]?.kind === "protocol") {
1446	        items[existingIndex] = protocolActivity;
1447	      } else {
1448	        protocolIndexByKey.set(protocolKey, items.length);
1449	        items.push(protocolActivity);
1450	      }
1451	      continue;
1452	    }
1453	
1454	    if (entry.type === "tool_call") {
1455	      finishOpenThinking(entry.timestamp);
1456	      const key = entry.toolCallId || entry.id;
1457	      const existingIndex = toolIndexById.get(key);
1458	      if (existingIndex != null) {
1459	        const existing = items[existingIndex];
1460	        if (existing?.kind === "tool") {

1463	        }
1464	      }
1465	
1466	      const toolActivity = createToolActivity(entry);
1467	      toolIndexById.set(key, items.length);
1468	      items.push(toolActivity);
1469	      continue;
1470	    }
1471	
1472	    if (entry.type === "tool_call_update") {
1473	      finishOpenThinking(entry.timestamp);
1474	      const key = entry.toolCallId || entry.id;
1475	      const existingIndex = toolIndexById.get(key);
1476	      if (existingIndex != null) {
1477	        const existing = items[existingIndex];
1478	        if (existing?.kind === "tool") {
1479	          applyToolUpdate(existing, entry);
1480	          continue;
1481	        }
1482	      }
1483	
1484	      const toolActivity = createToolActivity(entry);
1485	      toolIndexById.set(key, items.length);

1487	    }

1489	
1490	  if (openThinking && !shouldKeepTrailingThinkingOpen(snapshot)) {

62	function slugBranchName(value: string) {
63	  return value
64	    .trim()
65	    .toLowerCase()
66	    .replace(/[^a-z0-9/_-]+/g, "-")
67	    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
68	    || "workspace";
69	}
70	
71	function suggestCheckoutPath(repoRoot: string | undefined, branchName: string) {
72	  if (!repoRoot) {
73	    return "";
74	  }
75	  const pieces = repoRoot.split("/");
76	  const repoName = pieces.pop() || "repo";
77	  const parent = pieces.join("/") || "/";
78	  return `${parent}/${repoName}-${slugBranchName(branchName).replace(/\//g, "-")}`;
79	}

81	function isPreflightConfirmationMessage(message: MessageRecord) {
82	  return message.role === "supervisor" && message.kind === "implementation_confirmation";
83	}

85	const PREFLIGHT_CONFIRMATION_APPROVED_RESPONSE = "Yes, implement it";

87	interface ConversationExecutionStatusProps {
88	  liveExecutionStatus: { label: string; detail: string; tone: "error" | "warning" | "muted" | "active" };
89	  liveThoughts: Array<{ agentName: string; text: string; snippet: string; isLive: boolean }>;
90	}

92	function formatActivityWorkerTitle(worker: SupervisorActivityWorker) {
93	  if (worker.title) {
94	    return worker.title;
95	  }
96	  if (worker.workerNumber !== null) {
97	    return t("supervisor.activity.worker.fallbackName", { number: worker.workerNumber });
98	  }
99	  return worker.workerId;
100	}
101	
102	function renderActivityText(worker: SupervisorActivityWorker) {
103	  if (worker.activityKey) {
104	    return t(worker.activityKey, worker.activityParams);
105	  }
106	  return worker.activityText;
107	}
108	
109	const WORKER_MENTION_PATTERN = /\b[Ww]orker\s+(\d+)\b/g;
110	
111	function renderTextWithWorkerLinks(
112	  text: string,
113	  workers: ConversationWorkerRecord[],
114	  onOpenWorker?: (workerId: string) => void,
115	): React.ReactNode {
116	  if (!text || !onOpenWorker || workers.length === 0) {
117	    return text;
118	  }
119	  const workerByNumber = new Map<number, ConversationWorkerRecord>();
120	  for (const worker of workers) {
121	    if (typeof worker.workerNumber === "number") {
122	      workerByNumber.set(worker.workerNumber, worker);
123	    }
124	  }
125	  if (workerByNumber.size === 0) {
126	    return text;
127	  }
128	  const nodes: React.ReactNode[] = [];
129	  let cursor = 0;
130	  let keyCounter = 0;
131	  for (const match of text.matchAll(WORKER_MENTION_PATTERN)) {
132	    const matchStart = match.index ?? 0;
133	    const number = Number.parseInt(match[1]!, 10);
134	    const target = Number.isFinite(number) ? workerByNumber.get(number) : null;
135	    if (!target) {
136	      continue;
137	    }
138	    if (matchStart > cursor) {
139	      nodes.push(text.slice(cursor, matchStart));
140	    }
141	    const workerId = target.id;
142	    nodes.push(
143	      <button
144	        key={`worker-link-${keyCounter++}`}
145	        type="button"
146	        onClick={(event) => {
147	          event.stopPropagation();
148	          onOpenWorker(workerId);
149	        }}
150	        className="omni-worker-mention-link inline cursor-pointer underline decoration-dotted underline-offset-2 hover:text-foreground"
151	      >
152	        {match[0]}
153	      </button>,
154	    );
155	    cursor = matchStart + match[0].length;
156	  }
157	  if (cursor === 0) {
158	    return text;
159	  }
160	  if (cursor < text.length) {
161	    nodes.push(text.slice(cursor));
162	  }
163	  return nodes;
164	}

166	function ConversationExecutionPanel({

399	function inferWorkerIdFromMessage(message: MessageRecord) {
400	  if (message.workerId?.trim()) {
401	    return message.workerId.trim();
402	  }
403	
404	  const promptedMatch = message.content.match(/^Prompted\s+([^\s:]+):/);
405	  return promptedMatch?.[1] ?? null;
406	}
407	
408	function renderSupervisorActivityText(text: string) {
409	  const match = text.match(/^(Starting (?:worker \d+|planning agent)|Steering worker \d+)([\s\S]*)$/);
410	  if (!match) {
411	    return text;
412	  }
413	
414	  return (
415	    <>
416	      <strong className="font-semibold text-foreground">{match[1]}</strong>
417	      {match[2]}
418	    </>
419	  );
420	}
421	
422	function renderSupervisorActivityIcon(item: Extract<ConversationTimelineItem, { type: "activity" }>) {
423	  const eventType = item.event?.eventType;
424	  const text = item.text.trim();
425	
426	  if (eventType === "worker_spawned" || item.id.startsWith("worker-start:") || text.startsWith("Starting worker ") || text.startsWith("Starting planning agent")) {
427	    return <CirclePlay className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" strokeWidth={1.8} />;
428	  }
429	
430	  if (
431	    eventType === "worker_cancelled"
432	    || eventType === "worker_stopped"
433	    || eventType === "worker_stop_requested"
434	    || text.startsWith("Cancelled worker ")
435	    || text.startsWith("Stopped worker ")
436	    || text.startsWith("Stopping worker ")
437	  ) {
438	    return <CircleStop className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" strokeWidth={1.8} />;
439	  }
440	
441	  if (
442	    eventType === "worker_prompt_deferred"
443	    || text.startsWith("Steering worker ")
444	    || text.startsWith("Waiting to steer worker ")
445	  ) {
446	    return <Route className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" strokeWidth={1.8} />;
447	  }
448	
449	  if (
450	    eventType === "worker_failover_started"
451	    || eventType === "worker_failover_completed"
452	    || eventType === "worker_handoff_emitted"
453	  ) {
454	    return <ArrowLeftRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" strokeWidth={1.8} />;
455	  }
456	
457	  return null;
458	}
459	
460	function SupervisorActivityMessage({ item }: { item: Extract<ConversationTimelineItem, { type: "activity" }> }) {
461	  const icon = renderSupervisorActivityIcon(item);
462	
463	  return (
464	    <div className="group ml-6 flex w-[calc(100%-1.5rem)] px-1 py-0.5 text-sm" aria-label="Supervisor action">
465	      <div className="omni-activity-icon mt-[0.18em] flex h-3.5 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center pr-1">
466	        {icon}
467	      </div>
468	      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-start justify-between gap-3">
469	        <p className="omni-activity-text min-w-0 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-[13px] leading-[1.45]">
470	          {renderSupervisorActivityText(item.text)}
471	        </p>
472	        <span className="shrink-0 pt-[0.18em] text-[10px] text-muted-foreground/50">
473	          {formatExecutionTimestamp(item.createdAt)}
474	        </span>
475	      </div>
476	    </div>
477	  );
478	}

480	function WorkerOutputMessage({
481	  message,
482	  agent,
483	  projectRoot,
484	  onOpenProjectFile,
485	  onCopy,
486	}: {
487	  message: MessageRecord;
488	  agent: AgentSnapshot | null;
489	  projectRoot?: string | null;
490	  onOpenProjectFile?: (file: ProjectFileReference) => void;
491	  onCopy?: (content: string, messageId: string) => void | Promise<void>;
492	}) {
493	  const { fullOutputOpenByMessageId } = useManagerSnapshot(conversationMainManager);
494	  const fullOutputOpen = Boolean(fullOutputOpenByMessageId[message.id]);
495	  const inferredWorkerId = inferWorkerIdFromMessage(message);
496	  const summaryText = extractLatestPlainTextTurn({
497	    outputEntries: agent?.outputEntries,
498	    currentText: agent?.currentText,
499	    lastText: agent?.lastText || message.content,
500	  }) || message.content.trim();
501	  const fullOutputAgent = agent ?? {
502	    name: inferredWorkerId ?? "worker",
503	    state: "done",
504	    currentText: "",
505	    lastText: message.content,
506	  };
507	
508	  return (
509	    <Collapsible open={fullOutputOpen} onOpenChange={(open) => conversationMainManager.setFullOutputOpen(message.id, open)}>
510	      <div className="omni-worker-output group/worker-output-actions overflow-hidden rounded-lg">
511	        <div className="space-y-3 p-4">
512	          <MarkdownContent
513	            content={summaryText}
514	            className="text-foreground"
515	            projectRoot={projectRoot}
516	            onOpenProjectFile={onOpenProjectFile}
517	          />
518	          {summaryText.trim() && onCopy ? (
519	            <WorkerOutputCopyAction
520	              messageId={message.id}
521	              content={summaryText}
522	              onCopy={onCopy}
523	            />
524	          ) : null}
525	          <CollapsibleTrigger
526	            className="omni-worker-output-toggle inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring"
527	            aria-label={fullOutputOpen ? "Hide full worker output" : "Show full worker output"}
528	          >
529	            <ChevronDown className={cn("h-3.5 w-3.5 transition-transform", fullOutputOpen && "rotate-180")} />
530	            {fullOutputOpen ? "Hide full output" : "Show full output"}
531	          </CollapsibleTrigger>
532	        </div>
533	        <CollapsibleContent>
534	          <div className="border-t border-border/35 bg-muted/20 p-2 dark:bg-background/70">
535	            <Terminal
536	              agent={fullOutputAgent}
537	              className="h-72"
538	              projectRoot={projectRoot}
539	              onOpenProjectFile={onOpenProjectFile}
540	              scrollAnchorKey={message.id}
541	            />
542	          </div>
543	        </CollapsibleContent>
544	      </div>
545	    </Collapsible>
546	  );
547	}

549	function WorkerOutputCopyAction({
550	  messageId,
551	  content,
552	  onCopy,
553	}: {
554	  messageId: string;
555	  content: string;
556	  onCopy: (content: string, messageId: string) => void | Promise<void>;
557	}) {
558	  useI18nSnapshot();
559	  const { copiedMessageId } = useManagerSnapshot(conversationCopyNoticeManager);
560	
561	  return (
562	    <div className="flex w-full justify-end">
563	      <span className="relative inline-flex flex-col items-center opacity-100 transition-opacity md:opacity-0 md:focus-within:opacity-100 md:group-hover/worker-output-actions:opacity-100">
564	        <button
565	          type="button"
566	          aria-label={t("conversation.message.copyAria")}
567	          title={t("conversation.message.copyAria")}
568	          onClick={() => void onCopy(content, messageId)}
569	          className="hidden h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring md:inline-flex"
570	        >
571	          <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />
572	        </button>
573	        <DropdownMenu>
574	          <DropdownMenuTrigger
575	            aria-label={t("conversation.message.actionsAria")}
576	            title={t("conversation.message.actionsAria")}
577	            className="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring md:hidden"
578	          >
579	            <MoreHorizontal className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
580	          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
581	          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" side="top">
582	            <DropdownMenuItem className="min-h-10 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => void onCopy(content, messageId)}>
583	              <Copy className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
584	              {t("conversation.message.copyAria")}
585	            </DropdownMenuItem>
586	          </DropdownMenuContent>
587	        </DropdownMenu>
588	        {copiedMessageId === messageId ? (
589	          <span
590	            role="status"
591	            aria-live="polite"
592	            className="pointer-events-none absolute top-full z-20 mt-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border border-border/70 bg-popover px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none text-popover-foreground shadow-sm"
593	          >
594	            {t("conversation.message.copiedNotice")}

602	function PlannerOutputMessage({
603	  message,
604	  agent,
605	  projectRoot,
606	  onOpenProjectFile,
607	}: {
608	  message: MessageRecord;
609	  agent: AgentSnapshot | null;

627	      thoughtsDefaultOpen
628	      emptyState={null}
629	      projectRoot={projectRoot}
630	      onOpenProjectFile={onOpenProjectFile}

632	    />
633	  );

718	function FailoverChip({ events }: { events: ExecutionEventRecord[] }) {
719	  for (let i = events.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
720	    const event = events[i]!;
721	    if (event.eventType !== "worker_failover_completed") continue;
722	    let outgoingType: string | null = null;
723	    let newType: string | null = null;
724	    try {
725	      const details = event.details ? JSON.parse(event.details) : null;
726	      if (details && typeof details === "object") {
727	        const record = details as Record<string, unknown>;
728	        outgoingType = typeof record.outgoingType === "string" ? record.outgoingType : null;
729	        newType = typeof record.newType === "string" ? record.newType : null;
730	      }
731	    } catch {
732	      outgoingType = null;
733	      newType = null;
734	    }
735	    if (!outgoingType || !newType) return null;
736	    const outgoingLabel = WORKER_TYPE_LABELS[outgoingType as SupportedWorkerType] ?? outgoingType;
737	    const newLabel = WORKER_TYPE_LABELS[newType as SupportedWorkerType] ?? newType;
738	    return (
739	      <div className="flex">
740	        <span
741	          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-amber-200/40 bg-amber-100/30 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-amber-900 dark:border-amber-900/40 dark:bg-amber-950/30 dark:text-amber-200"
742	          aria-label={t("conversation.chip.switchedWorkersAria")}
743	        >
744	          <ArrowLeftRight className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" strokeWidth={1.8} />
745	          {t("conversation.chip.switchedWorkers", { outgoing: outgoingLabel, incoming: newLabel })}
746	        </span>
747	      </div>
748	    );
749	  }
750	  return null;
751	}
752	
753	function LatestRecoveryAction({
754	  selectedRun,
755	  canRetryConversation,
756	  isSelectedConversationLoaded,
757	  recoverRun,
758	  showRecoverableRunningState,
759	  hasStuckWorker,
760	  latestUserCheckpoint,
761	  handleRetryMessage,
762	}: {
763	  selectedRun: RunRecord | null;
764	  canRetryConversation: boolean;
765	  isSelectedConversationLoaded: boolean;
766	  recoverRun: { isPending: boolean };
767	  showRecoverableRunningState: boolean;
768	  hasStuckWorker: boolean;
769	  latestUserCheckpoint: MessageRecord | null;
770	  handleRetryMessage: (messageId: string) => void;
771	}) {
772	  if (!shouldShowLatestRecoveryAction({
773	    selectedRun,
774	    canRetryConversation,
775	    isSelectedConversationLoaded,
776	    latestUserCheckpoint,
777	    showRecoverableRunningState,
778	    hasStuckWorker,
779	  })) {
780	    return null;
781	  }
782	  const checkpoint = latestUserCheckpoint;
783	  if (!checkpoint) {
784	    return null;
785	  }
786	
787	  return (
788	    <div className="flex justify-start">
789	      <Button
790	        variant="outline"
791	        size="sm"
792	        onClick={() => handleRetryMessage(checkpoint.id)}
793	        disabled={recoverRun.isPending}
794	      >
795	        <RotateCcw className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {selectedRun?.status === "failed" ? "Resume worker" : "Unstick latest"}
796	      </Button>
797	    </div>
798	  );
799	}

801	/**

804	 * spread from `sharedSidebarProps`, whose callbacks are now stable, so the
805	 * shallow comparison is meaningful.

807	const ConversationMain = memo(function ConversationMain({

809	  selectedRunId,
810	  selectedRun,

813	  isPlanningConversation,

816	  conversationFailure,
817	  onCredentialReauthenticate,

820	  sendingUserMessageIds,

824	  primaryConversationWorkerId,
825	  planSurfaceOwner,
826	  initialWorkerEntries = {},

828	  isHydratingConversations,
829	  isSelectedConversationPreviewAvailable,
830	  isSelectedConversationLoaded,
831	  promotePlanningConversation,
832	  onStartReview,
833	  reviewRuns = [],
834	  reviewRounds = [],
835	  reviewFindings = [],
836	  conversationTimelineItems,
837	  recoverRun,
838	  recoveryState,
839	  recoveryIncidents,
840	  resumeRunRecovery,
841	  stopRunRecovery,
842	  showRecoverableRunningState,
843	  hasStuckWorker,
844	  latestUserCheckpoint,
845	  handleRetryMessage,
846	  handleResumeRunRecovery,
847	  handleStopRecoveryWait,
848	  handleStartEditingMessage,
849	  handleForkMessage,
850	  handleForkMessageIntoWorktree,
851	  handleForkMessageToDifferentCli,
852	  handleQuotaHandoffToDifferentCli,
853	  handleConfirmForkMessageIntoWorktree,
854	  editingMessageId,
855	  editingMessageValue,
856	  setEditingMessageValue,
857	  handleCancelEditingMessage,
858	  handleSaveEditedMessage,
859	  handlePreflightConfirmationAnswer,
860	  isPreflightConfirmationAnswering,
861	  conversationAgents,
862	  showDirectControlWorkingIndicator,
863	  directControlPendingAssistantStatus,
864	  showConversationExecution,
865	  liveExecutionStatus,
866	  liveThoughts,
867	  executionEvents,
868	  activeWorkers,
869	  emptyComposer,
870	  projectRoot,
871	  onOpenProjectFile,
872	  onOpenWorkerActivity,
873	  onOpenMobileConversationList,
874	  onRespondElicitation,
875	  onRespondPermission,
876	  respondingElicitationRequestId = null,
877	  respondingPermissionRequestId = null,
878	}: ConversationMainProps) {
879	  const i18nSnapshot = useI18nSnapshot();
880	  const isCompactLayout = useIsCompactLayout();
881	  const { hasOutputBelow } = useManagerSnapshot(conversationMainManager);
882	  const { handledMessageIds: handledPreflightConfirmationMessageIds } = useManagerSnapshot(preflightConfirmationActionsManager);
883	  const selectedRunIsTerminal = isTerminalRunStatus(selectedRun?.status);
884	  // Subscribe to the unified worker conversation stream for the
885	  // direct-control worker. When the flag is enabled this drives the
886	  // Terminal directly; otherwise the legacy `agent` + `userMessages`
887	  // path renders. `null` workerId returns an empty state and skips
888	  // the fetch.
889	  const directWorkerStream = useWorkerStream(
890	    unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled ? primaryConversationWorkerId : null,
891	    primaryConversationWorkerId ? initialWorkerEntries[primaryConversationWorkerId] ?? [] : [],
892	    {
893	      refreshIntervalMs: resolveDirectWorkerStreamRefreshInterval({
894	        unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled,
895	        primaryConversationWorkerId,
896	        activeRefreshIntervalMs: DIRECT_WORKER_STREAM_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS,
897	        validationIntervalMs: DIRECT_WORKER_STREAM_VALIDATION_INTERVAL_MS,
898	        showDirectControlWorkingIndicator,
899	        selectedRunIsTerminal,
900	      }),
901	    },
902	  );
903	  // The single-worker stream above is only the *active* worker's
904	  // transcript. When a run has cycled through multiple workers
905	  // (cancel → respawn) we also fetch the merged conversation
906	  // transcript so prior workers' content stays visible. The merged
907	  // stream is preferred when available; we fall back to the active
908	  // worker stream during the brief moment before the transcript hook
909	  // produces its first response.
910	  const conversationTranscript = useConversationTranscript(
911	    unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled ? selectedRunId : null,
912	    {
913	      enabled: unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled && Boolean(selectedRunId),
914	      refreshIntervalMs: resolveDirectWorkerStreamRefreshInterval({
915	        unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled,
916	        primaryConversationWorkerId,
917	        activeRefreshIntervalMs: DIRECT_WORKER_STREAM_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS,
918	        validationIntervalMs: DIRECT_WORKER_STREAM_VALIDATION_INTERVAL_MS,
919	        showDirectControlWorkingIndicator,
920	        selectedRunIsTerminal,
921	      }),
922	    },
923	  );
924	  // The run's worker list, not the entries loaded so far, decides how the
925	  // conversation is ordered — otherwise the rule flips from seq to timestamp
926	  // the moment the first transcript page arrives and the list re-sorts under
927	  // the reader. Undefined until the transcript answers, which leaves the
928	  // Terminal on its own inference for that first paint.
929	  const conversationRanOnMultipleWorkers = conversationTranscript.workerIds.length > 0
930	    ? conversationTranscript.workerIds.length > 1
931	    : undefined;
932	  // The server re-parses `supersededSeqRanges` into a fresh array on every
933	  // snapshot, so depending on it by identity would invalidate the merge below
934	  // on every frame even though its contents almost never change. The list is
935	  // tiny (usually empty), so round-tripping it is far cheaper than the rebuild
936	  // it prevents.
937	  const supersededSeqRangesKey = JSON.stringify(primaryConversationAgent?.supersededSeqRanges ?? []);
938	  const supersededSeqRanges = useMemo(
939	    () => JSON.parse(supersededSeqRangesKey) as SupersededSeqRange[],
940	    [supersededSeqRangesKey],
941	  );
942	  // One pass instead of a nested scan. This used to be a `.some()` over the
943	  // whole timeline *inside* the timeline map — O(n^2) with two `Date`
944	  // allocations per candidate, so a 500-item supervisor conversation cost
945	  // ~250k iterations and ~500k Date parses on every render.
946	  const latestClarificationAnswerMsByRunId = useMemo(() => {
947	    const latest = new Map<string, number>();
948	    for (const candidate of conversationTimelineItems) {
949	      if (candidate.type !== "message" || candidate.message.kind !== "clarification_answer") {
950	        continue;
951	      }
952	      const candidateMs = new Date(candidate.message.createdAt).getTime();
953	      const existing = latest.get(candidate.message.runId);
954	      if (existing === undefined || candidateMs > existing) {
955	        latest.set(candidate.message.runId, candidateMs);
956	      }
957	    }
958	    return latest;
959	  }, [conversationTimelineItems]);
960	  // Memoized because this array is the `entries` prop Terminal keys its
961	  // activity derivation on. Rebuilding it every render handed Terminal a new
962	  // reference each time, so the activity rebuild — measured at ~3ms for a
963	  // 2k-entry conversation, and several times that on a phone — re-ran on every
964	  // SSE frame while an agent was streaming, for an identical result.
965	  const conversationEntries = useMemo(
966	    () => selectDirectConversationEntries({
967	      transcriptEntries: conversationTranscript.entries,
968	      directWorkerEntries: directWorkerStream.entries,
969	      supersededSeqRanges,
970	      workerOrder: conversationTranscript.workerIds,
971	    }),
972	    [
973	      conversationTranscript.entries,
974	      conversationTranscript.workerIds,
975	      directWorkerStream.entries,
976	      supersededSeqRanges,
977	    ],
978	  );
979	  // Only the runtime owns a promise that can consume an answer. Stream rows
980	  // are durable history and may outlive that promise across a runner crash;
981	  // rendering them as forms makes a dead question look actionable.
982	  const pendingElicitations = primaryConversationAgent?.pendingElicitations ?? [];
983	  // Gated here rather than upstream because this is the only place that knows
984	  // what the prompt cards below the Terminal are actually rendering: the
985	  // elicitation card also draws on `directWorkerStream.entries`, which the
986	  // upstream resolver never sees. Note the refresh-interval calls above
987	  // deliberately keep using the ungated prop — polling cadence should follow
988	  // "is the worker busy", not "is a card on screen".

990	    pendingAssistantVisible: showDirectControlWorkingIndicator,
991	    primaryConversationWorkerId,
992	    renderedElicitationCount: pendingElicitations.length,
993	    renderedPermissionCount: primaryConversationAgent?.pendingPermissions?.length ?? 0,
994	  });
995	  const isUsingConversationTranscriptEntries = conversationTranscript.entries.length > 0;
996	  // Latched (`hasEverLoaded` / `hasLoadedOnce`), not momentary (`isLoaded`):
997	  // isLoaded flips false on every appended entry and every 2s transcript
998	  // poll, and each flip removed fallback-rendered user bubbles from the
999	  // transcript — the just-sent message visibly blinked in and out until its
1000	  // stream entry arrived. The gate only exists to keep stale historical
1001	  // rows from rendering before the stream's *first* load.

1003	    !unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled
1004	      || directWorkerStream.hasEverLoaded
1005	      || conversationTranscript.hasLoadedOnce,
1006	  );
1007	  const directConversationLoadState = deriveConversationLoadState({
1008	    snapshotLoaded: isSelectedConversationPreviewAvailable,
1009	    unifiedWorkerStreamEnabled,
1010	    primaryConversationWorkerId,
1011	    streamState: directWorkerStream.state,
1012	    selectedRunIsTerminal,
1013	  });
1014	  const isDirectWorkerStreamLoading = shouldShowDirectConversationLoading(directConversationLoadState)
1015	    || Boolean(
1016	      selectedRun
1017	      && !isTerminalRunStatus(selectedRun.status)
1018	      && directConversationMessages.length === 0
1019	      && (!directWorkerStream.entries || directWorkerStream.entries.length === 0)
1020	    );
1021	  const forkWorkspaceSelector = useMemo(() => {
1022	    return (state: ReturnType<typeof gitWorkspaceManager.getSnapshot>) => {
1023	      const dialog = state.activeDialog?.kind === "fork_message_worktree" || state.activeDialog?.kind === "fork_session_worktree"
1024	        ? state.activeDialog
1025	        : null;
1026	      const projectPath = dialog?.projectPath ?? null;
1027	      return {
1028	        dialog,
1029	        snapshot: projectPath ? state.snapshotsByProject[projectPath] : undefined,
1030	        loading: projectPath ? Boolean(state.loadingByProject[projectPath]) : false,
1031	        pendingOperation: state.pendingOperation,
1032	        dialogDraft: state.dialogDraft,
1033	        lastError: projectPath ? state.lastErrorByProject[projectPath] ?? null : null,
1034	      };
1035	    };
1036	  }, []);
1037	  const { dialog: forkWorkspaceDialog, snapshot: forkWorkspaceSnapshot, loading: forkWorkspaceLoading, pendingOperation: forkWorkspacePendingOperation, dialogDraft: forkWorkspaceDraft, lastError: forkWorkspaceError } = useManagerSelector(
1038	    gitWorkspaceManager,
1039	    forkWorkspaceSelector,
1040	    shallowEqualRecord,
1041	  );
1042	  const forkBranchName = forkWorkspaceDraft.branchName;
1043	  const forkCheckoutPath = forkWorkspaceDraft.checkoutPath;
1044	  useEffect(() => {
1045	    if (!forkWorkspaceDialog) {
1046	      return;
1047	    }
1048	    void gitWorkspaceManager.loadStatus(forkWorkspaceDialog.projectPath).catch(() => undefined);
1049	  }, [forkWorkspaceDialog]);
1050	  useEffect(() => {
1051	    if (!forkWorkspaceDialog || !forkWorkspaceSnapshot) {
1052	      return;
1053	    }
1054	    const nextBranch = `fork/${slugBranchName(selectedRun?.title || forkWorkspaceSnapshot.branchName || forkWorkspaceSnapshot.detachedLabel || "workspace")}`;
1055	    gitWorkspaceManager.setDialogDraft({
1056	      branchName: nextBranch,
1057	      checkoutPath: suggestCheckoutPath(forkWorkspaceSnapshot.repoRoot, nextBranch),
1058	    });
1059	  }, [forkWorkspaceDialog, forkWorkspaceSnapshot, selectedRun?.title]);
1060	  const handleCopyDirectMessage = useCallback(async (content: string, messageId: string) => {
1061	    try {
1062	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(content);
1063	      conversationCopyNoticeManager.showCopiedMessage(messageId);
1064	    } catch (error) {

1073	  // fallback filter, order-key assignment and the final sort — re-ran on every
1074	  // single render instead of only when the entries changed.
1075	  const getUserMessageActions = useCallback((message: Pick<MessageRecord, "id" | "content">): UserInputMessageAction[] => {
1076	    if (!canRecoverUserMessage) {

1078	    }

1080	    const copyAction: UserInputMessageAction = {
1081	      label: t("conversation.message.copyAria"),
1082	      title: t("conversation.message.copyAria"),
1083	      icon: <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
1084	      onClick: () => void handleCopyDirectMessage(message.content, message.id),
1085	      feedback: "copy-message",
1086	    };
1087	
1088	    const retryActions: UserInputMessageAction[] = [
1089	      {
1090	        label: t(isImplementationConversation ? "conversation.message.action.resumeFromHere" : "conversation.message.action.retryFromHere"),
1091	        icon: <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
1092	        disabled: recoverRun.isPending,
1093	        onClick: () => handleRetryMessage(message.id),
1094	      },
1095	    ];

1097	    if (!isDirectConversation) {
1098	      return retryActions;
1099	    }
1100	
1101	    return [
1102	      copyAction,
1103	      ...retryActions,
1104	      {
1105	        label: t("conversation.message.action.editInPlace"),
1106	        icon: <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
1107	        disabled: recoverRun.isPending,
1108	        onClick: () => handleStartEditingMessage(message),
1109	      },
1110	      {
1111	        label: t("conversation.message.action.forkFromHere"),
1112	        icon: <GitBranch className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
1113	        disabled: recoverRun.isPending,
1114	        menuItems: [
1115	          {
1116	            label: t("conversation.message.action.forkFromHere"),
1117	            icon: <GitBranch className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,

1119	            onClick: () => handleForkMessage(message),
1120	          },
1121	          {
1122	            label: t("git.workspace.action.forkMessageWorktree"),
1123	            icon: <FolderGit2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
1124	            disabled: recoverRun.isPending,
1125	            onClick: () => handleForkMessageIntoWorktree(message),
1126	          },
1127	          {
1128	            label: t("conversation.message.action.forkDifferentCli"),
1129	            icon: <ArrowLeftRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
1130	            disabled: recoverRun.isPending,
1131	            onClick: () => handleForkMessageToDifferentCli(message),
1132	          },

1134	      },
1135	    ];
1136	  }, [
1137	    canRecoverUserMessage,
1138	    isDirectConversation,
1139	    isImplementationConversation,
1140	    recoverRun.isPending,

1142	    handleRetryMessage,
1143	    handleStartEditingMessage,
1144	    handleForkMessage,

1149	    i18nSnapshot,
