```text
1484	                              onClick={() => preflightConfirmationActionsManager.rememberMessage(msg.id)}
1485	                            >
1486	                              <Pencil aria-hidden="true" />
1487	                              {t("conversation.preflightConfirmation.no")}
1488	                            </Button>
1489	                          </div>
1490	                        ) : null}
1491	                      </>
1492	                    ) : msg.content}
1493	                  </div>
1494	                )}
1495	              </div>
1496	              );
1497	            })
1498	          ) : (
1499	            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-24 text-sm text-muted-foreground sm:pt-32">
1500	              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted/50">
1501	                <Blocks className="h-6 w-6 opacity-50" />
1502	              </div>
1503	              <p>No output recorded yet for this run.</p>
1504	            </div>
1505	          )}
1506	
1507	          {isPlanningConversation ? (
1508	            <PlanningArtifactsPanel
1509	              specPath={selectedRun?.specPath}
1510	              planPath={selectedRun?.artifactPlanPath}
1511	              plannerArtifactsJson={selectedRun?.plannerArtifactsJson}
1512	              isPromoting={promotePlanningConversation.isPending}
1513	              projectRoot={projectRoot}
1514	              onOpenProjectFile={onOpenProjectFile}
1515	              runId={selectedRunId ?? undefined}
1516	              isReviewing={selectedRun?.status === "reviewing_plan" || selectedRun?.status === "revising_plan"}
1517	              latestReviewRun={reviewRuns[0]}
1518	              latestReviewRound={reviewRounds.filter(r => r.reviewRunId === reviewRuns[0]?.id).pop()}
1519	              reviewFindings={reviewFindings.filter(f => f.reviewRunId === reviewRuns[0]?.id)}
1520	              onStartReview={onStartReview}
1521	              onPromote={(planPath) => {
1522	                if (!selectedRunId) {
1523	                  return;
1524	                }
1525	
1526	                promotePlanningConversation.mutate({ runId: selectedRunId, planPath });
1527	              }}
1528	            />
1529	          ) : null}
1530	
1531	          {isImplementationConversation && showConversationExecution ? (
1532	            <ConversationExecutionPanel
1533	              runId={selectedRunId}
1534	              selectedRun={selectedRun}
1535	              liveExecutionStatus={liveExecutionStatus}
1536	              liveThoughts={liveThoughts}
1537	              executionEvents={executionEvents}
1538	              activeWorkers={activeWorkers}
1539	              conversationAgents={conversationAgents}
1540	              onOpenWorkerActivity={onOpenWorkerActivity}
1541	            />
1542	          ) : null}
1543	
1544	          {isImplementationConversation ? (
1545	            <RecoveryIncidentInspector
1546	              runId={selectedRunId}
1547	              incidents={recoveryIncidents}
1548	            />
1549	          ) : null}
1550	
1551	          {!conversationFailure && (showRecoverableRunningState || hasStuckWorker) ? (
1552	            <LatestRecoveryAction
1553	              selectedRun={selectedRun}
1554	              canRetryConversation={canRetryConversation}
1555	              isSelectedConversationLoaded={isSelectedConversationLoaded}
1556	              recoverRun={recoverRun}
1557	              showRecoverableRunningState={showRecoverableRunningState}
1558	              hasStuckWorker={hasStuckWorker}
1559	              latestUserCheckpoint={latestUserCheckpoint}
1560	              handleRetryMessage={handleRetryMessage}
1561	            />
1562	          ) : null}
1563	
1564	          {appErrors.length > 0 ? (
1565	            <div className="space-y-3">
1566	              {appErrors.map((error) => (
1567	                <ErrorNotice key={appErrorKey(error)} error={error} />
1568	              ))}
1569	            </div>
1570	          ) : null}
1571	
1572	          {conversationFailure ? (
1573	            <div className="space-y-3">
1574	              <ErrorNotice error={conversationFailure} />
1575	              {onCredentialReauthenticate ? (
1576	                <div className="flex justify-start">
1577	                  <Button type="button" size="sm" onClick={onCredentialReauthenticate}>
1578	                    {t("conversation.failure.reauth.action")}
1579	                  </Button>
1580	                </div>
1581	              ) : (
1582	                <LatestRecoveryAction
1583	                  selectedRun={selectedRun}
1584	                  canRetryConversation={canRetryConversation}
1585	                  isSelectedConversationLoaded={isSelectedConversationLoaded}
1586	                  recoverRun={recoverRun}
1587	                  showRecoverableRunningState={showRecoverableRunningState}
1588	                  hasStuckWorker={hasStuckWorker}
1589	                  latestUserCheckpoint={latestUserCheckpoint}
1590	                  handleRetryMessage={handleRetryMessage}
1591	                />
1592	              )}
1593	            </div>
1594	          ) : null}
1595	
1596	        </div>
1597	      )
1598	    ) : (
1599	      <div className="omni-conversation-text-scale flex h-full w-full flex-col items-center justify-center pb-32 text-center">
1600	        {appErrors.length > 0 ? (
1601	          <div className="mx-auto mb-6 w-full max-w-3xl space-y-3 px-6 text-left">
1602	            {appErrors.map((error) => (
1603	              <ErrorNotice key={appErrorKey(error)} error={error} />
1604	            ))}
1605	          </div>
1606	        ) : null}
1607	        {/* The composer's workspace pill floats at -top-5, so the heading needs
1608	            clearance below it on the breakpoints where that pill is visible. */}
1609	        <h1 className="mx-auto mb-8 w-full max-w-3xl px-6 text-[1.7rem] font-semibold leading-tight sm:mb-14">What shall we build in {welcomeRepoName}?</h1>
1610	        {emptyComposer}
1611	      </div>
1612	    )}
1613	  </ScrollArea>
1614	  <div
1615	    className={cn(
1616	      "pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-3 z-20 flex justify-center transition-all duration-150 ease-out motion-reduce:transition-none",
1617	      hasOutputBelow ? "translate-y-0 opacity-100" : "translate-y-1 opacity-0",
1618	    )}
1619	    aria-hidden={!hasOutputBelow}
1620	  >
1621	    <Button
1622	      type="button"
1623	      size="icon"
1624	      tabIndex={hasOutputBelow ? 0 : -1}
1625	      onClick={handleScrollToLatestOutput}
1626	      aria-label="Scroll to latest output"
1627	      title="Scroll to latest output"
1628	      className="pointer-events-auto h-8 w-8 rounded-full bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-lg shadow-primary/15 transition-all hover:bg-primary/90"
1629	    >
1630	      <ArrowDown className="h-[17px] w-[17px]" />
1631	    </Button>
1632	  </div>
1633	  <Dialog
1634	    open={Boolean(forkWorkspaceDialog)}
1635	    onOpenChange={(open) => {
1636	      if (!open) {
1637	        gitWorkspaceManager.setKey("activeDialog", null);
1638	      }
1639	    }}
1640	  >
1641	    <DialogContent>
1642	      <DialogHeader>
1643	        <DialogTitle>{t("git.workspace.dialog.fork.title")}</DialogTitle>
1644	        <DialogDescription>{t("git.workspace.dialog.fork.description")}</DialogDescription>
1645	      </DialogHeader>
1646	      <div className="grid gap-3">
1647	        <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm">
1648	          <span className="font-medium">{t("git.workspace.field.branchName")}</span>
1649	          <Input value={forkBranchName} onChange={(event) => {
1650	            const nextBranch = event.target.value;
1651	            gitWorkspaceManager.setDialogBranchName(nextBranch, suggestCheckoutPath(forkWorkspaceSnapshot?.repoRoot, nextBranch));
1652	          }} />
1653	        </label>
1654	        <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm">
1655	          <span className="font-medium">{t("git.workspace.field.checkoutPath")}</span>
1656	          <Input value={forkCheckoutPath} onChange={(event) => gitWorkspaceManager.setDialogCheckoutPath(event.target.value)} />
1657	        </label>
1658	        {forkWorkspaceError ? (
1659	          <div className="rounded-md border border-destructive/25 bg-destructive/10 px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-destructive">
1660	            <div className="font-medium">{forkWorkspaceError.message}</div>
1661	            {forkWorkspaceError.details?.length ? <div className="mt-1 opacity-80">{forkWorkspaceError.details.join(" ")}</div> : null}
1662	          </div>
1663	        ) : null}
1664	      </div>
1665	      <DialogFooter>
1666	        <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => gitWorkspaceManager.setKey("activeDialog", null)}>
1667	          {t("common.cancel")}
1668	        </Button>
1669	        <Button
1670	          type="button"
1671	          onClick={confirmForkMessageIntoWorktree}
1672	          disabled={forkWorkspaceLoading || !forkBranchName.trim() || !forkCheckoutPath.trim() || Boolean(forkWorkspacePendingOperation) || recoverRun.isPending}
1673	        >
1674	          {t("git.workspace.dialog.fork.confirm")}
1675	        </Button>
1676	      </DialogFooter>
1677	    </DialogContent>
1678	  </Dialog>
1679	  </div>
1680	
1681	  );
1682	});
1683	export { ConversationMain };
1684
```

```text
169	export function getTerminalActivityVersion(activity: TerminalActivityItem[]) {
170	  return activity.map((item) => {
171	    switch (item.kind) {
172	      case "pending_assistant":
173	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.status}`;
174	      case "thinking":
175	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.inProgress}:${item.thoughts.join("\n").length}`;
176	      case "tool":
177	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.status}:${item.title.length}:${item.inputPane?.text.length ?? 0}:${item.outputPane?.text.length ?? 0}`;
178	      case "tool_group":
179	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.status}:${item.tools.length}:${item.tools.map((tool) => `${tool.id}:${tool.status}:${tool.title.length}:${tool.inputPane?.text.length ?? 0}:${tool.outputPane?.text.length ?? 0}`).join(",")}`;
180	      case "permission":
181	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.status}:${item.title.length}:${item.text.length}:${item.detail?.length ?? 0}`;
182	      case "message":
183	      case "user_message":
184	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.text.length}`;
185	      case "generated_images":
186	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.images.map((image) => image.id).join(",")}`;
187	      case "work_summary":
188	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.durationMs}:${item.inProgress}:${item.items.length}`;
189	    }
190	  }).join("|");
191	}
192	
193	export function shouldTerminalConnectorExtend(
194	  activityKind: TerminalActivityKind,
195	  adjacentActivityKind: TerminalActivityKind | undefined,
196	) {
197	  return activityKind !== "user_message"
198	    && adjacentActivityKind !== undefined
199	    && adjacentActivityKind !== "user_message";
200	}
```

```text
326	    // No entry is newer than the message: it belongs after everything loaded.
327	    const anchor = nextEntry ? nextEntry.seq : latestSeq + 1;
```

```text
1	/**
2	 * Single source of truth for the unified worker conversation stream on
3	 * the frontend.
4	 *
5	 * Per (workerId) we hold a contiguous *window* [lowestSeq..latestContiguousSeq]:
6	 *   - entries: the contiguous window in seq order, never with gaps.
7	 *   - lowestSeq: the lowest seq currently in `entries` (0 if empty).
8	 *   - latestContiguousSeq: the highest seq we have proven contiguous
9	 *     from lowestSeq. Used as the cursor for forward refetch.
10	 *   - latestKnownSeq: the highest seq the server has told us exists.
11	 *     Updates immediately on a wake-up frame; the fetch fills the gap.
12	 *   - hasOlder: true if older entries exist on the server before
13	 *     lowestSeq. Set by initial tail-load and `loadOlder`.
14	 *   - status: "idle" → "loading" → "loaded"; "error" on a fetch failure.
15	 *
16	 * Initial load fetches the last N entries via `?limit=N`. Forward
17	 * extension uses `?afterSeq=latestContiguousSeq`. Backward hydration
18	 * uses `?beforeSeq=lowestSeq&limit=N` via `loadOlder`.
19	 *
20	 * SSE frames carry only `{ workerId, seq }` — the content is fetched
21	 * via the HTTP endpoint. The frame is a wake-up hint; we never trust
22	 * it as the entry payload.
23	 */
24	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

29	/**
30	 * Collapse multiple rows that share an entry `id` into a single
31	 * conversation item. The server's unified stream intentionally appends
32	 * a new row whenever an entry's text grows (so a streaming assistant
33	 * message can be revised in place — see the
34	 * `appends changed bridge message revisions so streaming prose can expand`
35	 * test in `tests/server/workers/output-store.test.ts`). It also
36	 * writes the same user_input id onto a newer worker's stream when a run
37	 * cycles through workers.
38	 *
39	 * Coalesce policy:
40	 *   - Keep the entry at its FIRST appearance in the input order, so
41	 *     conversational position doesn't jump when text grows.
42	 *   - Use the LATEST revision's text / metadata (text grows; latest
43	 *     revision is the finished one).
44	 *   - Take the timestamp from the copy on the NEWEST worker, because that
45	 *     is the one the live thread actually contains. Neither "earliest" nor
46	 *     "latest" works on its own: a rewind re-delivers a message with a new
47	 *     timestamp (newest is right, earliest pins it back above output it was
48	 *     rewound past), while `reconcileWorkerUserMessagesInStream` backfills
49	 *     history onto a fresh worker with the original `createdAt` (earliest is
50	 *     right, latest drags the opening prompt into the middle of the day).
51	 *     Worker order settles both. Copies from the same worker — streaming
52	 *     revisions, which share a timestamp anyway — keep the earliest.
53	 *   - Entries without an id pass through unchanged.
54	 *
55	 * `workerOrder` is the run's workers in creation order. Without it (a single
56	 * worker stream, or a caller that has no worker list yet) the earliest
57	 * timestamp is kept, which is the conservative choice: a message stays where
58	 * it already is.
59	 */

64	// v2: transcript recovery renumbered seqs and changed entry ids on repaired

70	const DEFAULT_MAX_WORKERS = 16;

92	export interface WorkerStreamState {

122	function latestSeq(entries: WorkerEntry[]) {
123	  return entries.reduce((latest, entry) => (
124	    typeof entry.seq === "number" && Number.isFinite(entry.seq)
125	      ? Math.max(latest, entry.seq)

127	  ), 0);
128	}
129	
130	function initialState(
131	  workerId: string,
132	  entries: WorkerEntry[] = [],
133	  options: { needsTailValidation?: boolean } = {},
134	): WorkerStreamState {
135	  const latest = latestSeq(entries);
136	  const lowest = entries.length > 0
137	    ? entries.reduce((acc, entry) => (
138	      typeof entry.seq === "number" && entry.seq > 0 && (acc === 0 || entry.seq < acc) ? entry.seq : acc
139	    ), 0)
140	    : 0;
141	  return {
142	    workerId,
143	    entries,
144	    lowestSeq: lowest,
145	    latestContiguousSeq: latest,
146	    latestKnownSeq: latest,
147	    hasOlder: lowest > 1,
148	    status: entries.length > 0 ? "loaded" : "idle",
149	    lastError: null,
150	    needsTailValidation: options.needsTailValidation,
151	  };
152	}
153	
154	function defaultStorage(): WorkerEntryStorage | null {
155	  // Worker transcript bodies are server-owned and fetched through the

213
```

```text
311	  ensureLoaded(workerId: string): Promise<void> {
312	    const state = this.getState(workerId);
313	    if (state.status === "loading") {
314	      return this.inFlightByWorker.get(workerId) ?? Promise.resolve();

321	      state.status === "loaded"

349	      // start the older-page request after the refresh settles.

370	  /**

509	        // drop the page — `loadOlder` can be retried.
```

```text
598	  private applyFetchResult(
599	    workerId: string,
600	    cursorBeforeFetch: number,
601	    response: FetchResponse,
602	  ): void {
603	    const previous = this.getState(workerId);
604	    // The endpoint returns entries strictly newer than `afterSeq`. If
605	    // the caller's view advanced between request and response, drop
606	    // anything we've already merged.
607	    const incoming = response.entries
608	      .filter((entry) => entry.seq > previous.latestContiguousSeq)
609	      .sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq);
610	
611	    let nextContiguous = previous.latestContiguousSeq;
612	    const merged = [...previous.entries];
613	    for (const entry of incoming) {
614	      if (entry.seq === nextContiguous + 1) {
615	        merged.push(entry);
616	        nextContiguous = entry.seq;
617	      } else if (entry.seq > nextContiguous + 1) {
618	        // Gap. Don't include the entry; the next forward load (kicked off
619	        // below) will cover both this seq and the missing ones.
620	        break;
621	      }
622	    }
623	
624	    const nextKnown = Math.max(previous.latestKnownSeq, response.latestSeq, nextContiguous);
625	    // If we started with no entries, lowestSeq becomes the first merged
626	    // entry's seq; otherwise the window's lower bound is unchanged.
627	    const nextLowest = previous.lowestSeq > 0
628	      ? previous.lowestSeq
629	      : merged[0]?.seq ?? 0;
630	    const next: WorkerStreamState = {
631	      ...previous,
632	      // Reuse the previous array when the poll appended nothing. The spread
633	      // above always allocates, so an idle validation poll used to hand every
634	      // subscriber a brand-new `entries` identity and invalidate each memo
635	      // derived from it — the transcript merge and Terminal's activity
636	      // rebuild both re-ran for a byte-identical result.
637	      entries: merged.length > previous.entries.length ? merged : previous.entries,
638	      lowestSeq: nextLowest,
639	      latestContiguousSeq: nextContiguous,
640	      latestKnownSeq: nextKnown,
641	      // hasOlder is preserved; only tail-load or loadOlder updates it.
642	      // A forward fetch starting from lowestSeq=0 (empty window) can't
643	      // assert anything about whether older entries exist.
644	      status: "loaded",
645	      lastError: null,
646	      needsTailValidation: false,
647	    };
648	    this.updateState(workerId, next);
649	
650	    // Gap chasing happens in the fetch wrapper's .finally so that
651	    // inFlightByWorker is cleared before the recursive call.
652	  }
653	
654	  private updateState(workerId: string, next: WorkerStreamState): void {
655	    this.stateByWorker.set(workerId, next);
656	    if (next.status === "loaded" && !next.needsTailValidation) {
657	      this.everLoadedWorkers.add(workerId);
658	    }
659	    if (next.status === "loaded") {
660	      this.rememberState(workerId, next.entries);
661	    }
662	    const listeners = this.listenersByWorker.get(workerId);
663	    if (!listeners) {
664	      return;
665	    }
666	    for (const listener of listeners) {
667	      listener(next);
668	    }
669	  }
670	
671	  private getCachedEntries(workerId: string): WorkerEntry[] {
672	    const cached = this.readEnvelope().workers[workerId];
673	    if (!cached || !isWorkerEntryArray(cached.entries)) {
674	      return [];
675	    }
676	    // Trim to the tail window. The cache used to rehydrate full
677	    // transcripts which caused the worker view to render every entry
678	    // from seq 1 and then auto-scroll down — bad UX on long sessions.
679	    // We want the user to see the most recent N entries immediately,
680	    // then load older ones on scroll-back via loadOlder().
681	    if (cached.entries.length > DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT) {
682	      return cached.entries.slice(-DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT);
683	    }
684	    return cached.entries;
685	  }
686	
687	  private rememberState(workerId: string, entries: WorkerEntry[]): void {
688	    if (!this.storage) {
689	      return;
690	    }
691	
692	    const currentEnvelope = this.readEnvelope();
693	    if (entries.length === 0) {
694	      const { [workerId]: _removed, ...workers } = currentEnvelope.workers;
695	      this.envelope = { ...currentEnvelope, workers };
696	      this.scheduleFlush();
697	      return;
698	    }
699	
700	    this.envelope = this.pruneEnvelope({
701	      ...currentEnvelope,
702	      workers: {
703	        ...currentEnvelope.workers,
704	        [workerId]: {
705	          updatedAt: this.now(),
706	          entries,
707	        },
708	      },
709	    });
710	    this.scheduleFlush();
711	  }
712	
713	  /** Write the pending cache envelope to storage now. */
714	  flushCache(): void {
715	    if (this.flushTimer !== null) {
716	      clearTimeout(this.flushTimer);
717	      this.flushTimer = null;
718	    }
719	
720	    const envelope = this.envelope;
721	    if (!this.storage || !envelope) {
722	      return;
723	    }
724	
725	    const serialized = JSON.stringify({
726	      version: 1,
727	      workers: Object.fromEntries(
728	        Object.entries(envelope.workers).map(([id, cached]) => [id, {
729	          updatedAt: cached.updatedAt,
730	          entries: cached.entries.map(compactEntryForCache),
731	        }]),
732	      ),
733	    } satisfies WorkerEntriesCacheEnvelope);
734	    if (serialized.length > this.maxSerializedBytes) {
735	      return;
736	    }
737	
738	    try {
739	      this.storage.setItem(this.storageKey, serialized);
740	    } catch {
741	      // localStorage is a preview cache only; live server state remains authoritative.
742	    }
743	  }
744	
745	  private scheduleFlush(): void {
746	    if (this.flushTimer !== null) {
747	      return;
748	    }
749	
750	    if (this.flushIntervalMs <= 0) {
751	      this.flushCache();
752	      return;
753	    }
754	
755	    this.flushTimer = setTimeout(() => {
756	      this.flushTimer = null;
757	      this.flushCache();
758	    }, this.flushIntervalMs);
759	    (this.flushTimer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
760	  }
761	
762	  private readEnvelope(): WorkerEntriesCacheEnvelope {
763	    if (!this.storage) {
764	      return emptyEnvelope();
765	    }
766	
767	    if (this.envelope) {
768	      return this.envelope;
769	    }
770	
771	    try {
772	      const raw = this.storage.getItem(this.storageKey);
773	      if (!raw) {
774	        this.envelope = emptyEnvelope();
775	        return this.envelope;
776	      }
777	
778	      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<WorkerEntriesCacheEnvelope>;
779	      if (parsed.version !== 1 || typeof parsed.workers !== "object" || parsed.workers === null) {
780	        this.envelope = emptyEnvelope();
781	        return this.envelope;
782	      }
783	      this.envelope = {
784	        version: 1,
785	        workers: parsed.workers as Record<string, CachedWorkerStream>,
786	      };
787	      return this.envelope;
788	    } catch {
789	      try {
790	        this.storage.removeItem(this.storageKey);
791	      } catch {
792	        // Ignore cleanup failures.
793	      }
794	      this.envelope = emptyEnvelope();
795	      return this.envelope;
796	    }
797	  }
```

```text
797	  }
798	
799	  private pruneEnvelope(envelope: WorkerEntriesCacheEnvelope): WorkerEntriesCacheEnvelope {
800	    return {
801	      version: 1,
802	      workers: Object.fromEntries(
803	        Object.entries(envelope.workers)
804	          .sort((left, right) => {
805	            const updatedDelta = right[1].updatedAt - left[1].updatedAt;
806	            return updatedDelta !== 0 ? updatedDelta : left[0].localeCompare(right[0]);
807	          })
808	          .slice(0, this.maxWorkers),
809	      ),
810	    };
811	  }
812	}
813	
814	// A single process-wide instance is fine because manager state is keyed
815	// by workerId and the same worker is never owned by two tabs. The
816	// reset() method exists for tests; production code should not call it.
817	export const workerEntriesManager = new WorkerEntriesManager();
818	
819	/**
820	 * Subscribe to the unified worker conversation stream for a single
821	 * worker. Returns the live state plus a `isLoaded` boolean derived
822	 * from the manager's contiguous-prefix invariant.
823	 *
824	 * `workerId === null` returns a stable empty state and skips the
825	 * fetch.
826	 */
827	interface UseWorkerStreamOptions {
828	  refreshIntervalMs?: number | null;
829	}
830	
831	export function useWorkerStream(
832	  workerId: string | null,
833	  initialEntries: WorkerEntry[] = [],
834	  options: UseWorkerStreamOptions = {},
835	) {
836	  const runtimeApis = useRuntimeAPIs();
837	  workerEntriesManager.configure(runtimeApis.workers.listEntries);
838	  const state = useSyncExternalStore(
839	    useCallback((listener) => (
840	      workerId ? workerEntriesManager.subscribe(workerId, listener) : () => {}
841	    ), [workerId]),
842	    useCallback(() => (
843	      workerId ? workerEntriesManager.getState(workerId, initialEntries) : EMPTY_WORKER_STREAM_STATE
844	    ), [initialEntries, workerId]),
845	    () => EMPTY_WORKER_STREAM_STATE,
846	  );
847	  useEffect(() => {
848	    if (!workerId) {
849	      return;
850	    }
851	    void workerEntriesManager.ensureLoaded(workerId);
852	  }, [workerId]);
853	  useEffect(() => {
854	    if (!workerId || !options.refreshIntervalMs || options.refreshIntervalMs <= 0) {
855	      return;
856	    }
857	    const intervalId = window.setInterval(() => {
858	      void workerEntriesManager.refresh(workerId);
859	    }, options.refreshIntervalMs);
860	    return () => window.clearInterval(intervalId);
861	  }, [options.refreshIntervalMs, workerId]);
862	  const loadOlder = useCallback((limit?: number) => {
863	    if (!workerId) return Promise.resolve();
864	    return workerEntriesManager.loadOlder(workerId, limit);
865	  }, [workerId]);
866	  const entries = useMemo(() => coalesceWorkerEntriesById(state.entries), [state.entries]);
867	  return {
868	    state,
869	    entries,
870	    isLoaded: workerId ? workerEntriesManager.isLoaded(workerId) : false,
871	    hasEverLoaded: workerId ? workerEntriesManager.hasEverLoaded(workerId) : false,
872	    hasOlder: state.hasOlder,
873	    loadOlder,
874	  };
875	}
876
```

```text
1	/**
2	 * Single source of truth for the unified worker conversation stream on
3	 * the frontend.
4	 *
5	 * Per (workerId) we hold a contiguous *window* [lowestSeq..latestContiguousSeq]:
6	 *   - entries: the contiguous window in seq order, never with gaps.
7	 *   - lowestSeq: the lowest seq currently in `entries` (0 if empty).
8	 *   - latestContiguousSeq: the highest seq we have proven contiguous
9	 *     from lowestSeq. Used as the cursor for forward refetch.
10	 *   - latestKnownSeq: the highest seq the server has told us exists.
11	 *     Updates immediately on a wake-up frame; the fetch fills the gap.
12	 *   - hasOlder: true if older entries exist on the server before
13	 *     lowestSeq. Set by initial tail-load and `loadOlder`.
14	 *   - status: "idle" → "loading" → "loaded"; "error" on a fetch failure.
15	 *
16	 * Initial load fetches the last N entries via `?limit=N`. Forward
17	 * extension uses `?afterSeq=latestContiguousSeq`. Backward hydration
18	 * uses `?beforeSeq=lowestSeq&limit=N` via `loadOlder`.
19	 *
20	 * SSE frames carry only `{ workerId, seq }` — the content is fetched
21	 * via the HTTP endpoint. The frame is a wake-up hint; we never trust
22	 * it as the entry payload.
23	 */
24	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
25	import { coalesceWorkerEntriesById, type WorkerEntry } from "@/shared/worker-entries";
26	import type { RuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/types";
27	import { useRuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/provider";
28	
29	/**
30	 * Collapse multiple rows that share an entry `id` into a single
31	 * conversation item. The server's unified stream intentionally appends
32	 * a new row whenever an entry's text grows (so a streaming assistant
33	 * message can be revised in place — see the
34	 * `appends changed bridge message revisions so streaming prose can expand`
35	 * test in `tests/server/workers/output-store.test.ts`). It also
36	 * writes the same user_input id onto a newer worker's stream when a run
37	 * cycles through workers.
38	 *
39	 * Coalesce policy:
40	 *   - Keep the entry at its FIRST appearance in the input order, so
41	 *     conversational position doesn't jump when text grows.
42	 *   - Use the LATEST revision's text / metadata (text grows; latest
43	 *     revision is the finished one).
44	 *   - Take the timestamp from the copy on the NEWEST worker, because that
45	 *     is the one the live thread actually contains. Neither "earliest" nor
46	 *     "latest" works on its own: a rewind re-delivers a message with a new
47	 *     timestamp (newest is right, earliest pins it back above output it was
48	 *     rewound past), while `reconcileWorkerUserMessagesInStream` backfills
49	 *     history onto a fresh worker with the original `createdAt` (earliest is
50	 *     right, latest drags the opening prompt into the middle of the day).
51	 *     Worker order settles both. Copies from the same worker — streaming
52	 *     revisions, which share a timestamp anyway — keep the earliest.
53	 *   - Entries without an id pass through unchanged.
54	 *
55	 * `workerOrder` is the run's workers in creation order. Without it (a single
56	 * worker stream, or a caller that has no worker list yet) the earliest
57	 * timestamp is kept, which is the conservative choice: a message stays where
58	 * it already is.
59	 */
60	export { coalesceWorkerEntriesById };
61	
62	type WorkerEntryStorage = Pick<Storage, "getItem" | "setItem" | "removeItem">;
63	
64	// v2: transcript recovery renumbered seqs and changed entry ids on repaired
65	// worker streams. This cache merges with server data by entry id, so a v1
66	// cache keeps rendering stale pre-repair entries (raw streaming fragments)
67	// alongside the fixed ones — a transcript shredded mid-word. Bumping the key
68	// retires those caches instead of requiring users to clear storage by hand.
69	const DEFAULT_STORAGE_KEY = "omni-worker-entries-cache:v2";
70	const DEFAULT_MAX_WORKERS = 16;
71	const DEFAULT_MAX_SERIALIZED_BYTES = 4_000_000;
72	const DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT = 100;
73	const DEFAULT_OLDER_LIMIT = 100;
74	// Entries are remembered on every append and every stream poll. Serializing
75	// the whole cache envelope synchronously on each one is main-thread work that
76	// competes with keystrokes, so the write is coalesced onto a trailing timer
77	// while reads stay served from the in-memory envelope.
78	const DEFAULT_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS = 1_000;

80	interface CachedWorkerStream {
81	  updatedAt: number;
82	  entries: WorkerEntry[];
83	}
84	
85	interface WorkerEntriesCacheEnvelope {
86	  version: 1;
87	  workers: Record<string, CachedWorkerStream>;
88	}
89	
90	export type WorkerStreamStatus = "idle" | "loading" | "loaded" | "error";
91	
92	export interface WorkerStreamState {
93	  workerId: string;
94	  entries: WorkerEntry[];
95	  lowestSeq: number;
96	  latestContiguousSeq: number;
97	  latestKnownSeq: number;
98	  hasOlder: boolean;

100	  lastError: string | null;
101	  needsTailValidation?: boolean;

106	interface FetchResponse {

108	  latestSeq: number;
109	  hasOlder?: boolean;
110	}
111	
112	export interface WorkerEntriesManagerOptions {
113	  listEntries?: RuntimeAPIs["workers"]["listEntries"];
114	  storage?: WorkerEntryStorage | null;
115	  storageKey?: string;
116	  maxWorkers?: number;
117	  maxSerializedBytes?: number;
118	  now?: () => number;
119	  flushIntervalMs?: number;

122	function latestSeq(entries: WorkerEntry[]) {
123	  return entries.reduce((latest, entry) => (
124	    typeof entry.seq === "number" && Number.isFinite(entry.seq)
125	      ? Math.max(latest, entry.seq)

127	  ), 0);

129	
130	function initialState(

132	  entries: WorkerEntry[] = [],
133	  options: { needsTailValidation?: boolean } = {},
134	): WorkerStreamState {

136	  const lowest = entries.length > 0
137	    ? entries.reduce((acc, entry) => (
138	      typeof entry.seq === "number" && entry.seq > 0 && (acc === 0 || entry.seq < acc) ? entry.seq : acc

140	    : 0;
141	  return {
142	    workerId,
143	    entries,
144	    lowestSeq: lowest,
145	    latestContiguousSeq: latest,
146	    latestKnownSeq: latest,
147	    hasOlder: lowest > 1,
148	    status: entries.length > 0 ? "loaded" : "idle",
149	    lastError: null,
150	    needsTailValidation: options.needsTailValidation,
```

```text
154	function defaultStorage(): WorkerEntryStorage | null {
155	  // Worker transcript bodies are server-owned and fetched through the
156	  // unified worker stream. Persisting them in localStorage can crowd out
157	  // preferences and credentials, so browser storage is opt-in for tests or
158	  // explicitly bounded consumers only.
159	  return null;
160	}
161	
162	function emptyEnvelope(): WorkerEntriesCacheEnvelope {
163	  return {
164	    version: 1,
165	    workers: {},
166	  };
167	}
168	
169	function isWorkerEntryArray(value: unknown): value is WorkerEntry[] {
170	  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every((entry) => (
171	    typeof entry === "object"
172	    && entry !== null
173	    && typeof (entry as Partial<WorkerEntry>).id === "string"
174	    && typeof (entry as Partial<WorkerEntry>).seq === "number"
175	  ));
176	}
177	
178	function compactEntryForCache(entry: WorkerEntry): WorkerEntry {
179	  return {
180	    ...entry,
181	    raw: undefined,
182	  };
183	}

185	export const EMPTY_WORKER_STREAM_STATE: WorkerStreamState = initialState("__none__");

187	export class WorkerEntriesManager {
188	  private readonly stateByWorker = new Map<string, WorkerStreamState>();
189	  private readonly listenersByWorker = new Map<string, Set<Listener>>();
190	  private readonly inFlightByWorker = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
191	  private readonly olderLoadInFlightByWorker = new Set<string>();
192	  private readonly wakeVersionByWorker = new Map<string, number>();
193	  private readonly everLoadedWorkers = new Set<string>();
194	  private listEntries: RuntimeAPIs["workers"]["listEntries"] | null;
195	  private readonly storage: WorkerEntryStorage | null;
196	  private readonly storageKey: string;
197	  private readonly maxWorkers: number;
198	  private readonly maxSerializedBytes: number;
199	  private readonly now: () => number;
200	  private readonly flushIntervalMs: number;
201	  private envelope: WorkerEntriesCacheEnvelope | null = null;
202	  private flushTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
203	
204	  constructor(options: WorkerEntriesManagerOptions = {}) {
205	    const usesDefaultStorage = options.storage === undefined;
206	    this.listEntries = options.listEntries ?? null;
207	    this.storage = usesDefaultStorage ? defaultStorage() : options.storage ?? null;
208	    this.storageKey = options.storageKey ?? DEFAULT_STORAGE_KEY;
209	    this.maxWorkers = options.maxWorkers ?? DEFAULT_MAX_WORKERS;
210	    this.maxSerializedBytes = options.maxSerializedBytes ?? DEFAULT_MAX_SERIALIZED_BYTES;
211	    this.now = options.now ?? Date.now;
212	    this.flushIntervalMs = options.flushIntervalMs ?? DEFAULT_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS;
213	
214	    if (usesDefaultStorage && this.storage && typeof window !== "undefined") {
215	      const flushNow = () => this.flushCache();
216	      window.addEventListener("pagehide", flushNow);
217	      window.addEventListener("visibilitychange", flushNow);
218	    }
219	  }
220	
221	  configure(listEntries: RuntimeAPIs["workers"]["listEntries"]) {
222	    this.listEntries = listEntries;
223	  }
224	
225	  private load(input: Parameters<RuntimeAPIs["workers"]["listEntries"]>[0]) {
226	    if (!this.listEntries) {
227	      return Promise.reject(new Error("Worker entries are not connected to the runtime."));
228	    }
229	    return this.listEntries(input) as Promise<FetchResponse>;
230	  }
231	
232	  getState(workerId: string, initialEntries: WorkerEntry[] = []): WorkerStreamState {
233	    const existing = this.stateByWorker.get(workerId);
234	    if (existing) {
235	      if (
236	        initialEntries.length > 0
237	        && existing.latestContiguousSeq === 0
238	        && existing.entries.length === 0
239	      ) {
240	        const hydrated = initialState(workerId, initialEntries);
241	        this.stateByWorker.set(workerId, hydrated);
242	        return hydrated;
243	      }
244	      return existing;
245	    }
246	    const cachedEntries = initialEntries.length > 0 ? [] : this.getCachedEntries(workerId);
247	    const rawNextEntries = initialEntries.length > 0 ? initialEntries : cachedEntries;
248	    // Trim to the tail window — see getCachedEntries for the rationale.
249	    // Same trim is applied to caller-supplied initialEntries (e.g. an
250	    // event-snapshot hydration) so a long transcript doesn't render in
251	    // full on mount.
252	    const nextEntries = rawNextEntries.length > DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT
253	      ? rawNextEntries.slice(-DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT)
254	      : rawNextEntries;
255	    const next = initialState(workerId, nextEntries, {
256	      needsTailValidation: initialEntries.length === 0 && cachedEntries.length > 0,
257	    });
258	    this.stateByWorker.set(workerId, next);
259	    if (nextEntries.length > 0) {
260	      this.rememberState(workerId, nextEntries);
261	    }
262	    return next;
263	  }
264	
265	  isLoaded(workerId: string): boolean {
266	    const state = this.stateByWorker.get(workerId);
267	    if (!state) {
268	      return false;
269	    }
270	    return (
271	      state.status === "loaded"
272	      && state.latestContiguousSeq === state.latestKnownSeq
273	      && !state.needsTailValidation
274	    );
275	  }
276	
277	  /**
278	   * Latched variant of `isLoaded`: once a worker's stream has completed a
279	   * validated load, this stays true through wake-up catch-up fetches and
280	   * refresh cycles. `isLoaded` flips false on every appended entry until the
281	   * gap is fetched, which makes it unusable as a render gate — anything
282	   * conditioned on it flickers while the worker streams output.
283	   */
284	  hasEverLoaded(workerId: string): boolean {
285	    if (this.everLoadedWorkers.has(workerId)) {
286	      return true;
287	    }
288	    const state = this.stateByWorker.get(workerId);
289	    if (state && state.status === "loaded" && !state.needsTailValidation) {
290	      this.everLoadedWorkers.add(workerId);
291	      return true;
292	    }
293	    return false;
294	  }
295	
296	  subscribe(workerId: string, listener: Listener): () => void {
297	    let bucket = this.listenersByWorker.get(workerId);
298	    if (!bucket) {
299	      bucket = new Set<Listener>();
300	      this.listenersByWorker.set(workerId, bucket);
301	    }
302	    bucket.add(listener);
303	    return () => {
304	      bucket?.delete(listener);
305	      if (bucket && bucket.size === 0) {
306	        this.listenersByWorker.delete(workerId);

309	  }

311	  ensureLoaded(workerId: string): Promise<void> {
312	    const state = this.getState(workerId);
313	    if (state.status === "loading") {

338	   * returned page is prepended to the window. Caller can poll

370	  /**

389	  /**

423	  /**
```

```text
831	export function buildConversationTimelineItems({
832	  messages,
833	  executionEvents,
834	  supervisorInterventions = [],
835	  workers = [],
836	  isPlanningRun = false,
837	}: {
838	  messages: MessageRecord[];
839	  executionEvents: ExecutionEventRecord[];
840	  supervisorInterventions?: SupervisorInterventionRecord[];
841	  workers?: ConversationWorkerRecord[];
842	  isPlanningRun?: boolean;
843	}): ConversationTimelineItem[] {
844	  const items: ConversationTimelineItem[] = messages
845	    .filter(shouldRenderMessageInMainConversation)
846	    .map((message) => ({
847	      type: "message",
848	      id: message.id,
849	      createdAt: message.createdAt,
850	      message,
851	    }));
852	  const sortedExecutionEvents = [...executionEvents].sort((a, b) => {
853	    const timeDelta = timestampMs(a.createdAt) - timestampMs(b.createdAt);
854	    return timeDelta !== 0 ? timeDelta : a.id.localeCompare(b.id);
855	  });
856	  const workerIdsWithSpawnEvents = new Set(
857	    executionEvents
858	      .filter((event) => event.eventType === "worker_spawned" && event.workerId)
859	      .map((event) => event.workerId as string),
860	  );
861	
862	  for (const worker of workers) {
863	    if (!worker.createdAt || workerIdsWithSpawnEvents.has(worker.id)) {
864	      continue;
865	    }
866	
867	    items.push({
868	      type: "activity",
869	      id: `worker-start:${worker.id}`,
870	      createdAt: worker.createdAt,
871	      text: summarizeWorkerStartRecord(worker, isPlanningRun),
872	    });
873	  }
874	
875	  for (const event of sortedExecutionEvents) {
876	    if (!shouldShowExecutionEventInTimeline(event, messages)) {
877	      continue;
878	    }
879	
880	    const text = (event.eventType === "worker_spawned"
881	      ? summarizeWorkerSpawnEvent(event, isPlanningRun)
882	      : summarizeInlineEvent(event))?.trim() ?? "";
883	    if (!text) {
884	      continue;
885	    }
886	
887	    items.push({
888	      type: "activity",
889	      id: event.id,
890	      createdAt: event.createdAt,
891	      event,
892	      text,
893	    });
894	  }
895	
896	  for (const intervention of supervisorInterventions) {
897	    const text = summarizeSupervisorIntervention(intervention);
898	    if (!text) {
899	      continue;
900	    }
901	
902	    items.push({
903	      type: "activity",
904	      id: intervention.id,
905	      createdAt: intervention.createdAt,
906	      text,
907	    });
908	  }
909	
910	  return items.sort((a, b) => {
911	    const timeDelta = timestampMs(a.createdAt) - timestampMs(b.createdAt);
912	    if (timeDelta !== 0) {
913	      return timeDelta;
914	    }
915	    if (a.type !== b.type) {
916	      return a.type === "message" ? -1 : 1;
917	    }
918	    return a.id.localeCompare(b.id);
919	  });
920	}
921	
922	export function stripRunFailurePrefix(value: string | null | undefined) {
923	  if (!value) {
924	    return "";
925	  }
926	
927	  return value.replace(/^run failed:\s*/i, "").trim();
928	}
929	
930	export function extractWorkerFailureDetail(messages: MessageRecord[]) {
931	  const workerMessages = [...messages]
932	    .filter((message) => message.role === "worker")
933	    .sort(compareNewestByCreatedAtThenId);
934	
935	  for (const message of workerMessages) {
936	    const detailMatch = message.content.match(/"detail":"([^"]+)"/i);
937	    if (detailMatch?.[1]) {
938	      return detailMatch[1];
939	    }
940	
941	    const errorLine = message.content
942	      .split("\n")
943	      .map((line) => line.trim())
944	      .find((line) => /^error[:\s]/i.test(line) || /^failed[:\s]/i.test(line));
945	    if (errorLine) {
946	      return errorLine.replace(/^(error|failed)[:\s]*/i, "").trim();
947	    }
948	  }
949	
950	  return "";
951	}
952	
953	export function parseWorkerTypes(value: string | null | undefined): WorkerType[] {
954	  if (!value?.trim()) {
955	    return WORKER_OPTIONS.map((option) => option.value);
956	  }
957	
958	  try {
959	    const parsed = JSON.parse(value);

962	    }
963	
964	    const allowed = new Set(WORKER_OPTIONS.map((option) => option.value));
965	    const normalized = parsed
966	      .filter((entry): entry is string => typeof entry === "string")
967	      .map((entry) => entry.trim().toLowerCase())
968	      .filter((entry): entry is WorkerType => allowed.has(entry as WorkerType));
969	
970	    return normalized.length > 0 ? Array.from(new Set(normalized)) : WORKER_OPTIONS.map((option) => option.value);
971	  } catch {
972	    return WORKER_OPTIONS.map((option) => option.value);
973	  }
974	}
975	
976	export function parseProjectList(value: string | null | undefined) {
977	  if (!value?.trim()) {
978	    return [] as string[];
979	  }
```

```text
1	/**
2	 * Run-scoped sibling of `WorkerEntriesManager`. Fetches the merged
3	 * transcript across every worker in a run from
4	 * the typed conversation transcript API, so the conversation UI can
5	 * render full history when the run has cycled through multiple workers
6	 * (cancel + respawn).
7	 *
8	 * Pagination is token-based because seq is per-worker, not global. The
9	 * server hands back an opaque cursor; we hand it back on the next poll.
10	 */
11	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
12	import type { WorkerEntry } from "@/shared/worker-entries";
13	import { coalesceWorkerEntriesById } from "./WorkerEntriesManager";
14	import type { RuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/types";
15	import { useRuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/provider";
16	
17	export interface ConversationTranscriptEntry extends WorkerEntry {
18	  workerId: string;
19	}
20	
21	export interface ConversationTranscriptState {
22	  entries: ConversationTranscriptEntry[];
23	  latestToken: string | null;
24	  oldestToken: string | null;
25	  hasOlder: boolean;
26	  status: "idle" | "loading" | "loaded" | "error";
27	  lastError: string | null;
28	  workerIds: string[];
29	  /**
30	   * Latched: true once this run's transcript has loaded at least once.
31	   * `status` cycles back through "loading" on every poll refresh, so
32	   * render gates must use this instead of `status === "loaded"`.
33	   */
34	  hasLoadedOnce: boolean;
35	}
36	
37	interface TranscriptFetchResponse {
38	  entries: ConversationTranscriptEntry[];
39	  latestToken: string;
40	  oldestToken?: string;
41	  hasOlder?: boolean;
42	  workerIds: string[];
43	}
44	
45	const DEFAULT_TRANSCRIPT_LIMIT = 100;
46	
47	const EMPTY: ConversationTranscriptState = Object.freeze({
48	  entries: [],
49	  latestToken: null,
50	  oldestToken: null,
51	  hasOlder: false,
52	  status: "idle",
53	  lastError: null,
54	  workerIds: [],
55	  hasLoadedOnce: false,
56	});
57	
58	export const EMPTY_CONVERSATION_TRANSCRIPT_STATE = EMPTY;
59	
60	export interface ConversationTranscriptManagerOptions {
61	  transcript?: RuntimeAPIs["conversations"]["transcript"];
62	}
63	
64	export class ConversationTranscriptManager {
65	  private readonly stateByRunId = new Map<string, ConversationTranscriptState>();
66	  private readonly listenersByRunId = new Map<string, Set<() => void>>();
67	  private readonly inFlightByRunId = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
68	  private readonly olderLoadInFlightByRunId = new Set<string>();
69	  private transcript: RuntimeAPIs["conversations"]["transcript"] | null;
70	
71	  constructor(options: ConversationTranscriptManagerOptions = {}) {
72	    this.transcript = options.transcript ?? null;
73	  }
74	
75	  configure(transcript: RuntimeAPIs["conversations"]["transcript"]) {
76	    this.transcript = transcript;
77	  }
78	
79	  private load(input: Parameters<RuntimeAPIs["conversations"]["transcript"]>[0]) {
80	    if (!this.transcript) {
81	      return Promise.reject(new Error("Conversation transcripts are not connected to the runtime."));
82	    }
83	    return this.transcript(input) as Promise<TranscriptFetchResponse>;
84	  }
85	
86	  getState(runId: string): ConversationTranscriptState {
87	    return this.stateByRunId.get(runId) ?? EMPTY;
88	  }
89	
90	  subscribe(runId: string, listener: () => void): () => void {
91	    const listeners = this.listenersByRunId.get(runId) ?? new Set();
92	    listeners.add(listener);
93	    this.listenersByRunId.set(runId, listeners);
94	    return () => {
95	      const current = this.listenersByRunId.get(runId);
96	      if (!current) return;
97	      current.delete(listener);
98	      if (current.size === 0) {
99	        this.listenersByRunId.delete(runId);
100	      }
101	    };
102	  }
103	
104	  ensureLoaded(runId: string): Promise<void> {
105	    const state = this.getState(runId);
106	    if (state.status === "loaded" || state.status === "loading") {
107	      return this.inFlightByRunId.get(runId) ?? Promise.resolve();
108	    }
109	    return this.fetchTail(runId);
110	  }
111	
112	  refresh(runId: string): Promise<void> {
113	    if (this.inFlightByRunId.has(runId)) {
114	      return this.inFlightByRunId.get(runId)!;
115	    }
116	    const state = this.getState(runId);
117	    return state.latestToken ? this.fetchForward(runId, state.latestToken) : this.fetchTail(runId);
118	  }
119	
120	  loadOlder(runId: string, limit: number = DEFAULT_TRANSCRIPT_LIMIT): Promise<void> {
121	    if (this.inFlightByRunId.has(runId)) {
122	      const existing = this.inFlightByRunId.get(runId)!;
123	      if (this.olderLoadInFlightByRunId.has(runId)) {
124	        return existing;
125	      }
126	      // A refresh may already be occupying the single-flight slot when the
127	      // user reaches the top of the viewport. Preserve that scroll-back
128	      // intent and start the older-page request after the refresh settles.
129	      return existing.then(
130	        () => this.loadOlder(runId, limit),
131	        () => this.loadOlder(runId, limit),
132	      );
133	    }
134	    const state = this.getState(runId);
135	    if (!state.hasOlder || !state.oldestToken) {
136	      return Promise.resolve();
137	    }
138	    this.olderLoadInFlightByRunId.add(runId);
139	    return this.fetchOlder(runId, state.oldestToken, limit).finally(() => {
140	      this.olderLoadInFlightByRunId.delete(runId);
141	    });
142	  }
143	
144	  reset(): void {
145	    this.stateByRunId.clear();
146	    this.inFlightByRunId.clear();
```

## Claude subagent agent-ab18e47bbe4533571: Claude · 2026-08-27T15:21:34.590Z

```text
Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me create a comprehensive report.

## Comprehensive Client-Side Event Stream State Investigation

### 1. How Events Accumulate Client-Side Over Long-Running Sessions

The client-side state management is structured in layers:

**Global Event Snapshot (EventStreamStateManager)**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/EventStreamStateManager.ts:362-376`
  - Stores runs, plans, workers, sessions, supervisor messages, and review records
  - State is held in `private state: EventStreamState` (line 362)
  - Updates via listener-based pub/sub model (line 363: `private readonly listeners = new Set<EventStreamStateListener>()`)
  - No direct array truncation in the state manager itself; truncation happens at lower layers

**Unified Worker Entries Stream (WorkerEntriesManager)**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/WorkerEntriesManager.ts:188-202`
  - Maintains per-worker window: `private readonly stateByWorker = new Map<string, WorkerStreamState>()`
  - Each worker holds `entries: WorkerEntry[]` in a bounded **contiguous window** model (not unbounded append)
  - Per-worker listeners: `private readonly listenersByWorker = new Map<string, Set<Listener>>()`

**Agent Output (Server-Side, sent to client)**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/agent-runtime/output-store.ts:8-22`
  - Live in-memory entries: `LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_LIMIT = 80` (line 9)
  - Live entry text: `LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_TEXT_CHARS = 5_000` (line 10)
  - Archive always keeps complete history on disk; streaming chunks are individually persisted as JSONL

### 2. Caps/Limits and Truncation

**Output Entry Limits (Server-Side, affects what flows to client):**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/agent-runtime/output-store.ts:315-325`
  - Line 317: `const liveLimit = totalEntries > LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_LIMIT ? LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_LIMIT - 1 : LIVE_OUTPUT_ENTRY_LIMIT;`
  - Line 321: `record.outputEntries.splice(0, record.outputEntries.length - liveLimit);` — **sliding window truncation** from head when live entries exceed 80
  - Raw string compaction limits: `LIVE_RAW_STRING_CHARS = 4_000` (line 11), `ARCHIVE_TOOL_RAW_STRING_CHARS = 8_000` (line 15)
  - Array items in raw output: `RAW_ARRAY_ITEMS = 200` (line 17), `RAW_OBJECT_KEYS = 100` (line 18), `RAW_DEPTH = 8` (line 19)
  - Compacting happens per-entry at line 101-116

**Worker Stream Limits (Client-Side):**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/WorkerEntriesManager.ts:72, 209-210`
  - Tail window: `DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT = 100` entries per worker (line 72)
  - Cache size: `DEFAULT_MAX_SERIALIZED_BYTES = 4_000_000` (line 71)
  - Worker cache: `DEFAULT_MAX_WORKERS = 16` (line 70)
  - On initial load (line 252-254): entries trimmed to tail if `length > DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT`: `rawNextEntries.slice(-DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT)`
  - On cache hydration (line 681-682): `cached.entries.slice(-DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT)` if cache exceeds 100 entries
  - Cache pruning (line 799-811): `pruneEnvelope()` sorts workers by `updatedAt` and keeps top 16 by `.slice(0, this.maxWorkers)`
  - Cache serialization size check (line 734): refuses to persist if `serialized.length > this.maxSerializedBytes`

**Snapshot Cache Limits:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/EventStreamSnapshotCacheManager.ts:105-120` (check for similar constants)

### 3. How Derived State (Turns, Groups, Blocks) is Computed from Raw Events

**buildAgentOutputActivity: Full Reconstruction on Every Call**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/lib/agent-output.ts:1259-1508`
- **Called every render** via `useMemo` in Terminal component (line 2508-2573 of Terminal.tsx)
- **O(n) forward scan** over all `outputEntries`:
  - Line 1283-1488: Iterates once through entries, building a single-pass flat `items` array
  - Creates Maps for deduplication: `toolIndexById`, `messageIndexById`, `permissionIndexByRequestId`, `protocolIndexByKey`, `thoughtLocationById`, `seenOutputEntryFingerprints` (lines 1261-1270)
  - **Not O(n^2)** — each entry is visited exactly once

**Fragment Coalescing: O(n) Pass**
- Line 1267-1269: `coalesceFragmentedMessageEntries()` wraps entries in another O(n) pass
- Creates temporary Sets within the pass (line 302-303): `fragmentSet`, `fragmentIds`
- No accumulated external state; sets are created and discarded per call

**Tool Grouping: O(n) Tail Pass**
- Line 1196-1224: `groupConsecutiveTools()` does a single O(n) scan to group adjacent tool items
- No external state accumulation

**Terminal Component Re-Render Trigger:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/Terminal.tsx:2508-2573`
  - `useMemo` memoization depends on `[entries, agent, suppressAcceptedPlanRows, multiWorkerOrdering, allowUserMessageFallback]` (implicit from closure)
  - **Every time `entries` identity changes**, the entire `buildAgentOutputActivity()` recomputes from scratch (O(n) work)
  - The `WorkerStreamState.entries` array gets a **new identity** on every append (line 612 of WorkerEntriesManager: `const merged = [...previous.entries]` creates new array)

### 4. What Causes Full Re-Render vs Incremental Append

**Full Re-Render Triggers:**

1. **WorkerStreamState.entries array identity change:**
   - `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/WorkerEntriesManager.ts:612, 637`
   - Line 612: `const merged = [...previous.entries];` — spread creates new array object on forward fetch
   - Line 637: `entries: merged.length > previous.entries.length ? merged : previous.entries` — reuses only if identical length
   - **Every fetch that appends an entry forces a new identity**, triggering all memoized consumers

2. **EventStreamStateManager checksum mismatch:**
   - `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/EventStreamStateManager.ts:45-59`
   - Line 51-52: Checks `next.snapshotChecksum !== current.snapshotChecksum`
   - Returns early (no listener notify) if `isNoOpSnapshotUpdate` passes, preventing render on duplicate polls
   - But when checksum differs, full state update propagates to all listeners

3. **LiveEventConnectionManager state updates:**
   - `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/LiveEventConnectionManager.ts:286-307`
   - Line 296: `this.applyUpdate(data)` on every `update` event
   - Line 484: on polling snapshot hits, `this.applyUpdate(data)` again
   - No incremental patching — always passes full snapshot state to manager

**Incremental Append Mechanism (Rare):**
- Tool updates use `applyToolUpdate()` to mutate an existing tool item in place (line 1075-1119 of agent-output.ts)
- But this only applies to the *derived activity* items, not the raw entries
- Raw entries always force array identity changes

### 5. Memory-Holding Structures: Maps/Sets Keyed by Event ID That Never Get Cleaned

**Per-Render Maps (Temporary, Cleaned on Next Render):**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/lib/agent-output.ts:1261-1270`
  - `toolIndexById: Map<string, number>` — maps entry toolCallId to position in `items` array
  - `messageIndexById: Map<string, number>` — maps message id to position
  - `permissionDetailByRequestId: Map<number, string>` — maps permission request id to detail string
  - `permissionIndexByRequestId: Map<number, number>` — maps permission request id to position
  - `protocolIndexByKey: Map<string, number>` — maps protocol key to position
  - `thoughtLocationById: Map<string, { activity, index }>` — maps thought id to location
  - `seenOutputEntryFingerprints: Set<string>` — deduplicates entry fingerprints
  - **All created fresh on every call to `buildAgentOutputActivity()`** → no persistence issue

**Terminal Component Session Maps:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/Terminal.tsx:2548-2561`
  - Line 2548: `const seqByActivityId = new Map<string, number>();`
  - Populated for each render from `visibleEntries`
  - **Discarded at end of render** — no long-term accumulation

**WorkerEntriesManager Long-Lived Maps (Per-Session, Not Leaky):**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/WorkerEntriesManager.ts:188-193`
  - `stateByWorker: Map<string, WorkerStreamState>` — keyed by workerId
  - `listenersByWorker: Map<string, Set<Listener>>` — keyed by workerId
  - `inFlightByWorker: Map<string, Promise<void>>` — keyed by workerId
  - `wakeVersionByWorker: Map<string, number>` — keyed by workerId
  - `olderLoadInFlightByWorker: Set<string>` — holds workerIds with pending older-page loads
  - `everLoadedWorkers: Set<string>` — tracks which workers have ever completed a load
  - **Cleaned up:** `reset()` method (line 436-448) clears all maps when session ends
  - **Not auto-cleaned:** Maps grow as new workers are added, but max is capped at `maxWorkers=16` via pruning in cache (line 799-811)

**EventStreamStateManager Listener Set:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/EventStreamStateManager.ts:363`
  - `listeners: Set<EventStreamStateListener>` — React components add/remove via `subscribe()`/unsubscribe
  - **Cleaned on component unmount** via the unsubscribe closure (line 443-446)
  - No per-event-id storage here

**SnapshotCache Long-Lived State:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/EventStreamSnapshotCacheManager.ts` (not fully read above, but noted in grep)
  - Likely has `snapshots: Map<string, ...>` keyed by run id or scope
  - Pruning via `maxSnapshots` (seen in config, similar to maxWorkers pattern)

**Fragment Run Cache in buildAgentOutputActivity:**
- Line 295-344: `coalesceFragmentedMessageEntries()` uses temporary Sets
  - `fragmentSet: Set<AgentOutputEntry>` — references to actual entry objects, flushed at line 321
  - `fragmentIds: Set<string>` — entry ids, flushed at line 322
  - **No accumulation** — both cleared per pass

### Summary Table of Limits

| Layer | Structure | Key Attribute | Limit Value | Truncation Method |
|-------|-----------|---|---|---|
| Server Output Store | `record.outputEntries` | live entries | 80 (configurable) | `splice(0, count)` sliding window |
| Server Raw Value | Raw object keys | flattened depth | 100 keys (RAW_OBJECT_KEYS) | slice then annotate truncation |
| Server Raw Value | Raw array items | array length | 200 items (RAW_ARRAY_ITEMS) | slice then annotate truncation |
| Client Worker Stream | `entries` per worker | contiguous window | 100 (DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT) | `.slice(-100)` on hydration |
| Client Worker Cache | workers in envelope | number of workers | 16 (DEFAULT_MAX_WORKERS) | `pruneEnvelope()` by `updatedAt` |
| Client Worker Cache | serialized bytes | total cache size | 4MB (DEFAULT_MAX_SERIALIZED_BYTES) | refuse flush if exceeded |

### Key Findings: No Critical Memory Leaks

1. **Render efficiency concern**: Every entry append causes full `buildAgentOutputActivity()` recomputation (O(n)), but without data structure leaks.
2. **Maps are transient**: Per-render Maps in `buildAgentOutputActivity()` are GC'd after render.
3. **Long-lived Maps are bounded**: WorkerEntriesManager and cache managers cap worker/snapshot count and serialize size.
4. **Listener cleanup works**: useSyncExternalStore subscription pattern properly unsubscribes on unmount.
5. **Snapshot checksum guards redundant work**: If polled snapshot checksum matches current, listeners aren't notified, preventing cascading re-renders on idle validation polls (line 512-514 of EventStreamStateManager).
```

## Claude subagent agent-aa2e4dc58583dd638: Claude · 2026-08-27T15:21:43.045Z

```text
Perfect! Now I have all the key information. Let me compile my findings:

## Summary

Based on my thorough exploration of the omniharness codebase, here's the complete map of how session conversation/turn lists are rendered in the web UI:

### 1. **Core Rendering Components**

**Primary Renderer:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/Terminal.tsx` (lines 2932-3013)
  - The `Terminal` component is the main entry point for rendering conversation/turn lists
  - For "native" variant (used in conversation main): renders from the `filteredActivity` array (line 2965)
  - Maps each activity item through `ActivityRow` memoized component (lines 2965-2989)

**List Container for Non-Direct Conversations:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationMain.tsx` (lines 1359-1497)
  - Renders `conversationTimelineItems` array directly via `.map()` (line 1360)
  - No memoization on the list itself; each timeline item rendered as a full component tree

**Supporting Renderer:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/Terminal.tsx` line 2135
  - `ActivityRow` component: memoized per-entry renderer (uses `React.memo`)
  - Props are scalars, stable callbacks, and entries from Terminal's memoized activity array

### 2. **Virtualization/Windowing Status**

**NO virtualization/windowing is implemented.** The codebase uses:
- Plain DOM rendering with `.map()` over entire arrays
- No react-window, react-virtuoso, or tanstack/react-virtual dependencies
- Scroll-based history pagination instead: fetch older entries on scroll-to-top (line 2814 in Terminal.tsx)

The Terminal handles prepended history (older entries loaded above) via scroll position management (lines 2842-2880, `resolveTerminalPrependedScrollTop`).

### 3. **Event/Turn Array State Storage**

**Direct Control Path (Unified Worker Stream):**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/WorkerEntriesManager.ts`
  - Per-worker bounded window: `[lowestSeq..latestContiguousSeq]` (lines 5-23)
  - **Capped at 100 entries per worker**: `DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT = 100` (line 72)
  - Initial load and cold start fetch last N entries via `?limit=100` (line 331)
  - Stored in `WorkerStreamState.entries: WorkerEntry[]` (line 94)
  - Growth model: unbounded forward extension via SSE wake-up frames + HTTP fetch (line 375-387)
  - Trimmed on init: `slice(-DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT)` (line 253)

**Conversation Transcript Path (Multi-Worker):**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/ConversationTranscriptManager.ts`
  - Run-scoped merged transcript across all workers
  - **Capped at 100 entries**: `DEFAULT_TRANSCRIPT_LIMIT = 100` (line 45)
  - Token-based pagination (no seq-based cursor) for multi-worker ordering
  - Stored in `ConversationTranscriptState.entries: ConversationTranscriptEntry[]` (line 22)

**Timeline Items (Supervisor/Planning Conversations):**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/utils.ts` (lines 831-920)

  - **No explicit cap** — grows with message count
  - Stored in `conversationTimelineItems: ConversationTimelineItem[]`
  - Built on every render if any source changes (useMemo with deps: `[isPlanningConversation, selectedRunExecutionEvents, selectedRunSupervisorInterventions, selectedRunWorkersForDisplay, visibleMessages]`)

### 4. **Memoization Strategy**

**Terminal Activity Array:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/Terminal.tsx` (lines 2508-2771)
  - Memoized `activity` array built from entries via `useMemo` (line 2508)
  - Dependencies: agent, allowUserMessageFallback, entries, getUserMessageActions, hasMoreHistory, multiWorkerOrdering, pendingAssistantStatus, sendingUserMessageIds, showPendingAssistantIndicator, summarizeWorkBlocks, suppressAcceptedPlanRows, ungatedUserMessageIds, userMessages
  - Computed: 5 sub-activities built (agentActivity, generatedImagesActivity, userActivity, dedupedUserActivity, pendingAssistantActivity) then sorted in one pass

**Per-Entry Memoization:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/Terminal.tsx` (line 2135)
  - `ActivityRow = memo(function ActivityRow {...})`
  - Each entry re-renders only if its own props or the parent activity array reference changes
  - Per-row narrowed selector for copy state (lines 2175-2183)
  - Prevents full transcript re-render when one message is copied

**Conversation Timeline:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/useHomeViewModel.ts` (line 582)
  - `conversationTimelineItems` wrapped in `useMemo`
  - No per-item memoization; direct .map() in ConversationMain
  - Each item re-renders when timeline array reference changes

**Fallback User Messages Deduplication:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/Terminal.tsx` (lines 402-417)
  - `dedupeUserActivityByMessageId()` keeps last occurrence (newest delivery) per message id
  - Prevents duplicates when rewinding/editing

### 5. **Existing Limits & Constants**

| Constant | Location | Value | Purpose |
|----------|----------|-------|---------|
| `DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT` | WorkerEntriesManager.ts:72 | 100 | Initial tail load & cache trim for single-worker streams |
| `DEFAULT_OLDER_LIMIT` | WorkerEntriesManager.ts:73 | 100 | Backward pagination page size |
| `DEFAULT_TRANSCRIPT_LIMIT` | ConversationTranscriptManager.ts:45 | 100 | Multi-worker transcript page size |
| `DEFAULT_TRANSCRIPT_LIMIT` | conversation-transcript.ts (server):30 | 100 | Server-side default |

| `FULL_HISTORY_ENTRY_LIMIT` | agent-detail.ts (server):27 | 20,000 | Full history archive size limit |

| `DEFAULT_MAX_WORKERS` | WorkerEntriesManager.ts:70 | 16 | Max workers tracked in memory |
| `DEFAULT_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS` | WorkerEntriesManager.ts:78 | 1,000 | Cache flush debounce |

No explicit pruning/trimming of unbounded conversation timelines — they grow with message count. The 100-entry cap applies only to the scrollable activity window (direct control Terminal), not the full supervisor/planning conversation view.
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-27T15:22:31.865Z

I've confirmed both paths have the same unbounded growth issue — the forward fetch also concatenates entries without limit. I'm also realizing that `buildAgentOutputActivity` likely rebuilds the entire activity array on every append, creating new object references for every row and defeating the memoization on `ActivityRow`, forcing all rows to re-render unnecessarily. I need to verify this by checking whether it recreates entries each time.

So there are three compounding issues at play: unbounded entries growth on live append with no head trimming, no virtualization so every entry stays in the DOM, and a full activity rebuild with new object identity per row on every append that forces all ActivityRows to re-render despite memoization. This third issue seems like the real killer — O(n) rebuild plus O(n) React reconciliation on every streaming chunk, which becomes catastrophic in a day-long session with tens of thousands of entries and frequent updates. I want to confirm the memo comparator on ActivityRow and check how often rebuilds actually happen.

Given the user framed this as "am I right?" it reads as diagnosis-first rather than an explicit fix request, and the actual fix here involves a real design choice between capping entries and adding virtualization — so I should present my diagnosis and ask which direction they want before implementing anything. Let me nail down the ActivityRow memo comparator and rebuild trigger frequency to make sure my diagnosis is solid.

## Claude · 2026-08-27T15:23:03.946Z

Your diagnosis is right, and I can point at the exact lines. There are three compounding causes, not one.

**1. The entry window is capped only on mount, never on live append.**

`DEFAULT_TAIL_LIMIT = 100` is applied when hydrating from cache or a snapshot (`WorkerEntriesManager.ts:252`, `:681`), so a fresh page load looks fine. But the live merge path never trims the head:

```
src/interface/home/WorkerEntriesManager.ts:612-637
const merged = [...previous.entries];
for (const entry of incoming) { ... merged.push(entry) ... }
entries: merged.length > previous.entries.length ? merged : previous.entries,
```

Same flaw in the multi-worker path — `ConversationTranscriptManager.ts:200` does `[...current.entries, ...response.entries]` with no cap. So the 100-entry window is a lie the moment the stream starts: a session running a day grows to tens of thousands of entries. Reloading the tab fixes it temporarily, which matches "gets slow after a while."

**2. Nothing is virtualized.** `Terminal.tsx:2965` maps the entire `filteredActivity` array into the DOM. Every entry ever streamed stays mounted, each one rendering markdown/diff subtrees.

**3. The memoization is defeated on every append.** `ActivityRow` is `memo`'d, and the comment at `Terminal.tsx:2190` claims the shallow compare is meaningful because props come from "Terminal's memoized `activity` array." But `buildAgentOutputActivity` (`src/lib/agent-output.ts:1259-1260`) starts from `const items: AgentActivityItem[] = []` and constructs brand-new objects for every entry on every call. When `entries` changes identity, the memo rebuilds all N items, so all N rows get a new `activity` prop and all N re-render.

That's the freeze mechanism: each streaming chunk triggers an O(n) rebuild plus O(n) React reconciliation over the full history. At a few chunks per second against a 20k-entry transcript, the main thread never gets a break — exactly the "constantly re-rendering all of them" you described.

Cause 1 is the root; 2 and 3 are what turn it into a hang instead of just memory growth.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-27T16:10:25.189Z

I'm designing a two-tier approach: a bounded render window showing only the last N entries unless the user scrolls up, plus a memory retention layer that keeps older entries around beyond the render window but evicts them based on age or last-access time.

I'm working through the eviction rule — a hard cap on total entries per worker plus an age-based threshold, where entries in the older region get dropped if they haven't been accessed (scrolled into or refetched via loadOlder) within some retention window, and once trimmed, `hasOlder` flips true so the older content can be refetched on demand.

The trickier piece is object identity reuse in agent-output.ts, since buildAgentOutputActivity currently rebuilds fresh objects each pass with in-place mutation for things like tool updates and appended thoughts — making unchanged entries return identical references will take real care.

Given the scope, I want to read through the actual code before committing to a design: agent-output.ts's buildAgentOutputActivity, WorkerEntriesManager, ConversationTranscriptManager, Terminal.tsx's activity memoization, and the existing tests, plus check the project's agents.md and relevant skills for conventions before making changes.

```text
1	CRITICAL!!:

3	- NEVER EVER EVER delete the `bugdrop-screenshots` branch, locally or remotely. It is protected project history.
4	- NEVER create a worktree, UNLESS the user has specifically asked for it!
5	- NEVER delete files unless the user has specifically asked for it. If you think a file is misplaced, unrelated, or stale, that is an entirely separate request — ask first. "Looks unrelated to this task" is not authorization to delete.
6	
7	- When the user gives a UUID and asks what is going on with it, treat it as an OmniHarness conversation/session lookup: check `sqlite.db`, starting with the `runs` row for that UUID, then inspect related `workers`, `messages`, `execution_events`, queued messages, and validation/plan records as needed.
8	- To delete all conversations and associated persisted artifacts, use `scripts/delete-conversations.sh`
9	
10	Planning artifacts:
11	- When the user says “write the plan”, “write a full plan”, or “write the implementation plan”, treat that as a request to persist the complete plan to a file, not merely display it in chat.
12	- If the user gives a path, write the plan there. Otherwise use `docs/superpowers/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<kebab-case-feature-name>.md`, using the current date and a non-colliding filename.
13	- The saved file is the canonical full plan. The final response should link to it and summarize the result rather than replacing the file with a chat-only plan.
14	- This convention applies even when the current message omits the destination because “write the plan” is the project’s established shorthand for creating the plan artifact.
15	- If the same request explicitly says not to change the workspace or not to create a file, obey that more specific prohibition and ask for a destination when needed.
16	
17	Testing:
18	- After any UI/frontend change, always run `pnpm build:interface:web` before considering the work complete.
19	- When testing the app, use the already-running process if one exists instead of starting another server.
20	- The normal runner URL is `http://localhost:3050`; the Vite development interface is usually at `http://localhost:5173`.
21	- Clean up any test sessions/conversations and their associated persisted artifacts before finishing.
22	- For lifecycle / chaos-style regressions (reconnect, restart, FK-on-delete, plan-review leftover state), use `pnpm test:lifecycle`. Scenarios live under `tests/lifecycle/scenarios/` and drive the control plane via HTTP/SSE — no Chromium. To debug a specific reported bug, mirror it as a new scenario file.
23	- Read `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md` before adding new server-side state transitions. It is the spec all new code is held to.
24	- Worker conversation content (direct-control and supervisor-driven worker turns) lives in the unified worker stream — one append-only JSONL per worker at `app-data/run-data/<runId>/<workerId>.jsonl`. Persist new worker content through `appendWorkerEntry` in `src/server/workers/output-store.ts`; render it through `WorkerEntriesManager` and the `entries` prop on `Terminal`. Do NOT add a parallel persistence layer (a sibling JSONL, a new `messages.kind`, an in-memory cache that the frontend reconciles against the stream). See `docs/architecture/worker-conversation-stream.md`.
25	
26	Lifecycle observability rules (full doc: `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md`):
27	- Every server-side decision (spawn, reattach, recreate, give up, refuse, delete, fail) emits a typed named event via `emitNamedEvent` from `@/server/events/named-events`. Silent early returns and bare `catch {}` are bugs.
28	- User-relevant failures additionally emit `error.surfaced` with a stable `code` (typed union in `named-events.ts`), `surface`, and at least one relevant subject id such as `runId`/`workerId`/`conversationId`/`accountId`. Never funnel through a blanket wrapper in `api-errors.ts`.
29	- All SSE frames carry an `id:`. Clients reconnect with `Last-Event-ID`; the server replays from the ring buffer or emits `stream.resync_required`. Snapshot bootstrap is `GET /api/events?snapshot=1` (anchor id in the `x-omni-last-event-id` response header).
30	- Dev-only event log: `GET /api/events/log?since=<id>&runId=<id>` returns the ring buffer as JSON. Use this when triaging "X didn't happen" bug reports — if the event isn't there, the server didn't do the thing, and the next step is finding the silent branch.
31	- Chaos is a client-side concern. Never add fault-injection code paths to server code.
32	
33	Frontend i18n:
34	- EVERY user-facing frontend string MUST live in `shared/locales/*.json` and be rendered with the `t()` function from `@/lib/i18n`.
35	- NEVER hardcode user-facing JSX text, button labels, dialog titles, aria-labels, titles, placeholders, empty states, status labels, help text, error fallback text, or visible option labels directly in components.
36	- For every new user-facing string, add the key to `shared/locales/en.json` and every other locale file in `shared/locales/` in the same change. Use stable dotted keys like `settings.runtime.recovery` or `conversation.sidebar.search`.
37	- Components that render translated strings and need to update when the language changes MUST subscribe to the i18n manager by calling `useI18nSnapshot()` from `@/lib/i18n`; then render text with the existing `t("key", params?)` function. The hook is only for re-rendering; `t()` is the translation API.
38	- Non-React modules that produce user-visible text must still use `t("key", params?)` at the boundary where text is produced. Protocol values, storage keys, API paths, CSS classes, model ids, and database values are not translation keys unless they are displayed as copy.
39	- Do not pass translated strings through transactions or persist translated UI copy. Store stable ids/values and translate at render time.
40	
41	React Best Practices:
42	- MutableRefObject is deprecated in React; use RefObject instead.
43	- NEVER use require() to import modules. Use import instead.
44	- ALWAYS centralize all state in global single source of truth Manager classes. Components/listeners subscribe to updates from Managers and use Manager methods to update data.
45	- Always implement a custom data manager for any data structure. NEVER use separate state variables and arrays as the source of truth.
46	- NEVER send data around in transactions.
47	- Avoid using useEffect() to update state. Prefer useCallback() or Manager methods for explicit state transitions.
48	- Avoid callback hell, race conditions, and infinite loops.
49	- NEVER store string literals or UI settings for the frontend in the .env file. Use literals in files, inline arrays, or .json files instead.
50	- When debugging React: if a fix did not work, think hard about whether this is a race condition. Race conditions are common in React.
51
```
